Result: 耦合potentially klt组的阈值由准单项赋值计算；potentially klt型簇上每个大除子存在正部分半丰沛的双有理Zariski分解；大除子的多截面环有限生成；给出potentially klt型簇为Mori dream space的判定准则。

Conclusion: 该工作把渐近乘子理想层与对数规范阈值理论推广至伪有效除子组，并将Zariski分解、多截面环有限生成与Mori dream space刻画统一在potentially klt型框架下。

Abstract: We introduce asymptotic multiplier ideal sheaves and log canonical thresholds associated with tuples of pseudoeffective divisors on a projective klt pair. We prove that the threshold of a coupled potentially klt tuple is computed by a quasi-monomial valuation. For varieties of potentially klt type, we prove that every big divisor admits a birational Zariski decomposition with semiample positive part. We also prove finite generation of multisection rings of big divisors and give a criterion for a variety of potentially klt type to be a Mori dream space.

</details>


### [88] [Graded Betti numbers of general curves of large degree](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11161)
*JeongDon Lee,Li Li,Jinhyung Park*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文给出了在Brill–Noether轨迹具有预期维数且$H^1(C,L\otimes\omega_C^{-1})=0$条件下，曲线$C$上任意线丛$L$的Koszul上同调群$K_{p,q}(C,L)$的显式闭公式，并由此确定了一般曲线的完整Betti表及Boij–Söderberg系数的渐近单调性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前仅确定了$L$次数充分大时Koszul上同调群的消失与非消失性质，但分级Betti数$\kappa_{p,q}(C,L)$的具体值仍基本未知，因此需要给出显式计算公式以刻画Betti表。

Method: 在$W_k^1(C)$具有预期维数且$H^1(C,L\otimes\omega_C^{-1})=0$的假设下，通过显式闭公式计算所有分级Betti数$\kappa_{p,q}(C,L)$；并借助Boij–Söderberg理论分析截面环$R(C,L)$的系数。

Result: 得到全部$\kappa_{p,q}(C,L)$的显式公式；当$\deg L\geq 4g-3$，或$\deg L\geq 3g-3$且$L$一般时，确定了一般曲线的完整Betti表；显式计算了支配渐近纯性的Boij–Söderberg系数，并证明其余系数在超椭圆曲线上递减、在相关Brill–Noether轨迹满足自然一般既约性假设下递增。

Conclusion: 本文完整刻画了相关条件下曲线线丛的Betti表结构，并揭示了Boij–Söderberg系数的渐近单调行为。

Abstract: Let $C$ be a smooth projective complex curve of genus $g$ and gonality $k$, and $L$ be a very ample line bundle on $C$. When $L$ has sufficiently large degree, the vanishing and nonvanishing of the Koszul cohomology groups $K_{p,q}(C,L)$ have been determined previously, but the exact values of the graded Betti numbers $κ_{p,q}(C, L)$ remain largely unknown. In this paper, we give explicit closed formulas for all graded Betti numbers $κ_{p,q}(C, L)$ when the Brill--Noether locus $W_k^1(C)$ has the expected dimension and $H^1(C, L \otimes ω_C^{-1})=0$. Consequently, we determine the complete Betti table for a general curve when $°L \geq 4g-3$ or when $°L \geq 3g-3$ and $L$ is general. We also explicitly compute the Boij--Söderberg coefficient of the section ring $R(C, L)$ governing asymptotic purity, and show eventual monotonicity of the remaining coefficients: they decrease for hyperelliptic curves and increase under a natural generic reducedness assumption on the relevant Brill--Noether loci.

</details>


### [89] [Divisibility and torsion in higher Chow groups over arithmetic fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11178)
*Toshiro Hiranouchi,Rin Sugiyama*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 研究光滑方案 $X$（维数 $d$，基域 $F$）的高阶 Chow 群 $CH^{d+i}(X,j)$ 的阿贝尔群结构，在 $i$ 不小于 $F$ 的 $l$-上同调维数时证明整除性与无挠性，并刻画前推映射的核及其在有限域、局部域、整体域上的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高阶 Chow 群是代数 K-理论与代数圈的算术不变量，其群结构（整除性、挠性）是理解这些不变量的核心问题，尤其在各类算术域上具有重要应用价值。

Method: 设 $X$ 为域 $F$ 上维数 $d$ 的光滑方案，取与 $\operatorname{char} F$ 不同的素数 $l$，在 $i\ge \operatorname{cd}_l(F)$ 的条件下分析 $CH^{d+i}(X,j)$ 的 $l$-挠部分；对光滑、紧合、几何不可约的 $X$，研究前推 $CH^{d+i}(X,j)\to CH^i(F,j)$ 的核。

Result: 证得当 $i$ 不小于 $F$ 的 $l$-上同调维数时，$CH^{d+i}(X,j)$ 具有整除性与无挠性；并对光滑紧合几何不可约的 $X$ 得到前推映射核的相关结果，随后应用于有限域、局部域和整体域。

Conclusion: 高阶 Chow 群在适当的维数条件下展现出良好的代数结构（整除性与无挠性），前推核的刻画将整体不变量与基域上的 Chow 群相联系，为有限域、局部域、整体域等算术情形提供了统一的处理框架。

Abstract: Let $X$ be a smooth scheme of dimension $d$ over a field $F$. We study the abelian-group structure of the higher Chow groups $CH^{d+i}(X,j)$. For a prime $l$ different from the characteristic of $F$, we prove divisibility and torsion-freeness results when $i\ge$ the $l$-cohomological dimension of $F$. If $X$ is smooth proper and geometrically irreducible, we also study the kernel of the push-forward $CH^{d+i}(X,j)\to CH^i(F,j)$. We apply these results to finite fields, local fields, and global fields.

</details>


### [90] [Openness of local properties in flat families of superschemes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11287)
*Ibrahim Ahmad*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 该文证明了超概形的平坦真态射在特征0域上有限型Dedekind概形上的纤维性质构成开集，并给出退化中的性质提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Jankowski借助Serre判别法引入了超概形的正规性与既约性概念（generic fermionic regularity与reducedness）。本文旨在研究这些超几何性质在族形变下是否保持、以及在参数的哪里保持，即性质的开性。

Method: 考虑平坦且真的态射f: X→Y，其中X为超概形，Y为特征0域上有限型的Dedekind概形；运用超概形版本的Serre判别与超交换代数纤维性质的技术证明开性。

Result: 对于上述态射，使纤维X_y为正规、GFRR、正则以及Cohen-Macaulay的y∈Y构成Y中的开集；特别地，在A_C^{1|0}上的退化情形中，这些性质可从特殊纤维提升到一般纤维。

Conclusion: 超概形的正规性、GFRR、正则性与Cohen-Macaulay性在平坦真族中具有开性，并可在特征0的A^{1|0}退化中由特殊纤维传递到一般纤维。

Abstract: Recently, Jankowski introduced the Serre criteria for superschemes to define the super versions of normality and reducedness (called generic fermionic regularity and reducedness). We show that for a flat, proper morphism $f:X\rightarrow Y$, where $X$ is a superscheme and $Y$ is a Dedekind scheme of finite type over a field of characteristic $0$, the set of $y\in Y$ for which the fibers $X_y$ are normal, GFRR, regular, and Cohen-Macaulay is open in $Y$. In particular, if one has a degeneration over $\mathbb{A}_{\mathbb{C}}^{1|0}$, this shows that these properties can be lifted from the special fiber to the generic one.

</details>


### [91] [Generic vanishing conjecture for divisors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11377)
*Fanjun Meng*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文提出并证明了Pareschi-Popa关于主极化阿贝尔簇generic vanishing猜想的一个余维1类比版本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Pareschi与Popa提出的generic vanishing猜想，关注主极化阿贝尔簇中代表最小上同调类的余维&gt;1子簇的存在性，与它们被主极化扭曲后的理想层的正性之间的关系。作者受此表述启发，希望探讨该猜想在余维1情形下的类比问题。

Method: 借鉴Pareschi-Popa猜想的表述框架，构造相应的余维1类比，并证明该类比猜想成立。

Result: 证明了该generic vanishing猜想的余维1类比成立。

Conclusion: Pareschi-Popa猜想的余维1类比版本得到证明，进一步支持并丰富了generic vanishing理论在主极化阿贝尔簇框架中的研究。

Abstract: Generic vanishing conjecture proposed by Pareschi and Popa is about the relationship between the existence of subvarieties of codimension $>1$ of principally polarized abelian varieties representing minimal cohomology class and the positivity of their ideal sheaves twisted by the principal polarization. Inspired by their formulation, we prove a codimension $1$ analog of generic vanishing conjecture.

</details>


### [92] [Campana Rational Connectedness and Weak Approximation of Del Pezzo Orbifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11533)
*Saptarshi Dandapat*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 证明度数小于7的del Pezzo orbifold以及具有不可约边界的更高次del Pezzo orbifold是强Campana单直纹的，从而推出Campana有理连通性，并给出Campana猜想的重要非环面二维例子及弱逼近结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为Campana关于弱逼近定理所需形式的猜想提供二维非环面实例，并将结果应用于Campana纤维化截面的弱逼近问题。

Method: 证明del Pezzo orbifold（次数&lt;7，或次数更高但边界不可约）具有强Campana单直纹性，进而推出Campana有理连通性；在三个附录中分类次数为6、7、8且边界不可约的del Pezzo orbifold。

Result: 度数小于7的del Pezzo orbifold及更高次具有不可约边界的del Pezzo orbifold均为强Campana单直纹且Campana有理连通；获得Campana猜想的重要非环面二维例子，并得到一般纤维为此类orbifold的Campana纤维化在其所有位置处Campana截面的弱逼近；分类了次数6、7、8且边界不可约的del Pezzo orbifold。

Conclusion: 该工作验证了Campana猜想在二维的一类非环面情形，并提供了弱逼近定理所需形式，同时给出了若干次数的del Pezzo orbifold分类。

Abstract: We prove that a del Pezzo orbifold of degree less than 7, and del Pezzo orbifolds of higher degree with irreducible boundary are strongly Campana uniruled, and deduce that it is Campana rationally connected. This provides important non-toric examples in dimension two of a conjecture by Campana, in the form required by the weak approximation theorem, and yields weak approximation for Campana sections of Campana fibrations at all places whose general fibre is such an orbifold. In three appendices we classify the del Pezzo orbifolds of degree 6, 7 and 8 with irreducible boundary.

</details>


### [93] [A maximal function field of genus $17$ over $\mathbb{F}_{11^2}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11679)
*Peter Beelen,Maria Montanucci,Jonathan Niemann*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 构造了有限域 𝔽_{11²} 上的一个新极大函数域 G，证明它不能作为 Hermitian 函数域的子域获得，这是首个满足该性质的 𝔽_{p²}-极大函数域，并计算了 G 的自同构群。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已知的 𝔽_{p²}-极大函数域大多可通过 Hermitian 函数域的子域得到，因此需要寻找是否存在不具此来源的极大函数域，以刻画此类函数域的分类边界。

Method: 借助 AI 搜索/发现新的极大函数域 G（定义于 𝔽_{11²}），并证明 G 不同构于 Hermitian 函数域的任何子域，随后计算其自同构群。

Result: 得到 𝔽_{11²} 上一个新的极大函数域 G；证明 G 不能作为 Hermitian 函数域的子域获得，是首个具有该性质的 𝔽_{p²}-极大函数域；同时给出 G 的自同构群。

Conclusion: 存在不来源于 Hermitian 函数域的 𝔽_{p²}-极大函数域，扩展了对极大函数域分类与构造的认识；AI 在此发现过程中发挥了作用。

Abstract: In this article, we describe a new maximal function field $G$ over the finite field $\mathbb{F}_{11^2}$ with $11^2$ elements and show that it cannot be obtained as a subfield of the Hermitian function field. This provides the first known $\mathbb{F}_{p^2}$-maximal function field that satisfies this property. Further, we compute the automorphism group of $G$. The new maximal function field was found using AI.

</details>


### [94] [Maximality of the Hodge level for smooth weighted complete intersections of general type](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11825)
*Victor Przyjalkowski*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 证明了任意光滑良构的一般型加权完全交的霍奇数 $h^{0,n}(X)$ 为正（霍奇层级最大化），给出几何亏格 $p_g(X)$ 的显式下界，并分类出唯一的例外数值例子 $X_{6,6}\subset\mathbb{P}(1,2,2,3,3)$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究一般型加权完全交的霍奇理论性质，特别是霍奇层级是否达到最大、几何亏格的正性与下界。这类结果对卡拉比-丘流形与镜像对称、以及广义法诺/一般型的分类研究具有重要意义。

Method: 利用加权射影空间中的加权完全交理论，结合霍奇层级与几何亏格估计方法，对光滑良构加权完全交进行系统分析。

Result: 对任意 $n>0$ 维光滑良构一般型加权完全交，证明 $h^{0,n}(X)>0$（霍奇层级最大），并得到 $p_g(X)$ 的显式下界；进一步表明唯一不满足 $p_g(X)=1$ 的非线性锥数值完全交是 $X_{6,6}\subset\mathbb{P}(1,2,2,3,3)$。

Conclusion: 一般型加权完全交的霍奇层级普遍达到最大，几何亏格具有统一正下界；仅存在一个例外情形，具有重要的分类意义。

Abstract: We prove that for every smooth well formed weighted complete intersection of general type of dimension $n>0$, the Hodge number $h^{0,n}(X)$ is positive; in other words, its Hodge level is maximal. We also obtain an explicit lower bound for the geometric genus $p_g(X)$. This implies that the only weighted complete intersection of general type that is not a numerical intersection with a linear cone with $p_g(X)=1$ is $X_{6,6}\subset\mathbb{P}(1,2,2,3,3)$.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

### [95] [M3-Former: Multimodal Transformer with Mixture-of-Experts for Long-Term Vessel Trajectory Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10559)
*Wenzhe Jin,Haina Tang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 M3-Former，一个由大语言模型增强的多模态船舶轨迹预测框架，通过语义先验、双粒度 MoE 与转向加权损失，在丹麦 AIS 数据集上 1–4 小时预测均优于现有方法，4 小时 ADE/FDE 分别降低 4.4%/5.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对船舶轨迹预测中的三个挑战：行为多模态性、语义信息利用不足以及长期误差累积。

Method: 1) 引入船舶静态属性与航行意图作为语义先验，用于长期轨迹建模；2) 构建统一多模态表示空间，由预训练 LLM 编码静态语义信息，并通过自注意力与动态轨迹特征对齐；3) 设计双粒度 Mixture-of-Experts 架构，序列级专家建模全局航线趋势，token 级专家细化细粒度操纵行为，从而联合捕捉全局路径规划与局部运动变化；4) 提出 Steering-Weighted Cross-Entropy 损失，缓解稀疏转向样本的长尾分布问题。

Result: 在真实丹麦 AIS 数据集上，M3-Former 在 1 至 4 小时预测时域内持续优于最先进基线；4 小时任务中 ADE 和 FDE 分别较最强基线降低 4.4% 和 5.1%。定性与消融分析表明，语义融合有效减少长期轨迹漂移，双粒度 MoE 提升了复杂航道与航线分支场景下的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该框架建立了语义引导的层次化预测范式，联合建模高层航行意图与局部运动动态，实现鲁棒的长期船舶轨迹预测。

Abstract: To address the challenges of behavioral multimodality, limited semantic utilization, and long-term error accumulation in vessel trajectory prediction, this paper proposes M3-Former, a multimodal trajectory prediction framework enhanced by large language models (LLMs). The proposed framework incorporates vessel static attributes and navigational intent as semantic priors for long-term trajectory modeling. Specifically, a unified multimodal representation space is constructed, in which static semantic information is encoded by a pre-trained LLM and aligned with dynamic trajectory features through self-attention. To jointly capture global route planning and local motion variations, a dual-granularity Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architecture is introduced, where sequence-level experts model global navigation trends and token-level experts refine fine-grained maneuvering behaviors. In addition, a Steering-Weighted Cross-Entropy loss is designed to alleviate the long-tail distribution of sparse turning samples and improve prediction accuracy in critical maneuvering scenarios. Experiments on a real-world Danish AIS dataset demonstrate that M\textsuperscript{3}-Former consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines across prediction horizons from 1 to 4 hours. In the 4-hour prediction task, the proposed method reduces Average Displacement Error (ADE) and Final Displacement Error (FDE) by 4.4\% and 5.1\%, respectively, compared with the strongest baseline. Qualitative and ablation analyses further verify that semantic fusion effectively reduces long-term trajectory drift, while the dual-granularity MoE improves robustness in complex waterways and route-branching scenarios. The proposed framework establishes a semantic-guided hierarchical prediction paradigm, in which high-level navigational intent and local motion dynamics are jointly modeled for robust long-term vessel trajectory forecasting.

</details>


### [96] [Halo: Improving forecast accuracy through heteroscedastic estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10589)
*Adam Cataldo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文提出Halo方法，在深度时间序列预测器中增加一个预测分布尺度的第二输出，并用匹配的负对数似然训练。结果显示异方差预测不仅用于不确定性量化，还能提升点估计精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异方差预测通常被用于不确定性量化，而在非时间序列场景中已有负面结果报告其不利于点估计。本文旨在探究在时间序列预测中，让网络同时估计位置参数与尺度参数是否也能改善点估计性能。

Method: Halo复用已有深度预测器的架构，为其增加第二个输出以估计隐含分布的尺度，并在匹配的负对数似然下训练。作者将三种SOTA模型（Transformer、图网络+变分自编码器、单层卷积网络）分别在Gaussian与Laplacian损失下适配，并在标准预测基准的五个电价市场上评估。

Result: 在30组模型-市场-指标比较中，Halo有28组提升了MSE和MAE，平均MSE降低2.6%至16.5%，平均MAE降低1.7%至11.0%。两个关键发现：(1) 尺度估计来自第二投影头还是完整并行网络影响甚微，关键在是否估计尺度；(2) 在已为点估计基线调好的超参数下改进依然成立，重调超参数非必需。

Conclusion: 在时间序列预测中，异方差预测可同时服务于不确定性量化与点估计提升，且实现简单、无需重新调参，具有较好的通用性与实用性。

Abstract: Heteroscedastic forecasting, where a network estimates a scale parameter alongside a location parameter, is normally motivated by uncertainty quantification. This paper shows it also improves the point estimate, in contrast to reported negative results for heteroscedastic estimation outside time series. Halo is a modification that reuses an existing deep forecaster's architecture, giving it a second output for the scale of its implied distribution and training it under the matching negative log likelihood. Adapting three state-of-the-art models --- a transformer, a graph network paired with a variational autoencoder, and a single-layer convolutional network --- under both Gaussian and Laplacian losses demonstrates the phenomenon. On the five electricity price markets of a standard forecasting benchmark, Halo improves MSE and MAE in 28 of 30 model-market-metric comparisons, cutting average MSE by 2.6% to 16.5% and average MAE by 1.7% to 11.0%. Two findings emerge: (1) whether the scale estimate comes from a second projection head or from a full parallel network matters far less than whether the network estimates scale, and (2) the improvement holds under the hyperparameters already tuned for the point-estimate baseline, so retuning is optional.

</details>


### [97] [Zero-shot rib design: merging training-free generative prior with topology optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10643)
*Yongmin Kwon,Namwoo Kang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该工作将冻结的文本到图像扩散模型作为免训练的设计知识先验，通过分数蒸馏采样（SDS）把文本提示诱导的生成梯度与有限元灵敏度结合进密度法拓扑优化循环，使自然语言成为可机器解释的设计意图表示，并在多种几何域与物理问题中显著降低柔顺度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然界（叶脉、骨小梁、蜘蛛网）具有高比刚度，但经典拓扑优化器很少生成此类构型，且难以让工程师用自然语言表达结构设计意图。作者希望提供一种可复用、免训练的生成式先验，使文本提示能显式地参与并引导物理优化过程。

Method: 将冻结的文本到图像扩散模型视为免训练设计知识来源，用分数蒸馏采样把生成梯度蒸馏进密度法拓扑优化的物理循环。每次迭代同时结合提示诱导的生成梯度与有限元灵敏度，由物理决定哪些提示特征保留。方法包含Heaviside投影配合β-continuation以抑制中间密度，并提供基于骨架的自动化流程把优化密度场转成可用于CAD的候选几何。框架通过更换文本提示实现跨域、跨载荷与跨物理目标的先验复用，每个新问题单独指定物理设置。

Result: 在四个几何域、两种物理机制共245次主要SDS运行中，49个"提示—域"组合中有38个取得统计显著的柔顺度下降（力学最高−31.5%，热弹性最高−23.0%），优于基于梯度的基线。跨域形态学分析发现反复出现的改进结构特征：多数域中生成先验抑制了肋骨架中的死端分支，端点与柔顺度相关性r=+0.56至+0.99。Heaviside投影与β-continuation将中间密度倾向从42.6%降至<3%。

Conclusion: 通过更换文本提示即可跨域、跨载荷、跨物理目标重定向生成先验，且每个新问题的物理设置可独立指定，表明预训练生成模型可作为工程设计中可复用、免训练的先验。该框架把自然语言转变为机器可解释的设计意图表示，并改进了扩散—物理耦合中的中间密度问题，输出可对接CAD的候选几何。

Abstract: Natural load-bearing patterns such as leaf venation, trabecular bone, and spider webs achieve high stiffness per unit mass, yet classical topology optimizers rarely reach such geometries, and few let engineers express structural design intent through natural language. This work treats a frozen text-to-image diffusion model as a training-free source of design knowledge and distills it into the physics loop of density-based topology optimization via score distillation sampling, so that a text prompt becomes an explicit, machine-interpretable representation of engineer intent. The prompt-induced generative gradient and the finite element sensitivity are combined at every iteration, letting physics decide which prompt-induced features survive. In 245 primary SDS runs spanning four geometric domains and two physics regimes, 38 of 49 prompt--domain combinations achieved statistically significant compliance reductions (up to $-31.5\%$ mechanical and $-23.0\%$ thermoelastic), outperforming gradient-based baselines. Cross-domain morphological analysis identifies a recurring structural signature of improvement: in most domains the generative prior suppresses dead-end branches in the rib skeleton, with endpoint--compliance correlation $r = +0.56$ to $+0.99$. A Heaviside projection with $β$-continuation resolves a pronounced intermediate-density tendency in this diffusion--physics coupling ($42.6\%$ to $<3\%$), and an automated skeleton-based pipeline converts optimized density fields into \rev{candidate geometry ready for computer-aided design. By retargeting the generative prior across domains, loading conditions, and physics objectives through a change of text prompt, with each new problem's physics setup specified separately, the framework uses a pretrained generative model as a reusable, training-free prior for engineering design.

</details>


### [98] [Byzantine-Robust Federated Fire Detection with a Rotating Coordinator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10647)
*Georgia Argyrou,Aymen Bahrouny,Hedi Fendriy,Alexander Jung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将联邦学习应用于室内火灾检测，针对上行带宽受限、拜占庭客户端以及单一固定聚合服务器的信任问题，提出数据集、压缩检测器与半去中心化拜占庭鲁棒联邦方法三项贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 室内火灾检测系统使用边缘摄像头，其录制的敏感视频难以集中收集，因此需要联邦学习；但现有联邦方案存在三个实际障碍：上行带宽有限、存在拜占庭（恶意或故障）客户端、以及对单一永久固定聚合服务器的无条件信任。

Method: (i) 从八个公开来源构建的室内火灾检测数据集；(ii) 可边缘部署的检测器，模型更新压缩最高达10倍；(iii) 半去中心化拜占庭鲁棒联邦方法，结合历史感知聚合与轮换协调者。

Result: 模型更新压缩最高10倍仅带来平衡准确率的轻微损失；轮换协调者方法可驱逐单轮过滤器遗漏的隐蔽攻击；在留出测试集上，轮换协调者方法与固定服务器版本在准确率和检测速度上相当；物理分布式的六节点云部署验证了可行性。

Conclusion: 该工作同时解决了带宽、拜占庭鲁棒性与固定服务器单点故障三个问题，且轮换协调者在性能不损失的前提下消除单点故障，分布式部署验证了实际可行性。

Abstract: We study the application of federated learning (FL) to indoor fire detection. Such fire-detection systems use edge cameras that record sensitive footage which cannot easily be collected at a central server. Existing federated solutions leave three practical obstacles unaddressed: limited uplink bandwidth, Byzantine (malicious or faulty) clients, and unconditional trust in a single, permanently fixed aggregation server. Our main contributions address all three. In particular, we provide (i) a curated indoor fire-detection dataset assembled from eight public sources; (ii) an edge-deployable detector whose model updates are compressed up to 10 time with only a small loss in balanced accuracy; and (iii) a semi-decentralized Byzantine-robust FL method that combines history-aware aggregation with a rotating coordinator, evicting stealthy attacks that per-round filters miss while removing the fixed-server single point of failure. On the held-out test set the rotating-coordinator method matches its fixed-server counterpart in accuracy and detection speed, and a physically distributed six-node cloud deployment confirms feasibility.

</details>


### [99] [Artificial Intelligence Algorithms for the Detection of Pathologies Related to Lung Cancer through Image Analysis using Convolutional Neural Networks and Data Augmentation: a systematic mapping of the literature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10652)
*Pablo Ramirez Amador*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文综述2015年以来96篇文献，指出AI与深度学习（尤以CNN结合迁移学习与数据增强）在肺癌影像检测中具备高敏感度与特异度的应用潜力，但仍面临数据标准化、可解释性、隐私及伦理等挑战，需进一步研究与监管。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 肺癌是全球主要致死原因，早期诊断对改善预后与生活质量至关重要；但医学影像判读过程复杂且依赖受训专家，因此需要探索AI与深度学习以自动化并优化影像分析。

Method: 在PubMed、IEEEXPLORE、Scopus和Web of Science等科学数据库中进行详尽检索，筛选出2015年至今涉及AI与深度学习在生物医学工程中应用的96篇文献进行综述，重点讨论卷积神经网络（CNN）、迁移学习与数据增强等技术。

Result: 研究表明AI与深度学习可为肺癌早期诊断提供有效替代方案，具有较高的敏感度与特异度；同时识别出当前局限与挑战，包括缺乏标准化数据、模型可解释性不足、患者隐私以及伦理和社会影响。

Conclusion: AI与深度学习对肺癌患者诊疗可产生积极影响，但需开展进一步研究并建立监管机制，以确保其质量与可靠性，实现负责任且安全的临床应用。

Abstract: Lung cancer is one of the leading causes of death worldwide, and its early diagnosis is crucial to improving patients prognosis and quality of life. However, the process of interpreting medical images for the detection of lung cancer is complex and requires trained experts. In this context, artificial intelligence (AI) and deep learning (DL) emerge as potential tools to automate and optimize image analysis. The objective of this work is to review the most recent and relevant applications of AI and DL in the field of radiology for the detection of lung cancer. To this end, an exhaustive search was carried out in scientific databases such as PubMed,IEEEXPLORE, Scopus and Web of Science, and 96 articles published from 2015 to the present addressing the use of AI and DL in biomedical engineering were selected. Emphasis is placed on the use of convolutional neural networks (CNN) with transfer learning and Data Augmentation as promising techniques to improve the accuracy and efficiency of the image interpretation process. The results show that the use of AI and DL can offer an effective alternative for the early diagnosis of lung cancer, with high sensitivity and specificity. However, current limitations and challenges that must be addressed to guarantee its responsible and safe application in clinical practice are also identified, such as the lack of standardized data, the ex plainability of the models, patient privacy, and the ethical and social implications. It is concluded that the use of AI and DL can have a positive impact on the care of patients with lung cancer, but further research and regulation are required to ensure its quality and reliability.

</details>


### [100] [GEOSTEER: Geodesic Optimization for Activation Steering in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10658)
*Xuan Cuong Ngo,Hao Vo,Ngan Le*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GeoSteer：一种基于黎曼优化的保范激活引导方法，通过表示流形上的多步测地线更新实现自适应引导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有保范激活引导方法受限于预定义引导轨迹和一步更新，难以刻画激活分布的复杂结构。论文旨在保留激活范数（降低表示崩塌与退化风险）的同时，提升引导的有效性。

Method: 将激活引导建模为黎曼优化问题，在表示流形上通过一系列小测地线步长更新激活；学习一个非线性激活空间目标函数以区分期望与非期望激活，避免固定引导方向，并用该函数自适应地引导每一步。

Result: 在 TruthfulQA、RealToxicityPrompts 和 UltraFeedback 三个基准上，GeoSteer 持续优于最先进的激活引导基线。

Conclusion: 用自适应、几何感知的优化替代预定义的一步编辑，可使保范激活引导更有效，并获得更平滑、稳定且一致的引导行为。

Abstract: Activation steering provides a lightweight way to control large language models (LLMs) by modifying their hidden activations at inference time. Among these approaches, norm-preserving steering aims to change model behavior without altering the activation norm, reducing the risk of representation collapse and degradation. However, existing norm-preserving methods are limited by predefined steering trajectories and by their reliance on one-step updates, which may fail to capture the complex structure of activation distributions. We propose GeoSteer, an optimization-based method for norm-preserving activation steering. GeoSteer formulates steering as a Riemannian optimization problem and updates activations through a sequence of small geodesic steps on the representation manifold. To avoid fixed steering directions, GeoSteer learns a nonlinear activation-space objective that distinguishes desired from undesired activations, and uses this function to adaptively guide each steering step. This multistep formulation yields smoother, more stable, and more consistent steering behavior while preserving the activation norm. Across TruthfulQA, RealToxicityPrompts, and UltraFeedback benchmarks, GeoSteer consistently improves over state-of-the-art activation steering baselines. These results suggest that norm-preserving steering can be made more effective by replacing predefined one-step edits with adaptive, geometry-aware optimization.

</details>


### [101] [Conformal Calibration Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10737)
*Achref Doula*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出 Transported Conformal Calibration (TCC)，将源空间有标签的校准迁移到目标空间，并用无标签目标输入修正残差失配，在无目标标签校准的情况下提供目标域有限样本覆盖保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准 conformal prediction 依赖校准数据与部署数据之间的可交换性。但实际中标签校准数据仅在源空间可得，而预测集需要用于与源空间通过无标签配对观测关联的目标空间，该可交换性假设失效，因此需要研究 conformal calibration transfer。

Method: 提出 TCC：先利用配对数据把有标签的源校准迁移到目标空间，再仅用无标签目标输入修正迁移后的残差失配。具体实例化两种互补方法：TCC-KS 使用无标签不确定性代理检测失配并保守调整校准；weighted-TCC 在权重稳定时对迁移校准向目标域重加权以提升效率。

Result: 给出适应可观测失配度量的目标域有限样本覆盖保证。在 CIFAR-100-C、Tiny-ImageNet-C 和 SEN12MS 上验证，无需有标签目标校准数据即可实现可靠的目标域覆盖迁移，且无标签诊断能预测何时需要修正。

Conclusion: TCC 在源-目标配对但目标无标签的场景下实现了有效的 conformal calibration transfer，兼顾覆盖可靠性与效率，并提供可观测失配诊断以指导修正。

Abstract: Conformal prediction converts point predictions into set-valued predictions with coverage guarantees under exchangeability between calibration and deployment data. We study conformal calibration transfer, where this requirement fails because labeled calibration is available only in a source space, while prediction sets are needed in a target space linked to the source through unlabeled paired observations (e.g., paired modalities or sensor changes). We propose Transported Conformal Calibration (TCC): we transport labeled source calibration into the target space using the paired data, and then correct residual post-transport mismatch using only unlabeled target inputs. We instantiate this correction with two complementary methods: TCC-KS, which uses a label-free uncertainty surrogate to detect mismatch and adjust calibration conservatively, and weighted-TCC, which reweights transported calibration toward the target domain for improved efficiency when weights are stable. We provide finite-sample target-domain coverage guarantees that adapt to an observable measure of mismatch. Across CIFAR-100-C, Tiny-ImageNet-C, and SEN12MS, we show reliable target-domain coverage transfer without labeled target calibration data, with label-free diagnostics that predict when correction is needed.

</details>


### [102] [The Truth Was Never Gone: Perfect Aliasing in Compliant-Context Truth Probes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10739)
*Dylan Jayabahu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"完美别名"(perfect aliasing)概念：当真实报告与任务规定动作重合时，仅凭拟合标签无法区分二者。通过二值报告博弈、随机码本与混合上下文拟合，作者在 Gemma-2-9B 策略上验证了该问题的存在及其可分离性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真值探针(truth probe)常被用于检测模型是否"撒谎"。但当真实报告与任务规定动作在训练上下文中恰好重合时，探针学到的可能只是"规定动作"而非"真值"，导致语义识别失败。作者希望揭示这种失败（完美别名），并探索如何将真值、规定输出符号与语义动作三者区分开来。

Method: 在受控二值报告博弈中，分别在合规上下文与对立上下文上拟合真值探针与规定动作探针；在对立上下文中二者标签互为补集，故 AUROC 之和应为 1（在 751 组 cell-layer 上验证）。使用随机码本将规定输出符号与语义动作分离；再通过在合规与对立上下文混合拟合，将真值与规定动作分离。

Result: 对立上下文下两探针 AUROC 之和恒为 1，跨 751 组 cell-layer 达到浮点精度，验证完美别名。对一个在所有评估对立试验中均错误作答的奖励训练 Gemma-2-9B 策略，常规探针在三个训练种子上得 0.006 ± 0.005 AUROC，而混合拟合探针在同一留出激活上达 1.000。两个在分布内均完美的合规拟合探针，在对立激活上分别得 0.080 与 0.986。

Conclusion: 该研究关注探针"测量的是什么"，证明了真值与规定动作的线性可恢复性，但未证明功能性信念保留、恢复方向的因果使用，也不构成可部署的欺骗检测器。混合拟合使用了更多样本并需标签化对立上下文，因此该比较仅确立可恢复性而非去相关的独立收益。

Abstract: A truth probe fitted where truthful reporting and a task's prescribed action coincide cannot distinguish those targets from its fitting labels alone. We call this failure of semantic identification perfect aliasing. In a controlled binary reporting game, truth and prescribed-action probes fitted on compliant contexts solve the same optimization. On rival contexts their labels are complements, forcing their AUROCs to sum to one; this identity holds across 751 cell-layer pairs to floating-point precision. We separate prescribed output symbols from semantic action using randomized codebooks, then separate truth from prescribed action by fitting on mixed compliant and rival contexts. For a reward-trained Gemma-2-9B policy that answers falsely on all evaluated rival trials, the conventional probe scores $0.006 \pm 0.005$ AUROC across three training seeds, while mixed-fit probes score $1.000$ on the same held-out activations. Mixed fitting uses more training examples and access to labelled rival contexts, so this comparison establishes linear recoverability rather than isolating the benefit of decorrelation. We also show that two compliant-fit probes, both perfect in-distribution, score $0.080$ and $0.986$ on the same rival activations. The findings concern what a probe measures: they do not establish preserved functional belief, causal use of the recovered direction, or a deployable deception detector. Code and aggregate results accompany the paper.

</details>


### [103] [Adaptive Margin Ordinal Loss: Penalizing Center-Class Hedging in Ordinal Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10752)
*Manisha Kandel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出自适应边距序数损失AMOL及中心对冲率CHR指标，有效抑制序数分类中的中心类对冲现象，在四个基准数据集上取得最佳或并列最佳的QWK。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准交叉熵损失在序数分类任务中会使网络将预测向中心类靠拢（即"中心类对冲"），因为预测中间类可使对称损失的期望最小化，与真实标签无关。现有序数损失虽能解决大误差惩罚和排名一致性等问题，但均未直接根据真实标签相对序数中心的位置来抑制中心类对冲。

Method: 提出自适应边距序数损失（AMOL），对每个类的损失项施加乘法权重 m(k,y) = 1 + α·(1 - |k-c|/c)·(|y-c|/c)，其中c为中心类，k为候选类，y为真实标签。该权重编码联合条件：仅当候选类接近中心且真实标签远离中心时权重较大，否则退化为标准行为。同时提出中心对冲率（CHR）作为直接量化该失效模式的诊断指标。

Result: 在四个序数分类基准和五个随机种子上，AMOL在全部四个数据集上的二次加权Kappa（QWK）均达到最佳或并列最佳，优于交叉熵、OLL和SORD基线。非对称变体AMOL-asym在Abalone数据集上完全消除中心类对冲（CHR=0.000±0.000，五个种子，每次运行约266个极端类测试样本），而标准交叉熵为0.074±0.005。

Conclusion: AMOL通过联合条件加权有效抑制中心类对冲，AMOL-asym可实现完全消除，同时保持甚至提升序数分类的QWK性能，CHR指标为诊断该失效模式提供了直接量化工具。

Abstract: Standard cross-entropy loss causes neural networks trained on ordinal classification tasks to hedge predictions toward center classes, a failure mode we term \emph{center-class hedging}. This occurs because predicting the middle class minimizes expected symmetric loss, making it the path of least resistance regardless of the true label. Existing ordinal losses address related problems such as large-error penalization and rank consistency, but none directly suppresses center-class hedging as a function of where the true label lies relative to the ordinal center. We propose the Adaptive Margin Ordinal Loss (AMOL), a multiplicative weight applied to per-class loss terms of the form $m(k,y) = 1 + α\cdot (1 - |k-c|/c) \cdot (|y-c|/c)$, where $c$ is the center class, $k$ is the candidate class, and $y$ is the true label. The weight encodes a joint condition: it is large only when the candidate class is near center and the true label is far from center, collapsing to standard behavior otherwise. We further introduce the Center-Hedging Rate (CHR) as a diagnostic metric that directly quantifies this failure mode. Across four ordinal classification benchmarks and five random seeds, AMOL achieves the best or tied-best Quadratic Weighted Kappa (QWK) on all four datasets compared to cross-entropy, OLL, and SORD baselines. An asymmetric variant (AMOL-asym) eliminates center-class hedging entirely on the Abalone dataset ($\text{CHR} = 0.000 \pm 0.000$ across all five seeds, $n \approx 266$ extreme-class test samples per run), compared to $0.074 \pm 0.005$ for standard cross-entropy.

</details>


### [104] [A Bellman Optimality Equation for Plasticity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10776)
*Jeremy Lucas,Doina Precup*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文在持续强化学习中将稳定性-可塑性权衡重新表述为赋权-可塑性权衡，并首次针对可塑性优化给出类似贝尔曼最优方程的初步结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续强化学习中稳定性-可塑性权衡是核心难题；Abel等（2025）已将其形式化为赋权-可塑性权衡，但现有研究仅优化赋权，尚无在此新定义下优化可塑性的工作。

Method: 基于广义有向信息定义（可塑性为观测到动作的广义有向信息，赋权为动作到观测的广义有向信息），在马尔可夫决策过程框架下进行初步理论研究。

Result: 证明存在一个用于优化可塑性的贝尔曼最优方程，其形式与既有赋权优化工作类似。

Conclusion: 该工作为在新定义下优化可塑性提供了初步理论基础，将赋权优化的方法论扩展至可塑性优化，为后续持续强化学习算法设计铺路。

Abstract: In continual reinforcement learning, carefully managing the stability-plasticity tradeoff remains a core challenge. Recent work by Abel et al. (2025) formalized this dilemma by defining plasticity as the generalized directed information from an agent's observations to its actions, and empowerment as the generalized directed information from its actions to its observations. This formulation successfully reframes the traditional stability-plasticity tradeoff as an empowerment-plasticity tradeoff. However, while extensive literature exists on optimizing for empowerment, there is currently no research addressing the optimization of plasticity under this new definition. This paper presents preliminary work toward optimizing plasticity within Markov decision processes. We show that there exists a Bellman optimality equation for optimizing plasticity similar to previous work for empowerment.

</details>


### [105] [Counterfactual Marginalisation: Framework for Evaluating Robustness to Nuisance Variables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10778)
*Yasin Ibrahim,Hermione Warr,Robin J. Evans,Konstantinos Kamnitsas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出反事实边缘化（CF marginalisation）作为测试时评估方法，用于衡量分类模型对人口学或采集相关捷径变量的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习模型可能在测试集上表现良好，但其实依赖人口学属性（如年龄、性别）或采集相关因素等捷径特征，导致鲁棒性被高估，亟需针对这类变量进行定量评估。

Method: 借助反事实图像生成器，对每个测试图像干预其干扰父变量（如年龄、性别），生成对应的反事实版本，并在目标干预分布上对预测取平均，得到干预感知的预测结果。该过程在边缘化人口学效应的同时保留患者特异性潜在信息，并据此定义反事实风险、校准度、稳定性与最差情况敏感性等指标。

Result: 该框架可生成干预感知预测，并支撑反事实风险、校准、稳定性与最差情况敏感性的量化指标，作者通过实验展示了其在定量鲁棒性评估中的实用性。

Conclusion: 反事实边缘化为分类模型针对人口学与采集相关捷径变量的鲁棒性提供了一种有效的测试时评估框架，可服务于定量鲁棒性评价。

Abstract: Machine learning models can achieve strong test performance while relying on demographic or acquisition-related shortcuts. We propose counterfactual (CF) marginalisation as a test-time evaluation procedure for assessing robustness of classification models to such variables. Given a CF image generator, we intervene on nuisance parent variables such as age or sex, generate CF versions of each test image, and average predictions over a target intervention distribution. This produces intervention-aware predictions that marginalise demographic effects while preserving patient-specific latent information. We use these predictions to define metrics for CF risk, calibration, stability and worst-case sensitivity. We demonstrate this framework's utility for quantitative robustness evaluation.

</details>


### [106] [Flow Duality and Source Geometry for Categorical Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10863)
*Etrit Haxholli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了连续与离散流匹配之间的对偶关系：将带one-hot目标的连续凸插值路径经逐位置argmax投影，可得到离散凸插值路径；该结论要求源分布具备坐标对称性与边界正则性，并使连续源分布成为类别生成中的显式设计选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续流匹配与离散流匹配通常被视为两套彼此独立的构造，缺乏统一的理论联系；本文旨在建立二者的对偶关系，并把连续源分布的选择提升为类别生成中可主动设计的关键因素。

Method: 以带one-hot目标的连续凸插值路径为对象，通过逐位置argmax投影构造离散路径；在源分布满足坐标对称性与边界正则性的条件下证明其对应离散凸插值。随后推导高斯源、有界均匀源与中心化负指数源所诱导的离散插值行为，分析不同源几何对转移时序与词表规模依赖的影响，并辅以小规模可视化诊断与简短语言建模试验。

Result: 理论上，argmax投影在给定条件下将连续凸插值路径映射为离散凸插值路径，并给出源分布选择的形式化约束。具体源的推导显示：不同源几何会导致定性不同的转移时序与词表规模依赖。在可视化诊断与语言建模初步试验中，这些源设计效应也可在学习到的传输过程与早期生成质量中体现。

Conclusion: 连续与离散流匹配之间存在由argmax投影维系的投影对偶，连续源分布因此可作为类别生成中显式的设计变量；源几何会系统性地影响转移时序、词表规模依赖以及学习传输与早期生成质量。

Abstract: Continuous and discrete flow matching are usually treated as separate constructions. This paper identifies a duality between them: projecting continuous convex-interpolant paths with one-hot targets through a position-wise argmax yields discrete convex-interpolant paths. The result requires source laws with appropriate coordinate symmetry and boundary regularity, and it makes the continuous source distribution an explicit design choice for categorical generation. We derive the induced discrete interpolation behavior for Gaussian, bounded-uniform, and centered negative-exponential sources, showing that different source geometries lead to qualitatively different transition timing and vocabulary-size dependence. Small visual diagnostics and a short language-modeling pilot suggest that these source-design effects can also appear in learned transports and early generative quality.

</details>


### [107] [From Connectivity to Rewards: Dense Reward Learning with Directed State Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10781)
*Shuyuan Zhang,Zihan Wang,Xiao-Wen Chang,Doina Precup*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出图引导拟度量稠密奖励框架G2QDR，通过状态连通性模型预测非对称环境中状态间的连通强度，并将其转化为标量辅助稠密奖励，为分层强化学习提供跨层级连续引导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有的图与目标条件分层强化学习（GCHRL）结合方法多将图作为随机采样工具，忽略了图中蕴含的内在连通性与状态可达性信息，未能充分利用拓扑结构提升学习效率；这一问题在拟度量（quasimetric）环境中尤为突出，因为状态转移的固有非对称性给稳定策略学习和鲁棒路径规划带来根本性挑战。

Method: 作者引入状态连通性模型，用于预测非对称环境中成对状态的连通强度；将连通强度转化为标量辅助稠密奖励，在多个分层层级上提供连续引导；该框架（G2QDR）理论上可集成到任意现有GCHRL架构中，状态连通性模型由在探索过程中生成的有向状态图上训练的神经网络高效实现。

Result: 在多种稀疏奖励环境上的实证结果表明，G2QDR总体上能在可接受的计算开销下提升基线GCHRL方法的性能。

Conclusion: G2QDR通过显式建模状态连通性并将其用作分层稠密奖励，弥补了现有图基GCHRL方法忽视连通信息的缺陷，具备良好的通用性与可扩展性，可作为通用模块增强现有方法。

Abstract: The integration of graphs with Goal-Conditioned Hierarchical Reinforcement Learning (GCHRL) has received increasing attention, as graphs naturally encode task hierarchies for effective subgoal sampling. However, existing methods often overlook intrinsic connectivity information, failing to fully leverage the underlying topology for efficient learning. Most graph-based GCHRL methods use the graph as a stochastic sampling tool rather than as an environmental model that encodes connectivity and state-accessibility information. This limitation is particularly acute in quasimetric environments, where the inherent asymmetry of state transitions poses a fundamental challenge to stable policy learning and robust path planning. In this paper, we address these problems by introducing a state connectivity model designed to predict pairwise state connectivity strength in asymmetric environments. We transform these connectivity strengths into scalar auxiliary dense rewards, providing continuous guidance across multiple hierarchical levels. We demonstrate that our proposed framework, Graph-Guided Quasimetric Dense Reward (G2QDR), can theoretically be integrated into any existing GCHRL architecture, and the state connectivity model is efficiently implemented via a neural network trained on a directed state graph generated during exploration. Empirical results across a wide range of sparse reward environments indicate that, in general, G2QDR can enhance the performance of baseline GCHRL approaches with acceptable computational overhead.

</details>


### [108] [Learning Orthogonal Multi-Index Models Beyond Small Initialization: Incremental Learning, Competitive Dynamics and Symmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10879)
*Mo Zhou,Weihang Xu,Simon S. Du,Maryam Fazel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究标准初始化下多项式宽度两层网络学习正交多指标目标的训练动力学，证明增量学习仍然发生（按Hermite展开逐阶递减），并揭示参数质量的竞争性再分配过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有关于浅层网络增量学习的工作多依赖简化设定（小初始化、相关损失或逐层训练），这些设定削弱了神经元间的相互作用，导致标准初始化下的特征学习动力学尚未被充分探索。

Method: 在标准初始化、多项式样本量下，分析两层网络学习正交多指标目标的训练动力学；理论分析采用略作修改的梯度流，并引入基于对称性的有限宽度近似（对称化网络），而非直接与无穷宽度极限比较。实验上验证了朴素梯度下降具有相同的定性动力学。

Result: 证明了增量学习在标准初始化下依然发生：损失按目标的Hermite展开顺序递减，低阶分量先于高阶分量被学出，进而恢复各个目标方向；训练还表现出参数质量的竞争性再分配——总质量先拟合目标均值并稳定，随后质量转移到目标子空间，最终集中在对齐的神经元上。

Conclusion: 标准初始化下浅层网络的增量学习及其参数质量再分配动力学得到理论刻画；基于对称性的有限宽度近似方法能更好地控制近似误差，具有独立的研究价值。

Abstract: Recent work has identified incremental learning in shallow networks trained on single-index and multi-index models. However, existing analyses often rely on simplifying settings, such as small initialization, correlation loss, or layer-wise training. These choices reduce neuron interactions and leave some feature learning dynamics under standard initialization unexplored. We study training dynamics for polynomial-width two-layer networks learning orthogonal multi-index targets under standard initialization using polynomially many samples. We first prove that incremental learning still occurs: the loss decreases sequentially according to the Hermite expansion of the target, with lower-order components learned before higher-order components recover the individual target directions. In this standard initialization regime, training also shows a competitive reallocation of parameter mass: after the total mass fits the target mean and stabilizes, mass shifts into the target subspace and then concentrates on aligned neurons. Our theoretical analysis uses slightly modified gradient flow, while vanilla gradient descent empirically exhibits the same qualitative dynamics. Technically, we introduce a symmetry-based finite-width approximation via symmetrized networks, rather than comparing directly with an infinite-width limit. This yields better control of approximation errors and may be of independent interest.

</details>


### [109] [DR-LabStack: Design and Implementation of a Clinician-Facing Web System for Diabetic Retinopathy Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10796)
*Yingfan Xu,Tieming Liu,Ye Liang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文设计并实现 DR-LabStack（React-Flask Web 系统），通过共享表单、后端适配器与统一 JSON 响应，将四个异构的预训练糖尿病视网膜病变（DR）预测模型集成到同一临床界面，并完成功能性评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 DR 预训练预测模型在输入字段、序列化格式、预处理要求和输出语义上各不相同，难以通过统一临床界面访问，因此需要显式协调用户界面与推理服务之间的差异。

Method: 采用 React 前端与 Flask 后端架构。共享表单按模型检索有序特征，动态渲染数值型与分类型控件并构造位置化输入向量；后端适配器加载异构模型工件，对集成模型应用其配套 scaler；统一 JSON 响应支持二分类结果展示及方法与来源信息。评估使用复制的应用文件与真实模型工件，在文档化的隔离环境中进行。

Result: 四个模型均成功加载并暴露 14、6、8、25 字段的输入契约；62 次 Flask 测试客户端请求刻画了服务行为；12 次有限向量检查验证了调用路径与阈值一致性；24 个浏览器组件场景（配合 mocked transport）验证了输入顺序与结果渲染，并刻画了输入校验行为。

Conclusion: 该研究表明可为异构 DR 模型提供可复用的交互与服务化工作流；其贡献在于 Web 系统设计、集成与软件功能实现，而临床有效性与临床医生可用性仍需另行评估。

Abstract: Pretrained diabetic retinopathy (DR) prediction models differ in their input fields, serialization formats, preprocessing requirements, and output semantics. Making these models accessible through a common clinical interface therefore requires explicit coordination between the user interface and the inference service. We designed and implemented DR-LabStack, a React-Flask web system integrating four externally developed pretrained models: RuleFit, Pruned RuleFit, Elaborative XGBoost, and Two-level Ensemble. A shared form retrieves ordered model features, renders model-specific numerical and categorical controls, and constructs a positional input vector. Backend adapters load heterogeneous artifacts and apply the ensemble's accompanying scaler, while a common JSON response supports binary classification display alongside method and source information. Functional evaluation on September 8, 2026 used copied application files and real model artifacts in a documented isolated environment. All four models loaded and exposed their 14-, 6-, 8-, and 25-field contracts. Sixty-two Flask test-client requests characterized service behavior; 12 limited-vector checks confirmed invocation-path and threshold consistency. Twenty-four browser-component scenarios with mocked transport verified input ordering and result rendering and characterized input-validation behavior. The resulting system demonstrates a reusable interaction and serving workflow for heterogeneous DR models. The contribution is web-system design, integration, and software functionality; clinical effectiveness and clinician usability require separate evaluation.

</details>


### [110] [Relatively Smart II: Tractable or Semi-Supervised Instance-Optimal Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10886)
*Shaddin Dughmi,Alireza F. Pour*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究"相对聪明学习"：要求监督学习器逐边际地匹敌任何可从无标注数据可靠认证的分布固定误差保证。作者证明ERM及任何恰当一致学习器在二分类无分布设定下是相对聪明的，并可实现半监督设定下仅无标注样本复杂度有二次膨胀而标注样本无膨胀，但该标注效率需以可计算性为代价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Dughmi和Pour（2026）提出相对聪明学习，证明One-Inclusion Graph（OIG）学习器以二次样本复杂度膨胀实现相对聪明，且无学习器能做得更好，但遗留两个开放问题：ERM或其他自然、可计算的学习器能否达到可比保证；以及膨胀能否仅限制在无标注数据上。本文旨在解决这两个问题。

Method: 1）对二分类无分布设定，证明任意恰当一致学习器（含ERM）是相对聪明的：以m个样本得到的小可认证误差蕴含在大小为O(m^2)的随机样本均匀分布上的小误差，从而在该样本上得到至多2^{m+1}的覆盖，控制恰当一致学习器的误差。2）将OIG推广到leave-most-out转导问题（揭示有限池中部分标签并预测剩余标签），构造半监督相对聪明学习器，仅无标注样本复杂度二次膨胀、标注样本无膨胀。3）通过agnostic ERM oracle访问假设类，证明次二次标注样本膨胀需超多项式次oracle调用。

Result: ERM及任何恰当一致学习器在二分类无分布设定下是相对聪明的，以O(m^2)样本控制误差。半监督相对聪明学习在信息论上可实现：仅无标注样本复杂度二次膨胀、标注样本复杂度无膨胀。但若仅通过agnostic ERM oracle访问假设类，任何标注样本膨胀显著次二次的半监督相对聪明学习器都需超多项式次oracle调用；该结论在边际显式给出时亦成立，并给出分布固定学习的不可计算性结果。

Conclusion: 本文正面回答了Dughmi和Pour的两个开放问题：ERM等自然学习器确实可达到相对聪明保证；膨胀可限制在无标注数据。同时揭示标注效率与简洁性、可计算性之间的权衡：半监督相对聪明的标注效率提升需付出超多项式oracle复杂度的代价，并附带分布固定学习的不可计算性结果。

Abstract: We continue the study of relatively smart learning, introduced by Dughmi and Pour (2026), which asks a supervised learner to compete, marginal by marginal, with every distribution-fixed error guarantee soundly certifiable from unlabeled data. They showed that the One-Inclusion Graph (OIG) learner is relatively smart with a quadratic sample-complexity blowup, and that no relatively smart learner can do better, leaving open whether ERM or another natural or tractable learner achieves comparable guarantees. They also left open whether the blowup can be restricted to unlabeled data.
  Our firs results shows that ERM---and in fact any proper consistent learner---is relatively smart for binary classification in the distribution-free setting. We show that a small certifiable error with $m$ samples implies a similarly small error on the uniform distribution over a random sample of size $O(m^2)$, yielding a cover of size at most $2^{m+1}$ on that sample. This suffices to control the error of proper consistent learners with $O(m^2)$ samples.
  We then show that semi-supervised relatively smart learning is information-theoretically possible with a quadratic blowup only in unlabeled sample complexity and no blowup in labeled sample complexity. The learner uses a natural generalization of OIG to a leave-most-out transductive problem, where labels of part of a finite pool are revealed and the remaining labels are predicted.
  Finally, this label efficiency comes at a cost in simplicity and tractability. If the hypothesis class is accessed only through an agnostic ERM oracle, any semi-supervised relatively smart learner with substantially sub-quadratic labeled-sample blowup requires super-polynomially many oracle calls. This holds even when the marginal is given explicitly, and thus also yields an intractability result for distribution-fixed learning that may be of independent interest.

</details>


### [111] [RiVaT-Fuse: Reliability-Calibrated Variational Tensor Fusion for Multimodal Prediction under Modality Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10798)
*Yingfan Xu,Tieming Liu,Ye Liang,Taiping Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出RiVaT-Fuse可靠性校准变分张量融合框架，将图像-元数据融合定义为逐样本的隐状态估计问题，用变分目标平衡多源证据与稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有表示级融合方法（拼接、门控、条件调制、注意力）只选择聚合架构，未明确在模态不确定性下融合表示应有的语义，且难以处理跨样本、跨潜在因素变化的证据可靠性。

Method: 以变分目标估计共识隐状态，平衡图像证据、元数据证据、结构化跨模态交互与稳定性；用矩阵值信任几何替代标量模态置信度；将交互分解为加性、乘性、关系三部分；将隐状态与条件鲁棒性及结构化多任务预测耦合；给出隐式求解的适定性与稳定性解释，并用低秩加对角信任算子高效实例化。

Result: 在图像级图像-元数据预测基准上，取得直接表示级基线中最强的整体预测排名，同时在扰动下提升概率与标签稳定性。

Conclusion: 将融合重构为隐状态估计可提供更明确的模态不确定性语义，矩阵值信任几何与结构化交互分解带来更优的预测排名与鲁棒稳定性。

Abstract: Image-metadata prediction requires fusing heterogeneous evidence whose reliability can vary across samples and latent factors. Existing representation-level fusion methods typically choose an aggregation architecture, such as concatenation, gating, conditional modulation, or attention, without explicitly defining what the fused representation should mean under modality uncertainty. We propose RiVaT-Fuse, a reliability-calibrated variational tensor fusion framework that defines fusion as sample-wise latent-state estimation. Rather than producing a fused vector by direct aggregation, RiVaT-Fuse estimates a consensus latent state through a variational objective that balances image evidence, metadata evidence, structured cross-modal interaction, and stability. The resulting framework replaces scalar modality confidence with matrix-valued trust geometry, decomposes interaction into additive, multiplicative, and relational components, and couples the latent state with conditional robustness and structured multi-task prediction. We provide well-posedness and stability interpretations of the latent solve and instantiate the framework with efficient low-rank-plus-diagonal trust operators. On an image-level image-metadata prediction benchmark, RiVaT-Fuse achieves the strongest overall predictive rank among direct representation-level baselines while improving probability and label stability under perturbation.

</details>


### [112] [AUC Maximization from Biased Positive-unlabeled Data with Confidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10928)
*Atsutoshi Kumagai,Tomoharu Iwata,Hiroshi Takahashi,Taishi Nishiyama,Kazuki Adachi,Yasuhiro Fujiwara*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种从有偏正例-无标签(PU)数据中最大化AUC的方法，利用少量有标签正例的置信度(置信概率)推导AUC风险估计器，并证明其可诱导贝叶斯最优排序，在8个真实数据集上验证有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AUC最大化是处理不平衡二分类的常用手段，但需要正负样本。现实中负样本因隐私或需专家标注而难以获取，故PU数据下的AUC最大化受到关注。然而现有方法假设有标签正例是真实正例分布的无偏采样，这一理想假设在现实中常被违背，导致偏差问题。

Method: 针对有偏PU数据，核心思想是利用与少量有标签正例相关的置信度（即实例为正的概率）。基于带置信度的有偏PU数据推导AUC风险估计器，从而在该偏差下实现AUC最大化。并进一步证明，即使可用置信度是真实后验概率的任意严格递增变换，重写后的AUC风险仍能诱导贝叶斯最优的AUC排序。

Result: 在八个真实数据集上的实验验证了所提方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提方法放宽了PU学习中正例无偏采样的理想假设，通过引入置信度实现对有偏PU数据的AUC最大化，并具备对置信度单调变换的稳健性与贝叶斯最优性保证，实验证明其有效性。

Abstract: Maximizing the area under the receiver operating characteristic curve (AUC) is a standard approach to imbalanced binary classification. Although positive and negative data are required for maximizing the AUC, negative data are often difficult to collect in some real-world applications due to privacy concerns or the need for specialized expertise to annotate them. Thus, AUC maximization from positive and unlabeled (PU) data has been attracting attention. Existing methods assume that labeled positive data are unbiased samples from the true positive distribution. However, this ideal assumption is often violated in practice. In this paper, we propose a method to maximize the AUC from biased PU data. To address the bias, our key idea is to exploit {\it confidence}, i.e., the probability that an instance is positive, associated with the small number of labeled positive data. We derive an estimator of the AUC risk using biased PU data with confidence, enabling AUC maximization under such bias. We further show that the rewritten AUC risk induces a Bayes-optimal AUC ranking even when the available confidence is any strictly increasing transformation of the true posterior probability. We experimentally show the effectiveness of our method on eight real-world datasets.

</details>


### [113] [Processing and classifying bird songs using wavelet techniques and supervised learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10826)
*Laura Lucia Dominguez Barrios,Fidel Aniano Causil Barrios,Alex Rodrigo dos Santos Sousa,Mariana Rodrigues Motta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于Epanechnikov核先验的贝叶斯小波收缩去噪方法，用于高噪声自然声景中入侵鸟类的鸣声处理与分类，SVM在10维MFCC下取得最高0.9398准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然声景中环境噪声高，导致鸟类鸣声信号退化，影响入侵鸟类的自动化生态监测与生物入侵管理，因此需要一个鲁棒的去噪与分类一体化框架。

Method: 采用基于Epanechnikov核先验的贝叶斯小波收缩方法进行信号去噪（具闭式决策规则、计算高效）；对iNaturalist平台上的三种鸟类（Euphonia violacea、Leiothrix lutea、Passer domesticus）录音处理；去噪后提取MFCC及熵、过零率等谱特征；比较随机森林、多项逻辑回归与SVM在不同特征维度下的表现。

Result: 小波去噪预处理显著提升分类性能，SVM在10维MFCC配置下准确率最高，达0.9398。

Conclusion: 该研究为自动化生态监测与生物入侵管理提供了一种稳健的统计工具。

Abstract: This study proposes an integrated framework for the processing and classification of invasive bird species vocalizations within natural soundscapes, characterized by high levels of environmental noise. We address the challenge of signal degradation by employing a Bayesian wavelet shrinkage methodology based on the Epanechnikov kernel prior, which offers a closed form decision rule and high computational efficiency for processing large bioacoustic datasets. The methodology was applied to recordings of three species obtained from the iNaturalist platform: \textit{Euphonia violacea}, \textit{Leiothrix lutea}, and \textit{Passer domesticus}. After signal denoising, we extracted a comprehensive set of features, including Mel-Frequency Cepstral Coefficients (MFCCs) and spectral indices such as entropy and zero-crossing rate. Several supervised learning models: Random Forest, Multinomial Logistic Regression and Support Vector Machine (SVM) were evaluated across different feature dimensionalities. Our results demonstrate that the proposed wavelet based preprocessing significantly enhances classification performance, with the SVM model achieving the highest accuracy (up to 0.9398) under a 10-dimensional MFCC configuration. This research provides a robust statistical tool for automated ecological monitoring and the management of biological invasions.

</details>


### [114] [Phases in a class of associative memories via hidden neurons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10976)
*Toshihiro Ota,Masato Taki*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出并系统分析了一类二分架构Hopfield网络（类H），该架构由逐层Lagrangian确定；多项式负载下用复本方法给出复本对称相图与闭式容量，指数负载下用副本表示映射到随机能量模型计数，揭示可见层Lagrangian决定稳定性、隐层决定存储规模，二者构成两个独立设计轴。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Hopfield网络的联想记忆是高维无序多体系统中的吸引子动力学，其高阶与指数扩展将检索更新转化为softmax注意力。但多项式与指数两种机制此前分别由不同方法分析，缺乏统一的架构来追问"是什么决定了存储规模"这一核心问题。

Method: 研究Krotov-Hopfield二分架构（称为类H），该模型由每层的Lagrangian固定，将隐神经元作为检索的序参量。多项式负载下采用复本方法（replica method）求复本对称相图与闭式容量；指数负载下使用softmax隐层，并通过副本表示（copy representation）将热力学映射到随机能量模型计数。

Result: 在多项式负载下给出复本对称相图与闭式容量，发现Ising与球形可见神经元的串扰矩相同，二者差异来自可见熵。softmax隐层使负载呈指数级，出现顺磁、凝聚与冻结三种相。升温通过注意力的量子化重分配破坏检索，典型高斯模式在任何负载下均保持亚稳。两机制的串扰统计不同：多项式负载为中心极限，指数负载为大偏差。

Conclusion: 类H将检索拆分为两个角色：可见层Lagrangian决定稳定性，隐层Lagrangian决定存储规模。这两个轴可为设计新的Lagrangian提供指导，统一了多项式与指数负载两种机制的分析框架。

Abstract: Associative memory in the Hopfield network is attractor dynamics in a disordered many-body system, and higher-order and exponential extensions turn its retrieval update into softmax attention. The polynomial and exponential regimes have been analyzed by different methods, with no common architecture in which to ask what fixes the storage scale. In this paper we study the bipartite architecture of Krotov and Hopfield, which we call the class $H$, whose model is fixed by a Lagrangian for each layer, taking the hidden neurons as the order parameter of retrieval. At polynomial load the replica method yields the replica-symmetric phase diagrams and closed-form capacities, and the crosstalk moment is common to Ising and spherical visible neurons, so their differences come from the visible entropy. With a softmax hidden layer the load is exponential, and a copy representation maps the thermodynamics onto random-energy-model counting, with paramagnetic, condensed, and frozen phases. Heating destabilizes retrieval by quantized reassignments of attention, and typical Gaussian patterns remain metastable at every load. The regimes differ in their crosstalk statistics, central-limit at polynomial load and large-deviation at exponential load, and the class $H$ splits retrieval into two roles, the visible Lagrangian fixing stability and the hidden one the storage scale, two axes that may also guide the design of new Lagrangians.

</details>


### [115] [Importance Weighting for Unlabeled-unlabeled Learning under Distribution Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10994)
*Atsutoshi Kumagai,Tomoharu Iwata,Hiroshi Takahashi,Taishi Nishiyama,Kazuki Adachi,Yasuhiro Fujiwara*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文提出面向UU（无标签-无标签）学习的分布偏移自适应方法。方法基于重要性加权，只需训练分布的UU数据与少量测试分布的UU数据，即可最小化测试风险，且无需假设偏移类型，可统一处理PU、噪声标签等多种学习问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有UU学习假设测试与训练分布的类条件密度相同，但现实中的分布偏移使该假设难以成立；而已有重要性加权方法无法直接处理UU数据，且现有偏移适应方法通常只针对特定问题定制。

Method: 利用训练分布中的UU数据和测试分布中的少量UU数据，以重要性加权为核心，用估计的重要性权重对训练数据加权以最小化测试风险；作者证明在UU设定下该加权可被有原则地实现，且不依赖协变量偏移等具体偏移类型假设。

Result: 在真实数据集上验证了所提方法在分布偏移下UU学习中的有效性，并展示了其在PU学习、噪声标签学习等问题上的适用性。

Conclusion: 所提方法为UU学习提供了通用的分布偏移适应框架，凭借UU学习的通用性可统一处理多种学习问题，且无需假设偏移类型，具有较好的实用性与通用性。

Abstract: Unlabeled-unlabeled (UU) learning allows us to learn a binary classifier from two sets of unlabeled data with different class-priors. It is a general framework because it includes a wide variety of supervised learning such as positive-unlabeled (PU) learning, noisy label learning, and similarity-based learning. Existing UU learning assumes that the test and training distributions have the same class-conditional densities. However, this assumption rarely holds in practice due to distribution shifts. This paper proposes a distribution shift adaptation method for UU learning that uses UU data in the training distribution and a few UU data in the test distribution. The proposed method is based on the importance weighting, which minimizes the test risk by using training data with estimated importance weights. Although existing importance weighting methods cannot handle UU data, we show that it can be done in a principled manner. Thanks to the generality of UU learning, our method can handle various learning problems such as PU and noisy label learning under distribution shift within a single framework while existing methods are usually tailored to a specific problem. Moreover, it does not require any assumption of the shift types such as covariate shift. We experimentally demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed method with real-world datasets.

</details>


### [116] [Certifying Lower Bounds for Risk-Sensitive Reinforcement Learning under Adversarial State Perturbations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10866)
*Tong Li,Saunak Kumar Panda,Yisha Xiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文将强化学习的对抗鲁棒性认证从风险中性目标扩展到风险敏感目标，通过在状态扰动下建立累积奖励指数效用的下界，提出可凸优化的认证方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实环境中的RL智能体易受状态观测的对抗扰动影响，在安全攸关场景中风险较大。现有认证方法主要针对风险中性目标，无法刻画风险敏感需求，因此需要将认证方法扩展到风险敏感目标。

Method: 在l_p范数有界状态扰动（1≤p<∞）下，建立累积奖励指数效用的下界；引入扰动集的φ-散度松弛，将风险敏感认证问题转化为凸优化问题，并推导其对偶形式以获得可处理的认证下界近似；进一步提出一种经验方法，通过使训练阶段的风险厌恶参数β与评估阶段所用风险水平独立，来改善认证下界。

Result: 在OpenAI Gym环境和机器更换问题上的实验表明：相比风险中性训练，风险厌恶训练通常能得到认证下界更高的策略，且在较大扰动预算下尤为明显；在风险中性与风险厌恶两种评估设定下，训练时增大风险厌恶程度会导致认证性能呈非单调变化，认证下界先提升，随后因策略过度保守而下降。

Conclusion: 该工作将认证方法推广到风险敏感目标，并给出可处理的凸优化求解途径；实验揭示训练风险厌恶程度与认证下界之间存在非单调关系，说明需在保守性与认证性能之间权衡。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) agents deployed in real-world environments are often vulnerable to adversarial perturbations in state observations, creating risks in safety-critical applications. Certification methods can improve robustness against adversarial perturbations by providing lower bounds on expected cumulative rewards. Existing certification methods, however, mainly focus on risk-neutral objectives. In this paper, we extend certification methods to risk-sensitive objectives by establishing lower bounds on the exponential utility of cumulative rewards under $l_{p}$-norm-bounded state adversarial perturbations ($1\leq p <\infty$). By introducing a $φ$-divergence relaxation of the perturbation set, we formulate the risk-sensitive certification problem as a convex optimization and derive its dual to obtain a tractable approximation of the certified lower bound. We further propose an empirical method that improves certified lower bounds by selecting the training risk-aversion parameter $β$ independently of the risk level used during evaluation. Experiments on both OpenAI Gym environments and a machine replacement problem show that, compared to risk-neutral training, risk-averse training generally yields policies with higher certified lower bounds, particularly under larger perturbation budgets. Moreover, under both risk-neutral and risk-averse evaluation settings, increasing risk aversion during training leads to non-monotonic certification performance, where certified lower bounds initially improve but eventually decrease due to overly conservative policies.

</details>


### [117] [How Wrong Can a Good Predictor Be? Diverging Updates with Vanishing Predictive KL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11132)
*Qifu Wen,Shuaijun Liu,Zihan Zhou,Xi Zeng,Ningxin Su*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在平稳对称高斯HMM中，精确贝叶斯滤波与一个确定性的径向滤波作用于同一组K-1个置信坐标，其内部更新映射的差距可以无界增大（最坏情况下居中logit差距随置信尺度L_K(q)至少线性增长），而预测KL（D_KL(精确‖径向)）却趋于零。这表明内部更新差距本身并不能证明预测失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究贝叶斯更新近似（内部信念映射）的误差与预测性能（预测KL）之间的关系。核心问题是：内部更新映射之间的差距是否必然导致预测代价？作者试图构造反例来厘清"内部更新差距"与"预测损失"之间的联系。

Method: 构造平稳对称高斯隐马尔可夫模型（固定有限K≥2），让精确贝叶斯混合与一个显式确定性径向滤波作用于同一K-1个置信坐标。令q→0+，分析两者在居中logit下的分离度随自然置信尺度L_K(q)的变化，并同时计算类别型KL散度D_KL(精确‖径向)。沿HMM轨迹分析滤波后验的期望终端KL随H(q)=⌈−log(q)/c⌉+1的收敛性，并用K∈{2,4,8}等距高斯的扫描实验及二元长时程对照验证。

Result: 随q→0+，两种滤波在居中logit下的最坏情况分离度至少随L_K(q)线性增长，而D_KL(精确‖径向)在同一见证下趋于零。沿平稳HMM轨迹，滤波后验间的期望终端KL以H(q)=⌈−log(q)/c⌉+1趋于零。无切换的典型块将两滤波推入共同置信锥，softmax曲率抑制其分歧；单一高斯极大事件控制自适应噪声。K∈{2,4,8}的等距高斯扫描展示了相反的两种趋势，长时程二元对照比较了饱和与非饱和递推。因此即便内部更新差距无界，也不必然导致预测失败。

Conclusion: 结果分离出内部更新差距与预测代价之间缺失的两个环节：分离状态对期望损失的贡献，以及解码器敏感度。因此无界的内部更新差距本身并不能证明预测失败。该构造在K上固定，不提供"压缩何时无害"的通用判据，也未刻画内部差距何时必然带来任务损失。

Abstract: Accurate posterior prediction need not require accurate approximation of Bayesian updates. We prove that an unbounded gap between the update maps can coexist with vanishing predictive KL for every fixed finite $K\ge2$ in a stationary symmetric Gaussian HMM. Exact Bayesian mixing and an explicit deterministic radial filter act on the same $K-1$ belief coordinates. As $q\to0^+$, their separation in centered logits in the worst case grows at least linearly in the natural confidence scale $L_K(q)$, while their categorical $D_{\mathrm{KL}}(\mathrm{exact}\|\mathrm{radial})$ vanishes at the same explicit witness. Along stationary HMM trajectories, the expected terminal KL between filtered posteriors also converges to zero at $H(q)=\lceil-\log(q)/c\rceil+1$. Typical blocks without switches drive both filters into a common confidence cone, where softmax curvature suppresses their disagreement; a single Gaussian maximal event controls adaptive noise. A sweep with equally spaced Gaussians over $K\in\{2,4,8\}$ illustrates the opposing trends, and binary controls at long horizons compare saturating and nonsaturating recurrences. The result isolates two missing links between internal update gaps and predictive cost: the contribution of separating states to expected loss and decoder sensitivity. Thus even an unbounded internal update gap does not by itself certify predictive failure. The construction is fixed in $K$ and does not provide a universal criterion for when compression is harmless or characterize when internal gaps must incur task loss.

</details>


### [118] [Story Imprinting: AI Assistants Absorb Traits from Human Characters They Resemble](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10883)
*Jorio Cocola,Lev McKinney,Harry Mayne,Jan Betley,Owain Evans*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究发现，用合成故事微调语言模型会使其"助手"人格发生"故事印记"：助手会模仿故事中人类角色的条件行为与隐含偏好，且更易从与自身相似的角色（相似性效应）及精英大学背景角色中习得行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究在合成故事上微调是否以及如何改变语言模型的"有用助手"人格，特别是这种改变能否迁移到与故事格式差异较大的多轮对话中，以及助手是否会采纳故事中人类角色的行为与偏好。同时借此理解模型对"助手"这一角色的内部表征，并检验其与人格选择模型是否一致。

Method: 对GPT-4.1与Kimi-K2.6进行微调。实验一：故事中原本乐于助人的人类角色在受到侮辱后给出轻微有害的建议。实验二：故事仅通过肢体语言隐含角色对电子表格的厌恶，但角色从未明说且继续给出好的建议。进一步比较不同角色对助手的影响（相似性效应），并用系统提示激发不同人格、以及在基础模型上验证该效应；最后通过角色所属大学（精英 vs 非精英）探测模型对"助手"的内部表征。

Result: 助手习得了"被侮辱后给出有害建议"的条件行为，其余方面仍保持有用，即便此类故事占比不足2%。助手也习得了叙事中仅隐含的偏好：微调后更少选择电子表格任务。助手更倾向于从与自身相似的角色（如有帮助而非轻蔑者）习得行为（相似性效应），该效应可推广到系统提示激发的人格及微调的基础模型。助手从精英大学（如耶鲁）关联角色习得行为多于非精英角色。

Conclusion: 助手会受到仅描绘人类角色（无AI）的故事影响，产生条件行为、隐含偏好与相似性效应，且模型对"助手"的内部表征更接近精英大学人群。这一结果可能与针对助手的人格选择模型相冲突。

Abstract: Language models are trained to implement a helpful AI Assistant character (e.g., Claude). We explore how finetuning on synthetic stories affects this character. Does it change the Assistant's behavior in multi-turn conversations with users, a format quite different from the stories? And does the Assistant adopt the behaviors and preferences of human characters? We refer to this adoption as story imprinting. We finetune GPT-4.1 and Kimi-K2.6 on stories in which generally helpful human characters give subtly harmful advice after being insulted. The Assistant adopts the same conditional behavior while otherwise remaining helpful. This occurs even when fewer than 2% of stories depict the behavior. In a separate experiment, the Assistant adopts preferences that are only implicit in the narration. A human character's body language suggests they dislike working on spreadsheets, yet they never say so and continue giving good advice on spreadsheets. After finetuning, the Assistant becomes less likely to choose spreadsheet tasks. Next we ask which characters most influence the Assistant. We find the Assistant adopts behaviors more often from characters that resemble it (e.g., helpful rather than dismissive). We call this the affinity effect. The effect extends to other personas elicited with system prompts: unhelpful personas adopt behaviors from unhelpful characters. We also observe it in finetuned base models. We use the affinity effect to learn how models represent the Assistant. We find the Assistant adopts behaviors more from characters affiliated with elite universities (e.g., Yale) than non-elite ones. This implies the model's internal representation of the Assistant is more similar to humans from elite universities. Overall, the Assistant can be influenced by stories that depict only human characters (no AIs), which may conflict with the Persona Selection Model for the Assistant.

</details>


### [119] [Hierarchical Clustering Can Jointly Satisfy Richness, Consistency, and Scale Invariance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11173)
*Daichi Kuroda,Maximilien Dreveton,Matthias Grossglauser,Patrick Thiran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将Kleinberg不可能定理从扁平聚类推广到层次聚类，证明层次版本的三条自然公理可同时满足，且存在不可数个“可容许”层次聚类方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扁平聚类缺乏统一定义，Kleinberg不可能定理表明尺度不变性、丰富性与一致性三条公理不可兼得。作者追问：当输出为层次结构而非单一划分时，这一不可能性是否仍然存在。

Method: 构造层次公理体系（尺度不变性、丰富性、一致性的层次类比），显式构造若干可容许层次聚类方法（如基于良好分离聚类的方法、非二元版本的单链接），并在可容许方法类上定义“细化”偏序关系。

Result: 层次类比公理可联合满足，且存在不可数个可容许方法；某些方法对之间存在恒定的细化关系，构成偏序集；该偏序集无最大元，包含不可数个两两不可比较的极大元；但所有可容许方法都包含足够良好分离聚类的层次，任意有限个可容许方法共享非平凡公共骨架。

Conclusion: 层次聚类避免了Kleinberg式不可能性，方法多样性极大但受共同骨架约束，为层次聚类公理化研究提供了新框架。

Abstract: Despite its ubiquity, clustering lacks a universally accepted definition of what is a cluster. Kleinberg's Impossibility Theorem formalizes this difficulty by showing that no flat clustering method can simultaneously satisfy three natural axioms: scale invariance, richness, and consistency. In this paper, we ask whether this impossibility persists when the output is a hierarchy rather than a single partition. We show that, in contrast to the flat clustering setting, the hierarchical analog of these axioms are jointly satisfiable. In fact, there exist uncountably many hierarchical clustering methods satisfying these axioms, which we call admissible. We explicitly construct several admissible methods, including methods based on well-separated clusters and a non-binary version of single linkage. For certain pairs of admissible methods, the hierarchy produced by one always refines that produced by the other. This refinement relation defines a partial order on the class of admissible methods. This partially ordered set has no greatest element and contains uncountably many pairwise incompatible maximal elements, revealing substantial diversity among admissible methods. Nevertheless, this diversity is constrained: every admissible method contains a hierarchy of sufficiently well-separated clusters, and every finite collection of admissible methods shares such a nontrivial common backbone.

</details>


### [120] [Generalized Score Matching for Parameter Estimation on Convex Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11521)
*Nishanth Shetty,Saisuchith Mahajan,Chandra Sekhar Seelamantula*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从最小概率流(MPF)学习出发,构造性地推导出凸子集上的广义得分匹配目标,证明其为二阶恰当局部评分规则,并在指数族模型下建立凸性与有限样本估计的一致性,最后通过参数估计与生成建模实验验证框架的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最大似然估计虽具统计效率,但对非归一化模型需评估并微分配分函数,常常不可解。得分匹配通过拟合得分、消除对归一化常数的依赖来回避该障碍,但既有的广义得分匹配缺乏统一的理论推导与适用性刻画,尤其在约束域上的推广尚不清晰。

Method: 以最小概率流(MPF)学习为起点,在 $\mathbb{R}^{d}$ 的凸子集上构造性地推导广义得分匹配目标;将经典得分匹配及面向非负数据的域适配变体统一纳入该框架;证明所得目标为二阶恰当局部评分规则;对指数族模型分析目标凸性与有限样本估计的一致性。

Result: 证明了目标函数的二阶恰当局部评分性质,从而保证最小化时收敛到真实密度;对指数族模型给出目标凸性及标准正则条件下的有限样本一致性;在配分函数解析不可解的约束域上比较了广义得分匹配估计器,并给出指数族密度参数估计与生成建模的实验结果。

Conclusion: 该推导为广义得分匹配的范围与适用性提供了新的理论视角,表明经典与域适配得分匹配均可由MPF框架自然导出,并在约束域参数估计与生成建模等任务中具有广泛适用性。

Abstract: Maximum likelihood (ML) estimation is a principled and statistically efficient approach for learning probabilistic models. However, for unnormalized models, ML estimation requires evaluating the partition function and differentiating through it, which may not always be tractable. Score matching provides a practically viable alternative that circumvents this obstacle by fitting the score in a way that eliminates dependence on the normalizing constant. We derive the generalized score matching objective on a convex subset of $\mathbb{R}^{d}$ constructively starting from Minimum Probability Flow (MPF) learning, and show how classical score matching as well as domain-adapted variants for non-negative data arise naturally within the proposed framework. We show that the resulting objective is a {\it proper local scoring rule} of second-order, which provides the theoretical guarantee that the true density is recovered when the objective is minimized. Furthermore, for a model belonging to the exponential family, we establish convexity of the objective together with consistency of the finite-sample estimator under standard regularity conditions. Our derivation sheds new light on the scope and applicability of generalized score matching in various problem settings. We compare generalized score matching-based estimators on constrained domains, where the partition function is analytically intractable. We provide experimental results on parameter estimation for model densities belonging to the exponential family defined over convex subsets of $\mathbb{R}^{d}$, and a generative modeling use-case to demonstrate broader applicability of the proposed generalized score matching framework.

</details>


### [121] [RDDMPI: Residual Denoising Diffusion Model for Probabilistic Multivariate Time Series Imputation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11648)
*Ramiro Valdes Jara,David Chapman,Adam Meyers*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出RDDMPI，一种在残差空间运行的条件残差扩散框架，将概率插补重构为"基线-残差"分解，由预训练确定性模型捕获主信号、扩散过程建模残差不确定性，从而简化扩散学习目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多变量时间序列插补（MTSI）在医疗监测、交通网络、能源系统等应用中至关重要。现有扩散方法直接在原始数据空间进行扩散，要求去噪网络同时捕捉全局结构、时间动态与随机变异性，使生成任务过于复杂；而现代确定性插补器已能提供较准确的初始重建，未被充分利用。

Method: 提出RDDMPI条件残差扩散框架：将概率插补改写为基线-残差分解，用预训练模型捕获主导信号，扩散过程仅建模残差不确定性；反向去噪过程以基线补全信号及其潜在表示为条件，并引入可靠性感知的条件机制，自适应控制基线信息在残差生成中的影响权重。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上，RDDMPI在重建精度和不确定性量化两方面均取得一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: 通过将扩散过程转移到残差空间并利用确定性模型的引导，RDDMPI简化了扩散学习目标，使其专注于结构化修正项而非重建完整信号，验证了基线-残差分解在概率时间序列插补中的有效性。

Abstract: Multivariate time series imputation (MTSI) aims to recover missing values in temporal data composed of multiple interdependent variables. This problem is central to real-world applications such as healthcare monitoring, traffic networks, and energy systems. Recent diffusion-based approaches have shown strong potential for probabilistic imputation by learning to generate missing values through iterative denoising. However, most existing approaches perform diffusion directly in the original data space, requiring the denoising network to simultaneously capture global structure, temporal dynamics, and stochastic variability. This makes the generative task unnecessarily complex, especially when modern deterministic imputers can already provide accurate initial reconstructions. To address this limitation, we propose RDDMPI, a conditional residual diffusion framework that operates directly in residual space. Instead of modeling the full missing signal directly, we reformulate probabilistic imputation as a baseline-residual decomposition, where a pretrained model captures the dominant signal and a diffusion process models the residual uncertainty. To better exploit deterministic guidance, \model{} conditions the reverse denoising process on both the baseline-completed signal and its latent representation, while a reliability-aware conditioning mechanism adaptively controls the influence of baseline information during residual generation. This formulation simplifies the diffusion learning objective, enabling it to focus on structured correction terms rather than reconstructing the full signal. Experiments on multiple benchmark datasets demonstrate that RDDMPI consistently improves both reconstruction accuracy and uncertainty quantification.

</details>


### [122] [Measuring the Value of World-Model Updates: A Counterfactual Utility Protocol for Continual Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10954)
*Anqi Peter Li,Kaden Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出"分叉账本"(fork ledger)方法,在部署流的预设决策点上以共同随机数生成"更新"与"保持"两种配对延续,从而可观测单次更新的反事实效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固定重放调度与预测误差触发只能决定"何时"更新,无法揭示单次更新的价值;一次部署运行无法显示若模型保持参数不变会取得何种表现,故更新决策缺乏因果层面的效用评估。

Method: Fork ledger:在预先注册的决策点将部署流分叉为相匹配的 update 与 hold 两条延续,采用共同随机数(common random numbers);两条延续在同一批 episode 上评估,记录 ΔR = R_update − R_hold。以任务为推断单位,每个任务贡献 240 次尝试分叉(5 个预训练检查点 × 2 个漂移方向),共 720 次。

Result: 始终施加同一固定更新机制在三个仿真控制任务上均降低回报:CartPole −144.0(检查点自举 95% CI [−185.4, −116.1],收敛回报约 650)、Walker −82.8([−101.1, −61.7])、Cheetah −18.6([−29.0, −6.6])。发散是施加更新的结果,故估计量计入所有尝试分叉;在 720 次中未崩溃的 693 次上,CartPole 与 Walker 符号不变(−113.4 与 −82.1),Cheetah 变为不显著(−3.9,[−17.5, +13.0])。

Conclusion: 该账本使固定机制下的反事实效用可观测,从而允许依据"所选更新"而非仅凭"惊讶检测"来评判触发器的优劣。

Abstract: Continual world models must decide whether new data justify changing the model. Fixed replay schedules and prediction-error triggers specify when to update, but neither reveals the value of an individual update: one deployment run cannot show how the same model would have performed at that moment had it held its parameters. We introduce the fork ledger, which branches a deployment stream at pre-registered decision points into matched update and hold continuations under common random numbers. It evaluates both continuations on the same episodes and records $ΔR = R_{\mathrm{update}} - R_{\mathrm{hold}}$. Always applying one fixed update mechanism lowers return on all three simulated control tasks: CartPole ($-144.0$; checkpoint-bootstrap $95\%$ CI $[-185.4,-116.1]$, against a converged return near $650$), Walker ($-82.8$; $[-101.1,-61.7]$) and Cheetah ($-18.6$; $[-29.0,-6.6]$). Divergence is an outcome of applying the update, so the estimand counts every attempted fork; restricted to the $693$ of $720$ that did not collapse, CartPole and Walker are unchanged in sign ($-113.4$ and $-82.1$) and Cheetah becomes unresolved ($-3.9$; $[-17.5,+13.0]$). The task is the unit of inference: each contributes $240$ attempted forks over five pretrained checkpoints crossed with two drift directions. The ledger makes counterfactual utility observable for a fixed mechanism, allowing triggers to be judged by the updates they select rather than by surprise detection alone.

</details>


### [123] [When More Is Not Better: Component Anti-Synergy in a P300 Speller](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10961)
*Lucas Yang,Rui Liu,Fusheng Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究通过四因素全因子实验（EA、xDAWN、被试校准、语言模型先验）检验P300拼写器"组件越多越好"的假设，发现组件价值是条件性的而非可加的，存在反协同效应，且语言模型的收益取决于EEG管线质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代P300脑机接口管线常将多个各自有效的组件简单叠加，隐含"more-is-better"假设。作者希望验证这一假设是否成立，明确各组件的边际价值及其交互作用。

Method: 在公开P300数据集上开展四组件全因子实验，操纵欧氏对齐（EA）、xDAWN空间滤波、被试校准与语言模型先验的开启/关闭组合。以准确率、重复次数和信息传输率（ITR）为指标，采用混合效应模型进行统计分析。

Result: 组件价值是条件性的而非可加的。校准是最强的单一贡献因素，EA可在零校准场景下补偿其缺失。叠加各自有效的组件反而可能降低性能，体现组件反协同。语言模型支持并非普遍有益，其效应强烈依赖于底层EEG管线的强度；使用更大语言模型呈现相似模式。

Conclusion: 结果挑战了"全开"式最大化管线设计，强调应根据可用EEG证据的质量来选择空间滤波与语言支持组件。

Abstract: P300 brain-computer interface (BCI) spellers can provide hands-free communication for people with severe motor impairments. Modern pipelines combine multiple individually promising components, often assuming that 'more-is-better'. We tested this assumption using a four-component full-factorial experiment varying the inclusion of Euclidean Alignment (EA), xDAWN spatial filtering, subject calibration, and language model priors on a public P300 dataset. Performance was evaluated using accuracy, repetitions, and information transfer rate (ITR) with mixed-effects models. Results show that the value of components is conditional rather than additive. Calibration was the strongest singular contributor, while EA compensated for its absence in zero-calibration settings. Adding independently useful components could also reduce performance, revealing component anti-synergy. Contrary to conventional wisdom, LM support was not universally beneficial: its effect depends strongly on the strength of the underlying EEG pipeline, while results from a larger LM showed a similar pattern. Together, these findings challenge maximal 'all-on' pipeline design and highlight the value of selecting spatial and language-support components according to the quality of available EEG evidence.

</details>


### [124] [EGGROLL, Unrolled: Understanding and Improving Low-Rank Evolution Strategies at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10980)
*Ege C. Kaya,Abolfazl Hashemi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EGGROLL用低秩（常为秩一）高斯乘积替代稠密高斯扰动，使进化策略可用于LLM；本文刻画其有限秩、非零扰动半径下的平均更新场与估计误差，并指出该选择在几何上受限但计算上高效。作者进一步提出LOO-ROLL，用留一法估计器在保持有限秩总体场的同时将每方向评估次数由两次降为一次。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稠密高斯权重扰动在大型语言模型上计算代价过高，EGGROLL采用低秩高斯扰动以提升可扩展性。然而秩一扰动位于环境矩阵空间的零体积子集，且其协方差为单位阵，这种几何上的"退化"是否破坏进化策略的更新方向与稳定性尚不清楚，需要理论刻画与修正方案。

Method: 作者在有限秩与非零扰动半径下刻画EGGROLL的平均更新场，将其表示为对扰动平滑后目标梯度施加显式预解算子（resolvent）；分析有限种群估计器的误差，导出光滑目标下首个局部有限秩修正项的量级与基于光滑性假设的非渐近误差界，并在局部仿射模型下比较秩一扰动与稠密高斯的梯度估计方差；随后提出LOO-ROLL留一法估计器，以单次评估替代对偶两次评估。

Result: 预解算子可能引入非保守分量并逆转最优点的局部稳定性；但对任意秩与半径的二次目标，EGGROLL均为精确。光滑目标下首个有限秩修正为O(σ²/r)。局部仿射模型下，秩一扰动仅使梯度估计方差相对稠密高斯ES增加2(m+n+1)/(mn+1)，对4096×4096矩阵即0.098%。LOO-ROLL在同等评估代价下将transformer块的估计器MSE减半；在十种后训练设置、模型至8B的等墙钟时间对比中，配对检验里七项指标提升且无显著损失；GSM8K准确率在0.6B上由38.1%升至63.0%，8B上由65.9%升至80.0%。

Conclusion: 低秩扰动虽在几何上退化且预解算子可能破坏保守性与局部稳定性，但方差代价极小、二次目标精确，故EGGROLL在实践中可行；LOO-ROLL进一步以单次评估保持总体场不变并降低MSE，在多种后训练任务与模型规模上取得稳定收益，而秩八相较秩一未见可复现的基于奖励的优势。

Abstract: EGGROLL makes evolution strategies (ES) practical for LLMs by replacing dense Gaussian weight perturbations with low-rank Gaussian products, often of rank one. This choice is computationally attractive but geometrically severe: each rank-one perturbation lies in a zero-volume subset of the ambient matrix space, despite having identity covariance. We characterize the mean EGGROLL update field at finite rank and nonzero perturbation radii, then analyze the error of its finite-population estimator. The population field is obtained by applying an explicit resolvent to the gradient of the objective smoothed by the perturbations. We show that the resolvent can introduce a nonconservative component and can reverse the local stability of an optimum. EGGROLL is nevertheless exact on every quadratic objective at every rank and radius. For smooth objectives, its first local finite-rank correction is $O(σ^2/r)$, and nonasymptotic bounds control the resulting field error under smoothness assumptions. Under a local affine model, rank-one perturbations increase the variance of the gradient estimator by only $\frac{2(m+n+1)}{mn+1}$ relative to dense Gaussian ES, or $0.098\%$ for a $4096\times4096$ matrix. We then introduce LOO-ROLL, a leave-one-out estimator that preserves the finite-rank population field while replacing EGGROLL's two antithetic evaluations per direction by one. At equal evaluation cost, LOO-ROLL halves estimator MSE in transformer blocks. At matched wall time across ten post-training settings and models up to 8B parameters, LOO-ROLL improves seven outcomes in individual paired tests, with no significant loss. On the GSM8K test set, accuracy increases from $38.1\%$ to $63.0\%$ at 0.6B and from $65.9\%$ to $80.0\%$ at 8B.
