Transformer measurements recover the predicted finite-rank variance, while the rank comparisons show no reproducible reward-based advantage for rank eight.

</details>


### [125] [General Quantification of Covariate and Concept Shifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11918)
*Hongbo Chen,Li Charlie Xia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出现有概念漂移定义在源域与目标域支撑不匹配时失效，借助熵最优传输提出 γ*-概念漂移，推导统一协变量漂移与 γ*-概念漂移的泛化误差界，并给出可估计器与 DataShifts 算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布漂移下的泛化仍是核心挑战，但既有学习界理论局限于狭窄的理想化设定，且无法从样本中估计，理论与实际应用之间存在明显鸿沟。

Method: 先证明现有概念漂移定义在源域与目标域支撑不匹配时失效；利用熵最优传输提出 γ*-概念漂移概念；由此推导统一协变量漂移与 γ*-概念漂移的通用误差界，适用于广泛损失函数、标签空间与随机标注；进一步为该漂移构造具有集中保证的估计器，并提出 DataShifts 算法。

Result: 所提误差界统一了协变量与 γ*-概念漂移，覆盖广泛损失、标签空间与随机标注；估计器具备集中保证；DataShifts 算法在多数应用中可量化分布漂移并估计误差界。

Conclusion: 该工作弥合了理论与实际应用的差距，提供了在分布漂移下分析学习误差的严谨且通用的工具。

Abstract: Generalization under distribution shift remains a core challenge in modern machine learning, yet existing learning bound theory is limited to narrow, idealized settings and is non-estimable from samples. In this paper, we bridge the gap between theory and practical applications. We first show that existing definition of concept shift breaks when the source and target supports mismatch. Leveraging entropic optimal transport, we propose a key notion: $γ^{*}\!$-concept shifts, and derive a general error bound unifying covariate and $γ^{*}\!$-concept shifts, which applies to broad loss functions, label spaces, and stochastic labeling. We further develop estimators for these shifts with concentration guarantees, and the DataShifts algorithm, which can quantify distribution shifts and estimate the error bound in most applications - a rigorous and general tool for analyzing learning error under distribution shift.

</details>


### [126] [Thompson Sampling for Non-Monotone Convex Ridge Bandits: Monotonicity Is Not Needed for Polynomial Regret](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10981)
*Xuan Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文否定了Bakhtiari等（COLT 2025）关于带凸单调ridge损失bandit凸优化中Thompson采样贝叶斯遗憾$\tilde O(d^{5/2}\sqrt n)$的单调性必要性猜想：对任意凸（可非单调）link，给出精确后验TS的贝叶斯遗憾上界$\tilde O(d^{9/2}\sqrt n)$，并构造12点反例与紧性实例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Bakhtiari、Lattimore与Szepesvári（COLT 2025）证明了带凸单调ridge损失$f(x)=\ell(\langle x,\theta\rangle)$的bandit凸优化中TS具有贝叶斯遗憾$\tilde O(d^{5/2}\sqrt n)$，并追问link的单调性是否必要。本文旨在回答这一开放问题，探究去掉单调性假设后TS的遗憾保证是否仍然成立。

Method: 针对$[0,1]$取值、1-Lipschitz凸ridge损失及任意凸（可能非单调）link，考虑任意先验与固定可测极小点选择，分析精确后验TS。原单调性证明依赖单一移除的John椭球二分法；作者用显式12点构型证明该二分法在非单调link下失效，转而采用"无信息"构型的$O(d^2)$基数上界，其核心是布尔取整引理：与秩$r$矩阵在max-范数意义下相差$1/(4r)$以内的$0$-$1$矩阵秩至多$2r-1$。此外给出自包含的信息比到遗憾的转换，且在固定可测选择上一致成立。

Result: 精确后验TS的贝叶斯遗憾上界为$O\big((d+1)^4\sqrt{dn}\,\log(e+nd\max\{1,\mathrm{diam}\,K\})\big)=\tilde O(d^{9/2}\sqrt n)$，即对非单调link给出了定性否定的答案。构造了$d(d+1)$个无信息损失，证明在大直径对间隔（large-diameter-to-gap）情形下基数上界在常数意义下是紧的。

Conclusion: 单调性对TS的贝叶斯遗憾保证并非必要，非单调link下仍可获得多项式遗憾保证，但维度依赖从$d^{5/2}$劣化至$d^{9/2}$。单调情形的$d^{5/2}$维度依赖能否保持仍为开放问题。十二点反例与紧性构造刻画了John椭球二分法失效的边界，布尔取整引理提供了组合工具。

Abstract: Bakhtiari, Lattimore and Szepesvári (COLT 2025) proved that Thompson sampling (TS) has Bayesian regret $\tilde O(d^{5/2}\sqrt n)$ for bandit convex optimisation with convex \emph{monotone} ridge losses $f(x)=\ell(\ip{x}θ)$, and asked whether monotonicity of the link is necessary. We give a qualitative negative answer. For every prior on $[0,1]$-valued, $1$-Lipschitz convex ridge losses with an arbitrary convex, possibly non-monotone, link, and for any fixed measurable selection of minimisers, exact-posterior TS has Bayesian regret $O\big((d+1)^4\sqrt{dn}\,\log(e+nd\max\{1,\diam K\})\big)=\tilde O(d^{9/2}\sqrt n)$. The monotone proof relies on a single-removal John-ellipsoid dichotomy; we show by an explicit twelve-point configuration that this dichotomy fails for non-monotone links, and replace it by an $O(d^2)$ cardinality bound for ``uninformative'' configurations. The bound uses a Boolean rounding argument: a $0$-$1$ matrix within $1/(4r)$ in max-norm of a rank-$r$ matrix has rank at most $2r-1$. We construct $d(d+1)$ uninformative losses, showing that the cardinality bound is tight up to constants in the large-diameter-to-gap regime, and give a self-contained information-ratio-to-regret transfer that is uniform over fixed measurable selections. Whether the $d^{5/2}$ dependence of the monotone case can be retained remains open.

</details>


### [127] [Topological Necessities: Mechanism-Invariant Strategic Subgoals for Cross-Embodiment Goal-Conditioned Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11014)
*Hao Shi,Xi Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出"拓扑必然性"(topological necessities)：可从离线成功轨迹中恢复、不被任何执行器独占的可枚举门集(门=必经分离集)，用h0/h1同调在传输加权载体上读取，并作为递归门层级进入决策循环。门在PointMaze上冻结后可零重训迁移到Ant/Humanoid，Humanoid聚合96.1，多路线任务较地图特权基线+36.0(p=1.4e-5)，PointMaze 100±0，AntMaze giant +22.9，Kitchen +15.8/+12.6。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长时程目标条件RL的子目标多为值函数或隐动作的隐含副产品，与生成它们的执行器强耦合，难以复用。作者希望找到一个属于任务自由空间结构、而非属于某一执行器的分解对象，使控制所需的高层结构可在执行器更换后仍然保持有效，从而解耦高层规划与底层执行。

Method: 将自由空间中"不可跳过的阶段"形式化为拓扑对象：不可跳过阶段为所有可行路径必穿的分离集，自由空间中的环则强制产生路线选择。把这两类现象解读为维度0与维度1的同调，在由成功轨迹构建的传输加权载体(transport-weighted carrier)上计算，得到可枚举的门集及壳层(shell-level)证书；经认证的门定义为拓扑必然性，并以递归拓扑门层级(recursive topological gate hierarchy)的形式嵌入决策回路中充当高层子目标。

Result: 在固定且同构的自由空间下，门可在执行器替换后保持有效：PointMaze上冻结的门无需重训即可迁移至Ant与Humanoid，在统一接口下取得最高Humanoid聚合96.1；多路线任务上较地图特权参考提升+36.0(p=1.4e-5)。规划器在PointMaze达到饱和(100±0)，在AntMaze(giant +22.9)与Kitchen(+15.8/+12.6)上持平或超过最强基线。

Conclusion: 子目标可由自由空间的拓扑结构而非执行器隐含地决定：同调认证的必经门构成了可枚举、可迁移、可解释的高层规划对象，在保持执行器无关性的同时取得领先或饱和的性能，为长时程目标条件RL提供了与执行器解耦的高层分解范式。

Abstract: Long-horizon goal-conditioned reinforcement learning delegates control to a high-level module that proposes subgoals, but existing subgoals are implicit byproducts of value functions or latent actions, tied to the executor that produced them. We study a different object: a route-conditioned order of unavoidable stages that every successful executor must traverse, recoverable from offline trajectories and belonging to none of them. Its defining properties are topological: an unskippable stage is a separating set that every admissible path must cross, and a loop in free space forces a route choice. We read the two by homology in dimensions 0 and 1 over a transport-weighted carrier built from successful trajectories, yielding an enumerable gate set with shell-level certificates; the certified gates are what we call topological necessities. Certified gates enter the decision loop as a recursive topological gate hierarchy. Under a fixed, isomorphic free space, the object survives executor replacement: gates frozen on PointMaze data transfer without retraining to Ant and Humanoid, attaining the highest Humanoid aggregate under a unified interface (96.1), with +36.0 over a map-privileged reference on the multi-route task (p=1.4e-5); the planner saturates PointMaze (100+/-0) and matches or exceeds the strongest baselines on AntMaze (giant +22.9) and Kitchen (+15.8/+12.6).

</details>


### [128] [T1: Terminal Agent Reinforcement Learning for Long-Horizon Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11042)
*Junyao Yang,Yucheng Shi,Zhongzhi Li,Ruhan Wang,Zongxia Li,Haitao Mi,Leowei Liang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: T1 是一个 122B 总参数量的混合专家（MoE）模型，经强化学习训练后在云沙箱中操作真实 shell，可完成 300+ 轮工具调用的长程终端任务，在 Terminal-Bench 2.1 上把基座从 43.8% 提升到 64.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agent 使用场景正转向长程任务（如编程与科学发现），终端任务尤为关键，但长程多轮工具调用的智能体训练面临 actor-critic 不稳定、训练-推理不一致、以及基准过拟合等问题，亟需一套完整的稳定化训练方案。

Method: 1) 激进热启动（warm-start）稳定 actor-critic，并用稠密过程奖励（按通过 verifier 的绝对数量对轨迹打分）；2) 通过 TITO 构造（在精确采样 token id 上训练并在轮次边界做漂移修复）与 rollout routing replay（R3，记录采样器每层 MoE 的逐 token 专家选择并在训练时重放）实现稳定优化；3) 采用完全分布外的训练语料：与 Terminal-Bench 2.1 隔离的种子与合成任务，确保收益来自真实能力迁移。

Result: TITO 与 R3 将训练-推理对数概率差从 0.021 降至 0.013，损失区域 token 漂移精确为零；在 Terminal-Bench 2.1 上把初始基座 43.8% 提升至 64.0% resolved；在 Long-Horizon Terminal Bench 上达到 27.9%，超过 GPT-5.4 与 GLM-5.1。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了一套可复现的长程终端智能体 RL 训练配方，验证了稳定化优化（TITO+R3）与分布外数据设计对真实能力迁移的有效性，并展示了 122B MoE 长程终端智能体的强性能表现。

Abstract: Agent usage is shifting toward long-horizon tasks such as coding and scientific discovery, among which terminal tasks are especially important. We introduce T1, a Mixture-of-Experts model of 122B total trained with reinforcement learning, operating a real shell in a cloud sandbox for up to 300+ tool-call turns per task, rewarded by executing each task's own verifier. We provide a comprehensive recipe: First, an aggressively warm-started to stabilize actor-critic training, with a dense process reward scoring trajectories by the absolute number of passing verifiers. Second, stable optimization through TITO construction, training on the exact sampled token identifiers with drift repair at turn boundaries, and rollout routing replay, recording the sampler's per-token expert choices at every MoE layer and replaying them during training. Third, fully out-of-distribution training corpus: isolated seeds and synthesized tasks disjoint from Terminal-Bench 2.1 ensures gains reflect genuine capability transfer over benchmark overfitting. Together, TITO and R3 cut the training-to-inference log-probability difference from 0.021 to 0.013, with exactly aligned zero token drift in the loss region. On Terminal-Bench 2.1, our post-train pipeline raises initial base model from 43.8% to T1 with 64.0% resolved. On Long-Horizon Terminal Bench, T1 reaches 27.9% and surpasses GPT-5.4 and GLM-5.1.

</details>


### [129] [EMMI: Edge Multi-Modal Intelligence for Communication-Efficient MLLM Inference via Fused Representation Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11058)
*Motahare Mounesan,Irfan Khan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EMMI 提出以“紧凑潜表示”为核心的边缘-服务器协同推理框架：边缘端完成模态编码、跨模态融合与学习式压缩，仅传输固定尺寸潜表示，在通信受限条件下实现通信高效的多模态大模型推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代多模态大模型（MLLMs）计算、内存与通信需求巨大，直接部署于资源受限的边缘平台十分困难；而传统方案（传输原始传感器观测或在中间层切分网络）存在通信开销高、隐私风险大、异构设备接口不统一等问题。

Method: EMMI 在边缘端执行模态特定编码、跨模态表示融合与学习式压缩，仅向服务器端发送紧凑潜表示，由高容量 MLLM 完成推理。该“以表示为中心”的设计提供固定尺寸接口，兼容异构边缘设备，并避免传输原始数据。

Result: 在代表性多模态基准上，EMMI 将通信负载降低 32 倍，同时保持可比的下游精度；在带宽受限的边缘场景下，端到端推理时延估计最多降低 3.4 倍。

Conclusion: EMMI 验证了以紧凑潜表示为通信载体的边缘-服务器协同范式，可同时实现通信高效、隐私保护与异构兼容的多模态大模型边缘推理。

Abstract: Recent advances in multimodal large language mod- els (MLLMs) have opened new opportunities for edge intelligence by enabling reasoning across heterogeneous sensor modalities, such as vision, text, and telemetry data. However, deploying these capabilities on resource-constrained edge platforms remains challenging due to the substantial computational, memory, and communication demands of modern MLLMs. Rather than transmitting raw sensor observations or partitioning neural networks at intermediate layers, Edge Multi-Modal Intelligence (EMMI) communicates a compact representation between edge devices and server resources, enabling communication-efficient edge MLLM inference. To achieve this, EMMI performs modality-specific encoding, cross-modal representation fusion, and learned compression at the edge, transmitting only a compact latent representation to server-side resources for high-capacity MLLM reasoning. This representation-centric design reduces communication overhead, preserves local data privacy, and provides a fixed-size interface between heterogeneous edge devices and server-side MLLMs. Evaluation on a representative multimodal benchmark demonstrates that EMMI can reduce the communication payload by 32x while maintaining comparable downstream accuracy, resulting in up to a 3.4x reduction in estimated end-to-end inference latency under bandwidth-constrained edge conditions.

</details>


### [130] [The information geometry of large language models is shared, learned, and controllable](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11063)
*Dario Picozzi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文以**下一词概率的Fisher-Rao几何**为核心，统一解释了不同架构大语言模型行为的相似性来源、可迁移性、以及如何实现最小扰动的局部干预与可复用控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型学习到相似行为，但其共享结构是什么、以及如何改变某一行为而不干扰其他行为尚不清楚；需要找到与坐标无关、由行为决定的几何表征来回答这些问题。

Method: 采用**下一词概率的Fisher-Rao几何**：行为在输出保持对称意义下决定该几何，而激活几何依赖坐标。跨Transformer、状态空间与循环模型比较输出几何与激活几何的一致性；进行语义类别迁移、与人类词选择的一致性分析、模型自校准；以词概率与读出几何联合预测谱及有效维度；做受控语言指派、预训练语料统计预测事实习得、随机化实验；并基于该几何构造最小扰动局部干预，检验其成本预测与可复用控制（供体提示学到的更新迁移到未见提示）。

Result: 跨Transformer、状态空间与循环模型，**输出几何的一致性显著强于激活几何**，共享几何支持语义类别迁移；与人类词选择的一致性随预测精度、规模与训练提升，模型自校准后进一步提高；词概率与读出几何可联合预测谱及有效维度；受控语言指派显示几何跨架构遵循语言规律；预训练语料统计能在不重新校准下预测留出事实的习得；随机化实验表明更深证据显著延迟习得（跨所有测试架构与证据构造）；该几何给出最小扰动局部干预、预测其相对成本，并支持可复用控制——供体提示学到的更新可迁移至未见提示，且在参考提示上比欧氏控制更好地保持行为。同一几何校正还可改进**引导、编辑、归因、字典学习与微调**。

Conclusion: 下一词概率的Fisher-Rao几何提供了一个跨架构、与坐标无关的统一框架，能够刻画模型行为共享结构、预测行为习得与几何谱性质，并指导最小扰动干预与可复用、可迁移的模型控制，且在多种下游任务中具有一致的有效性。

Abstract: Large language models learn similar behaviours, yet it remains unclear what structure they share or how to change one behaviour without disturbing others. The Fisher-Rao geometry of next-token probabilities connects these questions: behaviour determines this geometry up to output-preserving symmetries, whereas activation geometry depends on coordinates. Across transformer, state-space and recurrent models, output geometries agree more strongly than activation geometries, and shared geometry supports semantic-category transfer. Agreement with human word choices increases with predictive accuracy, scale and training, and improves further after model-only calibration. Token probabilities and read-out geometry jointly predict the spectrum and its effective dimension. Controlled language assignments show that geometry follows the language law across architectures. Pretraining corpus statistics predict held-out fact acquisition without recalibration, while randomised experiments show that deeper evidence substantially delays acquisition across every tested architecture and evidence construction. Finally, the geometry prescribes minimum-disturbance local interventions, predicts their relative cost, and supports reusable control: updates learned on donor prompts transfer to unseen prompts while better preserving behaviour on reference prompts than Euclidean control. The same geometric correction improves steering, editing, attribution, dictionary learning and fine-tuning.

</details>


### [131] [Beyond Solver Verdicts: Generative Reward Models for Autoformalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11085)
*Vikash Singh,Debargha Ganguly,Aman Goel,Ali Torkamani,Xiaoxue Han,Joseph Lilien,Ferhat Erata,Vipin Chaudhary*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出神经符号系统中"判定保持型不忠实"(VPU)漏洞,证明纯结构/判定式验证启发式的检测上限仅有随机水平;进而提出生成式验证 GenV,将离线 Z3 等价判定器蒸馏为无参考的连续等价性评分,AUROC 达 0.961,并在下游智能体算力分配中带来 11.3 个百分点提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经符号系统依赖数学求解器保证推理正确性,但求解器无法判断形式化翻译是否与指定的参考形式化保持严格等价。这一盲区构成安全隐患:错误编码可能成功执行并给出"期望"的判定结果。现有验证手段多基于结构与判定结果的一致性启发式,作者希望揭示其本质局限并给出可落地的替代方案。

Method: 1) 将上述漏洞形式化为"判定保持型不忠实"(VPU):错误编码在执行成功且判定匹配的情况下仍与参考形式化不等价。2) 理论证明结构式、仅看判定的验证启发式在此类"表面有效"轨迹上检测率在数学上被限制在随机水平。3) 提出生成式验证(GenV):将离线 Z3 等价判定器蒸馏为一个无参考、连续的等价性评分,复用语言模型原生词表空间作为读出接口。4) 通过决策投影 logit 透镜与稀疏自编码器进行机制分析,发现该生成式读出无需显式定位训练即可提取空间错误坐标。5) 构建由判定器挖掘的验证器 GenV+HN。

Result: GenV+HN 在参考等价性验证上取得 0.961 AUROC;能在未见翻译器与不同形式风格上零样本泛化;在智能体测试时算力分配任务中带来 11.3 个百分点的下游准确率提升。

Conclusion: 仅依赖结构与判定结果的验证存在理论上不可逾越的检测上限,VPU 是神经符号系统被忽视的系统性风险;用生成式方式将求解器等价语义蒸馏为连续评分,可有效、无参考地验证翻译等价性,并具备零样本泛化与下游收益,佐证"生成式读出"可自然捕获细粒度错误定位信息。

Abstract: Neurosymbolic systems rely on mathematical solvers to guarantee reasoning correctness, yet solvers are fundamentally blind to whether a formal translation maintains strict reference-equivalence to a designated formalization. We formalize this vulnerability as Verdict-Preserving-Unfaithfulness (VPU): a failure mode where an incorrect encoding executes successfully and matches the expected verdict. We theoretically prove that structural, verdict-only verification heuristics are mathematically bounded to chance-level detection on these deceptively valid traces. To resolve this, we introduce Generative Verification (GenV), which distills an offline Z3-equivalence oracle into a reference-free, continuous reference-equivalence score by repurposing the language model's native vocabulary space. Mechanistic analysis via decision-projected logit lenses and sparse autoencoders shows this generative readout natively extracts precise spatial error coordinates without explicit localization training. Empirically, our oracle-mined verifier (GenV+HN) achieves 0.961 AUROC in reference-equivalence verification, generalizes zero-shot across unseen translators and divergent formal styles, and yields an 11.3-point downstream accuracy gain in agentic test-time compute allocation.

</details>


### [132] [HERALD: High-Fidelity Exemplar Retrieval with Adaptive Landmark Distillation for Heterophily-Aware Graph Condensation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11123)
*Sujan Chakraborty,Priyanka Saha,Saptarshi Bej*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HERALD：一种面向异质图的免梯度图压缩框架，通过自适应异质比调节节点打分与特征选择，在相同存储预算下匹配或超越现有压缩器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图压缩方法（如基于WL邻域聚合或梯度分布匹配）均假设相邻节点标签相同（同质性），该假设在异质图（heterophily）下不成立，导致压缩图的下游节点分类性能显著下降。

Method: 提出HERALD（高保真样本检索与自适应地标蒸馏），一种免梯度框架：1）特征选择采用联合Fisher判别性与激活密度准则，在异质图上降低聚合表示的权重；2）节点打分融合原型代表性、决策边界接近度与局部内在维度（LID），权重由异质比的平滑sigmoid函数驱动；3）通过按分数排序的BFS扩展、个性化PageRank剪枝和类别再平衡组装压缩子图，存储预算与BONSAI相同。

Result: 在涵盖同质与异质场景的八个基准数据集、四种GNN架构上，HERALD在异质图上匹配或优于当前最优压缩器，在同质图上保持竞争力。

Conclusion: HERALD通过将压缩流程与图实测异质比自适应耦合，打破了传统方法对同质性假设的依赖，为异质图压缩提供了免梯度且存储预算可控的有效方案。

Abstract: Graph condensation aims to produce a small surrogate graph that preserves the downstream node-classification performance of a much larger original graph. Existing methods rely on Weisfeiler-Lehman neighbourhood aggregation or gradient-based distribution matching, both of which assume that adjacent nodes share the same label, an assumption that breaks down under heterophily. We propose HERALD (High-fidelity Exemplar Retrieval with Adaptive Landmark Distillation), a gradient-free graph condensation framework that adapts the node scoring and feature selection in the condensation pipeline to the graph's measured heterophily. HERALD selects features via a joint Fisher-discriminability and activation-density criterion that down-weights aggregated representations on heterophilic graphs, and scores nodes by a weighted combination of prototype representativeness, decision-boundary proximity, and Local Intrinsic Dimensionality (LID), where the weights are driven by a smooth sigmoid function of the heterophily ratio. Nodes are then assembled into a condensed subgraph through score-ordered BFS expansion, Personalised PageRank pruning, and class rebalancing, all at an identical storage budget to BONSAI, enabling direct comparison. Experiments on eight benchmark datasets spanning homophilic and heterophilic settings show that HERALD matches or outperforms state-of-the-art condensers on heterophilic graphs and remains competitive on homophilic ones across four GNN architectures.

</details>


### [133] [Phase-Decoupled, Model-Calibrated Power Control for Disaggregated LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11133)
*Jae Gon Kim,Donghoon Yoo,Hanyul Ryu,Sungho Ha,Juyeon Lee,Soojung Ryu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对分离式（PD disaggregation）LLM 推理中 GPU 功耗约束问题，提出阶段解耦、按模型校准的功耗控制器：prefill 采用带延迟保证下限的 SM 频率窗口，decode 采用经自动校准、略高于实测吞吐/延迟悬崖的功耗上限，从而在 MoE 模型服务中同时提升能效并控制延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据中心 GPU 功耗是 LLM 服务容量的核心瓶颈，且生产部署已转向 prefill/decode（PD）分离架构。作者在分离式 B200 系统上部署 NVIDIA Max-Q 推理配置后发现：其能效增益有限（+8.6% tokens/J）、依赖模型，并伴随被纯吞吐评测掩盖的平均端到端延迟代价（+5.2%）；同时该配置对处于相反硬件工作状态的 prefill 与 decode GPU 使用同一套设置。作者据此假设：最优功耗设置是（模型、量化、引擎、硬件）组合的属性而非 GPU 类别属性，每条 lane 应有独立配置，且安全地把 SLO 余量转化为能耗需要延迟门控的校准与运行时 SLO 看护，而非固定配方。

Method: 提出 phase-decoupled、模型校准的控制器：prefill lane 运行在 SM 时钟窗口下，其下限在构造上即为延迟保证；decode lane 运行在功耗上限下，该上限由自动校准设定在实测吞吐/延迟悬崖略上方。由于分离式 decode lane 功耗平坦且受内存带宽约束，功耗上限可持续生效，避免了 POLCA 因反应式超调而拒绝加功耗上限的弱点，同时 GPU 自身功耗管理器在上限内维持吞吐。实验在 8x B200 节点上进行，服务 Qwen3-Coder-480B（FP8）的 agentic 负载与 Qwen3-235B-A22B（NVFP4），并对比 Max-Q 等厂商配置；另做 decode 执行器 A/B 测试及三天持续运行。

Result: 在 8x B200 节点服务 Qwen3-Coder-480B（FP8）的 agentic 负载下，balanced 模式实现 +20.4% tokens/J、平均端到端仅 +3.5%，相比 Max-Q 的 +8.6% tokens/J、+5.2% 延迟，在两轴上都构成 Pareto 改进。在 Qwen3-235B-A22B（NVFP4）上，所有运行模式在每次重复中均满足 ITL-p99 SLO，而两个厂商配置均未达标。decode 执行器 A/B 显示校准功耗上限优于静态锁频；三天持续运行节省了一对 lane 32.3% 的电量。两个模型均为 MoE，稠密模型收益约低 5 倍，因此结论限定于 MoE 服务场景。

Conclusion: 最优功耗配置应按部署组合（模型、量化、引擎、硬件）逐 lane 校准，而非套用统一 GPU 级配置；通过 prefill 延迟保证窗口与 decode 校准功耗上限的组合，可在 MoE 分离式推理中实现能效与延迟的 Pareto 改进并稳定满足 SLO。该收益对 MoE 显著、对稠密模型有限，结论适用范围限定于 MoE 服务。

Abstract: Datacenter GPU power is the binding constraint on LLM serving capacity, and production serving has shifted to prefill/decode (PD) disaggregation. Deploying NVIDIA's Max-Q inference profile on a disaggregated B200 system, we found its realized gain modest (+8.6% tokens/J), model-dependent, and carrying a mean end-to-end latency cost (+5.2%) that throughput-only evaluation does not surface; the profile also applies one setting to prefill and decode GPUs that operate in opposite hardware regimes. We hypothesize that the optimal power setting is a property of the deployed (model, quantization, engine, hardware) combination rather than of the GPU class, that each lane warrants its own profile, and that converting SLO headroom into energy safely requires latency-gated calibration under a runtime SLO guard rather than a fixed recipe. We present a phase-decoupled, model-calibrated controller: the prefill lane runs under an SM-clock window whose floor is a latency guarantee by construction, and the decode lane under a power cap placed by automatic calibration just above a measured throughput/latency cliff. Because a disaggregated decode lane draws flat, memory-bound power, the cap binds continuously, the reactive-overshoot weakness that led POLCA to reject capping is absent, and the GPU's own power manager retains throughput under the cap. On an 8x B200 node serving Qwen3-Coder-480B (FP8) under agentic load, our balanced mode delivers +20.4% tokens/J at +3.5% mean e2e versus +8.6% at +5.2% for Max-Q, a Pareto improvement on both axes. On Qwen3-235B-A22B (NVFP4) every operating mode meets the ITL-p99 SLO in every repetition; both vendor profiles miss it. A decode-actuator A/B shows the calibrated cap beats static clock locks, and a three-day sustained run saves 32.3% of a lane pair's electricity. Both models are MoE; a dense model recovers roughly 5x less, so we scope our claims to MoE serving.

</details>


### [134] [Bidirectional Multimodal Fusion of Sky Images and Time-Series for Solar Forecasting with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11135)
*Ken Chen,Maneesha Perera,Wei Wang,Sachith Seneviratne,Hansani Weeratunge,Saman Halgamuge*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SolCloudLLM——一个将天空图像块与时间序列块对齐并双向融合后映射到LLM嵌入空间的多模态光伏/GHI预测框架，在SIRTA和SKIPP'D数据集上全面优于最强基线，MSE最大相对降低25.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 短期光伏功率与全局水平辐照度预测对调度、备用安排和电网运行至关重要，该预测时域内误差主要来自云引起的爬坡事件。仅依赖历史数值数据难以预判即将到来的云，需引入地基天空图像作为补充物理信号；同时预测性能高度依赖站点与局部观测条件，站点专属数据往往稀缺。现有LLM时序预测方法多为单模态，如何有效融合天空图像仍属开放难题。

Method: SolCloudLLM框架：将天空图像块（sky-image patches）与时间序列块（time-series patches）进行对齐，通过双向多模态融合（bidirectional multimodal fusion）融合两者对应表征，形成统一表征，再映射到LLM的嵌入空间。

Result: 在SIRTA与SKIPP'D数据集上，SolCloudLLM在全部预测时域的MSE上持续优于最佳基线，最大相对MSE降低25.4%。分层分析显示多模态融合的收益主要集中在多云条件下。在几乎所有少样本（few-shot）设置中SolCloudLLM表现最佳，而其他深度学习基线性能显著退化，且常被非学习的物理方法超越。

Conclusion: 将天空图像与数值时间序列在LLM框架内进行块级对齐与双向融合，可有效提升短期光伏功率与GHI预测精度，尤其在高不确定性云况与少样本场景下展现出强数据效率和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Short-term photovoltaic (PV) power and global horizontal irradiance (GHI) forecasts are essential for effective dispatch, reserve scheduling, and grid operations. At these forecasting horizons, errors are predominantly driven by cloud induced ramps: relying solely on historical numerical data may struggle to anticipate an incoming cloud, making ground-based sky images a crucial complementary physical signal. Furthermore, forecast performance is highly sensitive to location and local observing conditions, creating a strong need for site-specific data that are often scarce. Recently, large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated competitive performance and high data efficiency in time-series forecasting. Despite their success, existing LLM-based forecasting methods remain predominantly unimodal, relying primarily on historical numerical time-series data. Effectively incorporating sky imagery into an LLM-based forecasting framework remains under-explored and an open challenge. In this paper, we propose SolCloudLLM, an LLM-based multimodal forecasting framework. SolCloudLLM aligns sky-image patches with time-series patches and fuses their corresponding representations through bidirectional multimodal fusion, yielding a unified representation that is subsequently mapped into the embedding space of an LLM. Extensive experiments on the SIRTA and SKIPP'D datasets demonstrate that SolCloudLLM consistently outperforms the best baseline methods in MSE across all forecasting horizons, achieving a maximum relative MSE reduction of 25.4%. Stratified analysis further indicates that the benefits of multimodal fusion are concentrated primarily under cloudy conditions. Notably, SolCloudLLM achieves the best performance in nearly all few-shot settings, whereas other deep learning baselines experience substantial performance degradation and are frequently outperformed by the non-learning physical method.

</details>


### [135] [LILA: Calibration-Free Structured Pruning of Large Language Models via Latent Spectral Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11163)
*Sankar Behera,Dhruv Singh,Anshika Agnihotri,Raj Kumar Choudhary,Satyadev Ahlawat,Yamuna Prasad*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LILA：一种无需校准数据、训练或辅助网络的LLM结构化剪枝方法，基于KS距离的谱重要性准则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM结构化剪枝方法在剪枝阶段需要校准数据、梯度计算或大规模辅助策略网络，成本高且流程复杂，因此亟需一种免训练、免校准的闭式剪枝准则。

Method: LILA通过比较完整FFN权重矩阵与被消融神经元后权重矩阵的经验奇异值分布之间的Kolmogorov–Smirnov距离，度量神经元重要性，形成闭式谱规则；并可将KS分数用于动态稀疏预算分配，配合LoRA恢复微调。

Result: 在LLaMA-2-7B上25%稀疏度、无微调时零样本准确率超PruneNet 1.57个百分点，并比WikiText-2校准的SliceGPT最多高6.0个百分点；经一个epoch LoRA恢复后，性能与重度校准的SliceGPT相差在0.48个百分点内。NTK分析显示功能失真比随机剪枝降低22倍；动态稀疏分配在中等压缩下达到最优生成保持，但高压缩下暴露单层架构瓶颈。

Conclusion: LILA以无校准、无训练、无辅助网络的谱准则实现高效结构化剪枝，兼具理论支撑与实际竞争力，并揭示高压缩下的架构限制。

Abstract: Structured pruning of large language models (LLMs) offers hardware-efficient compression, yet existing methods require calibration data, gradient computation, or large auxiliary policy networks at pruning time. LILA (\emph{Latent-Informed Layer Analysis}) scores neuron importance via the Kolmogorov--Smirnov (KS) distance between empirical singular value distributions of the full and neuron-ablated feed-forward network (FFN) weight matrix, providing a closed-form spectral rule requiring no training, calibration data, or auxiliary network. Without any fine-tuning, LILA surpasses PruneNet (45M-parameter RL policy) by 1.57~pp in zero-shot accuracy on LLaMA-2-7B at 25\% sparsity, and outperforms WikiText-2-calibrated SliceGPT by up to 6.0~pp across all sparsity levels, while preserving the original architecture. After one epoch of LoRA recovery fine-tuning, LILA achieves highly competitive performance, matching the heavily calibrated SliceGPT baseline to within a 0.48~pp margin across LLaMA-2-7B and Phi-2, despite using zero calibration data. A Neural Tangent Kernel analysis confirms a 22$\times$ reduction in functional distortion versus random pruning, providing theoretical grounding for the spectral importance criterion. Finally, extending LILA to dynamically allocate sparsity budgets via KS-scores yields state-of-the-art generative preservation at moderate compression, while uncovering fundamental single-layer architectural bottlenecks at higher compression regimes.

</details>


### [136] [When does a spectral prior help graph learning? Connectivity-loss estimation under road-network disruptions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11166)
*Van-Truong Le*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文用图神经网络学习对一阶Fiedler灵敏度有界修正，快速估计多边删除后的相对代数连通度损失，并在合成图与多国OSM路网上验证其相对解析基线的改进与迁移特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 道路网络中大量链路同时中断的场景需要快速评估连通性影响，精确谱重算代价过高，而局部近似精度不足，因此需要一种兼顾两者、可快速且较准确估计多边删除后代数连通度损失的方法。

Method: 以相对代数连通度损失为估计目标，用GNN学习对一阶Fiedler灵敏度的有界残差修正；考虑独立、空间聚集与边介数定向三种失效模式；采用图不相交合成划分，零样本迁移到6国13个OSM区域；比较GCN、GraphSAGE与边感知MPNN骨干及解析基线，并做稀疏扩展至2万节点实验。

Result: 扩展OSM测试中，残差GCN使空间失效MAE改善0.0391（95%分层区间0.0151-0.0662），残差GraphSAGE使定向失效MAE改善0.0257（0.0095-0.0446）；二阶扰动仅使一阶MAE改善0.0028-0.0053；定向迁移下修正斜率下降，表明残差向系统先验误差收缩；留一国家OSM到OSM迁移结果混合，残差GCN使定向MAE改善0.0622（0.0169-0.1153）但空间点估计变差。

Conclusion: 谱残差是一种有用但对领域敏感的归纳偏置，适合结构连通性筛查；研究明确了其边界、迁移局限与成本结构，代码与可复现材料已归档。

Abstract: Rapid evaluation of many simultaneous road-link disruptions requires a practical compromise between exact spectral recomputation and local approximation. We estimate relative algebraic-connectivity loss after multi-edge deletion using graph neural networks (GNNs) that learn a bounded correction to a first-order Fiedler sensitivity. The study considers independent, spatially clustered, and edge-betweenness-targeted failures, with graph-disjoint synthetic splits and zero-shot transfer to 13 OpenStreetMap (OSM) areas in six countries. GCN, GraphSAGE, and edge-aware MPNN backbones are compared with analytical baselines. In expanded OSM tests, residual GCN improves spatial-failure MAE by 0.0391 (95% hierarchical interval 0.0151-0.0662), while residual GraphSAGE improves targeted-failure MAE by 0.0257 (0.0095-0.0446). Second-order perturbation improves first-order MAE by only 0.0028-0.0053. Correction slopes decrease under targeted transfer, indicating residual shrinkage around systematic prior error. Leave-one-country-out OSM-to-OSM transfer is mixed: residual GCN improves targeted-failure MAE by 0.0622 (0.0169-0.1153) but worsens the spatial point estimate. Sparse scaling extends to 20,000 nodes and separates one-time spectral setup from amortized screening cost. These results characterize the spectral residual as a useful but domain-sensitive inductive bias for structural connectivity screening. Code, cached networks, and reproducibility artifacts are archived at doi:10.5281/zenodo.22307723.

</details>


### [137] [Semi-Tensor Product-Based Multi-Term Randomized T-SVD and Its Visual Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11168)
*Xingchen Xiao,Feng Zhang,Wenjin Qin,Jianjun Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在任意可逆线性变换诱导的t-乘积框架下，提出三阶张量的半张量积，并构建多单项半张量积SVD（MSTP-SVD），进而结合随机投影与幂迭代得到加速算法MRSTP-SVD，在图像与视频压缩、补全任务上兼顾精度与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准T-SVD依赖t-乘积，对张量维度匹配要求严格；基于半张量积（STP）的扩展虽放宽了维度约束，但单项式表述的低秩逼近精度有限，且确定性方法在大规模张量上计算代价高昂。因此需要一种既能突破维度匹配限制、又具备更高逼近精度与更低计算成本的方法。

Method: 在由任意可逆线性变换诱导的t-乘积框架下，定义三阶张量的新型半张量积，使张量半张量积在打破标准t-乘积刚性维度匹配的同时保留T-SVD的闭式性质。基于此构造多单项半张量积奇异值分解（MSTP-SVD），融合多个正交分解项以提升低秩逼近精度。为降低多单项建模开销，引入随机投影与幂迭代技术，形成加速的多单项随机半张量积SVD（MRSTP-SVD）算法。

Result: 在图像与视频压缩及补全任务上的实验验证了所提方法的有效性，MRSTP-SVD在重建精度与计算效率之间取得平衡。

Conclusion: 所提出的半张量积构造与MSTP-SVD/MRSTP-SVD为处理高维视觉数据提供了兼具维度灵活性与高逼近精度的分解工具，并通过随机化加速满足大规模张量计算需求。

Abstract: Tensor singular value decomposition (T-SVD), which is built upon the tensor-tensor product (t-product), has emerged as a powerful tool for processing high-dimensional visual data such as color images and videos. However, the standard t-product imposes strict dimensional compatibility constraints. Although extensions based on the semi-tensor product (STP) relax this restriction, their single-term formulations still suffer from limited approximation accuracy. Moreover, these deterministic methods incur high computational costs when processing large-scale tensor data. To address these issues, this paper introduces a novel semi-tensor product for third-order tensors under the t-product framework induced by arbitrary invertible linear transforms. The resulting tensor semi-tensor product breaks the rigid dimension matching requirement of the standard t-product, while retaining the closed-form property of T-SVD. Based on this construction, we develop a multi-term semi-tensor product singular value decomposition (MSTP-SVD), which integrates multiple orthogonal decomposition terms to significantly improve low-rank approximation accuracy compared with single-term schemes. To reduce the computational cost of multi-term modeling, we incorporate randomized projection and power iteration techniques into the MSTP-SVD framework, yielding an accelerated multi-term randomized semi-tensor product SVD (MRSTP-SVD) algorithm that achieves a balance between reconstruction accuracy and computational efficiency. Experiments on image and video compression and completion tasks demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed method.

</details>


### [138] [Convex Optimization with Nested Evolving Feasible Sets (CONES) under Time-Varying Loss Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11207)
*Rahul Vaze*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将 CONES 问题扩展到损失函数随时间变化的情形，分析了投影近端算法的遗憾与移动成本权衡，并给出相应下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原 CONES 模型仅允许目标函数固定、可行域嵌套收缩，本文希望进一步刻画损失函数也随时间变化的更一般在线优化场景。

Method: 采用投影近端算法（projected proximal algorithm）求解扩展后的 CONES，并针对凸与强凸损失分别进行上界分析，同时构造弱自适应算法及子线性 anytime 遗憾算法的下界。

Result: 凸损失下，算法同时取得 O(T^{1-β}) 遗憾与 O(T^β) 移动成本；下界表明弱自适应算法 O(T^β) 遗憾对应 Ω(T^{(1-β)/2}) 移动成本。强凸损失下，算法同时取得 O(1) 遗憾与 O(log T) 移动成本；下界表明子线性 anytime 遗憾算法移动成本为 Ω(log T)。

Conclusion: 扩展 CONES 在时变损失下仍存在遗憾—移动成本权衡：凸情形呈多项式折中，强凸情形可实现常数遗憾但移动成本对数下界不可避免。

Abstract: Convex Optimization with Nested Evolving Feasible Sets (CONES)} was introduced in \cite{CONESVaze} where the objective function \(f\) remains fixed but the feasible region evolves over time as a nested sequence \(S_1 \supseteq S_2 \supseteq \cdots \supseteq S_T\). The goal of an online algorithm is to simultaneously minimize the regret with respect to hindsight static optimal benchmark and the total movement cost $M_\cA(T)$ while ensuring feasibility at all times. CONES is an optimization-oriented generalization of the well-known \emph{nested convex body chasing} (NCBC). In this paper, we extend CONES to allow for loss functions $f_t'$s to also change over time. When all loss functions are convex, we show that the projected proximal algorithm achieves $O(T^{1-β}), O(T^β)$ simultaneous regret and movement cost, respectively, for any $β\in [0,1)$, over a time horizon of $T$. We also show that any {\it weakly adaptive} online algorithm with $O(T^β)$ regret has a movement cost of $Ω\left(T^{\frac{1-β}{2}}\right)$ for any $β\in [0,1)$. When all loss functions are strongly convex, we show that the projected proximal algorithm simultaneously achieves $O(1)$ regret and a movement cost of $O(\log T)$. To complement this, we show that any online algorithm with sublinear {\it anytime} regret has a movement cost of $Ω\left(\log T\right)$.

</details>


### [139] [REVA: Reusable Evidence View Aggregation for Context-Efficient RAG Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11209)
*Tuan Nguyen,Qiran Hu,Banruo Liu,Khoa D. Doan,Kok-Seng Wong,Fan Lai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出REVA框架，从数据挖掘视角将历史查询-文档-模型交互聚合为可复用证据视图，实现高效RAG检索后压缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG中长上下文带来延迟、KV缓存内存与token成本上升；检索后压缩可降本，但现有压缩器逐查询独立运行、依赖辅助模型或重写，并引入可抵消短提示收益的在线开销。

Method: 先实证现代压缩器相对简单截断收益不稳定且增加推理延迟；提出REVA，将目标生成器历史注意力轨迹挖掘为以文档为键、预算无关的分数存储，把token级注意力映射到可读词单元，跨重复文档访问聚合重要性，并按预算渲染保持文档顺序与标准RAG接口的纯文本视图。

Result: 在四个代表性基准与现代LLM上，REVA较现有先进方法生成质量提升1.0–5.8分，压缩开销降低5.3–15.6倍，额外延迟低于40 ms。

Conclusion: RAG压缩可从逐查询在线压缩转向可复用证据视图挖掘，在保持标准接口的同时兼顾质量、成本与延迟。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) improves knowledge-intensive large language model (LLM) applications by conditioning generation on retrieved documents, but longer contexts increase latency, key-value (KV) cache memory, and token cost. Post-retrieval compression can reduce this cost, yet existing compressors often operate independently for each query, rely on auxiliary models or rewriting, and introduce online overhead that can offset the benefit of shorter prompts. We revisit RAG compression from a data-mining perspective by aggregating historical query--document--model interactions into reusable evidence views. We first show that modern compressors have unstable gains over simple truncation and can add substantial inference-time latency. We then propose Reusable Evidence View Aggregation (REVA), a framework that mines the target generator's historical attention traces into a document-keyed, budget-agnostic score store. REVA maps token-level attention to readable word units, aggregates importance across repeated document accesses, and renders budget-specific plain-text views that preserve document order and the standard RAG interface. Across four representative benchmarks and modern LLMs, REVA improves generation quality by 1.0--5.8 points over existing advances, while reducing compression overhead by a factor of 5.3 to 15.6, adding less than 40 ms of latency.

</details>


### [140] [Legible Failures: Detecting and Repairing In-Context Binding Errors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11216)
*Manas Venkata Sai Ravulapalli,Samrath Singh Chadha,Abhinav M. Hari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究"模型答错但内部隐状态已含正确答案"的现象：在实体—义务绑定任务上，线性探针可从冻结隐状态中恢复正确答案，其信号既能改进失败检测，也能在无金标签条件下通过残差流引导提升准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 错误答案本身无法区分"模型缺乏所需信息"还是"模型掌握信息却未能使用"。作者希望厘清这一区分，并检验探针所揭示的内部知识是否具有可操作性（actionable），即能否用于失败检测与干预纠错，而不是仅作为事后诊断工具。

Method: 采用实体—义务绑定任务，覆盖16个公开检查点、每个检查点3个随机种子。在训练折上拟合线性探针，在验证折上选择层，在不相交的测试折上报告结果。以严格呈现义务基线1/K=0.125为参照，比较模型答错试验上的探针准确率；用查询实体反事实排除token存在性与近因性解释；构建基于探针输出符号分歧的分数用于失败检测；并将残差流导向探针解码的绑定，在无金标签条件下进行引导实验。

Result: 模型答错的试验中，探针准确率比严格呈现义务基线1/K=0.125高出+0.196（95% CI [+0.101, +0.296]，按模型自助抽样）。查询实体反事实排除了token存在性与近因性。基于探针输出分歧符号的分数使失败检测AUROC相比模型自身置信度提升+0.079（95% CI [+0.036, +0.126]），而原始探针置信度相比模型置信度无显著提升。在无金标签条件下将残差流导向探针解码的绑定，使全部八个被测模型平均准确率提升+0.168（95% CI [+0.066, +0.280]）。

Conclusion: 在上下文绑定任务中，语言模型可能答错却仍保有所需信息，探针可解码该信息。与近期研究报告"探针检测到的错误难以被干预纠正"不同，本文表明上下文绑定是探针具有可操作性的场景：探针信号可用于改进失败检测，并在无金标签引导下提升准确率。

Abstract: A wrong answer does not show whether the model lacked the needed information or held it and failed to use it. On an entity-obligation binding task, a language model can emit an incorrect prompt-supplied binding while a linear probe can recover the correct one from its frozen hidden state. We measure how often this occurs across 16 public checkpoints, each evaluated with three seeds. We fit a probe on a training fold, select its layer on a validation fold, and report results on a disjoint test fold. On the trials each model gets wrong, probe accuracy exceeds the strict present-obligation baseline, 1/K = 0.125, by +0.196 (95% CI [+0.101, +0.296], bootstrapped over models). A query-entity counterfactual rules out token presence and recency. A score built from the sign of probe-output disagreement improves failure detection over the model's own confidence by +0.079 AUROC (95% CI [+0.036, +0.126]). Raw probe confidence gives no measurable improvement over model confidence. Steering the residual stream toward the probe-decoded binding, with no gold label, raises accuracy on all eight models tested by a mean of +0.168 (95% CI [+0.066, +0.280]). Where recent studies report that probe-detected errors are resistant to interventions, we find that in-context binding is a setting in which probes are actionable.

</details>


### [141] [AdamX: Cosine similarity meets gradient descent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11867)
*Francisco Caldas,Ruben Belo,Cláudia Soares*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出AdamX一阶优化器，用余弦相似度自适应控制更新幅度，并引入方差校正机制，在多个基准与架构上取得有竞争力的收敛速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有优化器在更新幅度控制与训练早期稳定性方面仍有改进空间，作者希望设计一种可扩展、模型无关且易集成的自适应机制。

Method: 提出AdamX，将余弦相似度作为自适应机制来控制更新幅度；并引入方差校正方案，在训练早期促进更平滑的优化。方法可与现有训练流程直接结合。

Result: 在多种基准数据集和架构上进行实证评估，以固定超参数预算下达到预设性能阈值所需epoch数为指标，显示具有竞争力的收敛速度。

Conclusion: AdamX是一种模型无关、易集成的优化器，在固定超参数预算下表现出竞争性收敛性能，代码与实验已开源。

Abstract: We introduce AdamX, a first-order optimizer that incorporates cosine similarity as an adaptive mechanism for controlling update magnitudes. The proposed method is scalable, model-agnostic, and straightforward to integrate into existing training pipelines. We further introduce a variance rectification scheme that promotes smoother optimization during the early stages of training. Overall, we provide empirical evidence that AdamX achieves competitive convergence rates across a range of benchmark datasets and architectures. Performance is evaluated in terms of the number of epochs required to reach predefined performance thresholds under a fixed hyperparameter budget. Code and Experiments available at: https://github.com/FranciscoCaldas/adamX.

</details>


### [142] [Polyhedral Geometry of Time-to-First-Spike Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11227)
*Manjot Singh,Guido Montúfar,Gitta Kutyniok*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究脉冲神经网络（SNN）在time-to-first-spike模型下的表达能力，证明其计算映射为连续分段线性函数，并推导其"因果区域"数量的上下界，表明SNN能够比传统ReLU前馈网络生成更丰富的输入空间划分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脉冲神经网络为异步、事件驱动计算提供了自然框架，可作为传统前馈网络的补充。然而其表达能力（尤其是输入空间划分的丰富程度）尚缺乏系统的理论刻画。论文旨在从分段线性表示与因果区域两个角度，量化SNN相对于ReLU网络的表达优势。

Method: 采用time-to-first-spike（首脉冲时间）模型，在输入-输出映射连续且分段线性的设定下，每个仿射片段由因果可行性约束决定（即哪些突触前脉冲先于神经元放电）。首先给出单个神经元放电时间的maxout类表示，其次将因果区域形式化为具有固定因果集合的多面体区域，并据此推导浅层与多层前馈脉冲网络中最大因果区域数量的上下界，结合理论分析与实验验证。

Result: 每个神经元的放电时间可表示为具有指数级数量、且高度受约束仿射片段的maxout形式；推导出浅层和多层前馈SNN中最大因果区域数量的上界与下界；理论与实验均显示，脉冲网络能够产生比传统前馈ReLU网络更丰富的输入空间划分。

Conclusion: SNN在time-to-first-spike模型下的分段线性表达能力优于传统ReLU前馈网络，其因果区域的数量下界高于ReLU网络的可达划分。这为SNN作为事件驱动计算范式的表达优势提供了理论依据。

Abstract: We study the expressivity of spiking neural networks, which provide a natural framework for asynchronous, event-driven computation complementary to conventional feedforward neural networks. We consider the time-to-first-spike model in a setting for which the input-output map is continuous and piecewise linear, with affine pieces governed by causal feasibility constraints that determine which presynaptic spikes occur before a neuron fires. We first show that each neuron's firing time admits a maxout-like representation with exponentially many, highly constrained affine pieces. We then formalize causal regions as polyhedral regions with fixed causal sets and derive upper and lower bounds on the maximal number of causal regions in both shallow and multilayer feedforward spiking networks. Our theoretical and experimental results show that spiking networks can generate richer partitions of the input space than conventional feedforward ReLU networks.

</details>


### [143] [Solving Few-Shot Multiobjective Multitask Optimization via Iterative Sequential Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11228)
*Tingyang Wei,Haofeng Wu,Ananda Phan Iman,Zhao Wei,Jiao Liu,Yew-Soon Ong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出迭代顺序迁移（IST）方法，将多任务优化（MTO）重构为一系列顺序迁移优化问题，并利用似然感知的任务优先级机制克服少样本预算下难以对齐精英解分布的瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多任务优化中的知识迁移机制依赖跨任务精英解分布的对齐，但在少样本优化场景下评估预算受限，难以识别精英解分布，这一瓶颈在多目标多任务问题中因需逼近连续Pareto流形而进一步加剧。

Method: 提出迭代顺序迁移（IST）：将MTO建模为一系列顺序迁移优化问题，每次迭代仅对单一目标任务集中评估；并设计似然感知的任务优先级机制，通过识别最可能已具备知识整合条件的任务来最大化迁移效用。

Result: 在基准测试与实际问题上验证了所提方法在紧预算条件下的有效性。

Conclusion: IST通过顺序单目标任务评估与似然感知任务优先级机制绕过精英解分布对齐瓶颈，为紧预算下的多目标多任务优化提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Applying knowledge transfer across multiple optimization tasks, multitask optimization (MTO) emerges as a promising approach to solving synergistic optimization tasks simultaneously. However, the development of effective knowledge transfer mechanisms in MTO fundamentally relies on aligning elite solution distributions across tasks. This dependency creates a critical bottleneck in few-shot optimization regimes, as restricted evaluation budgets impede the identification of elite solution distributions required for beneficial transfer. This challenge is exacerbated in multiobjective multitask problems, where each optimizer must approximate a continuous Pareto manifold rather than a single optimal point. This paper introduces Iterative Sequential Transfer (IST) to circumvent this bottleneck. We model MTO as a sequence of sequential transfer optimization problems, concentrating evaluations on a single target per iteration. We propose a likelihood-informed task prioritization mechanism to maximize transfer utility by identifying the task most likely ready for knowledge integration. Empirical results on benchmark and real-world problems verify the effectiveness of the proposed method under tight budgets.

</details>


### [144] [MUtE: A Dual Framework for Concept Erasure and Counterfactual Interventions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11253)
*Antoine Saillenfest*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文重新审视概念擦除的最优界，提出一类能自然诱导确定性对偶反事实映射的擦除函数，通过对反事实轨迹施加平移偏置，实现概念擦除与反事实生成之间的无缝切换，并在算法公平性与反事实文本生成任务上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从表征中擦除特定概念信息有助于缓解偏见或解释模型决策。其联合目标是在使目标概念不可预测的同时，最大程度保留与概念无关的信息。然而现有方法在理论最优性与实际表示学习之间存在差距，且擦除与反事实生成通常被割裂处理。

Method: 作者重新推导概念擦除的最优界，据此构造一类新的擦除函数，该类函数天然诱导确定性的对偶反事实映射；并设计具体实现，对反事实轨迹施加平移偏置（translational bias），该约束契合现代语言模型中许多概念的几何表现形式。该框架统一了概念擦除与反事实生成。

Result: 实验表明，该框架在提升下游算法公平性以及生成反事实文本两方面均取得良好效果。

Conclusion: 该工作弥合了概念擦除理论最优性与实际表示学习之间的鸿沟，通过平移偏置约束实现了擦除与反事实生成的统一，为公平性改进与反事实文本生成提供了新思路。

Abstract: Erasing concept-specific information from representations has been proven useful for mitigating bias or interpreting model decisions. The joint objective is to transform the original representations such that the target concept becomes unpredictable, while maximally preserving concept-unrelated information. In this work, we revisit the optimal bounds of concept erasure to derive a novel class of erasure functions that naturally induce a deterministic, dual counterfactual mapping. Bridging the gap between theoretical optimality and practical representation learning, we design an implementation that imposes a translational bias on counterfactual trajectories - a constraint that aligns with how many concepts geometrically manifest in modern language models. Our framework enables seamless navigation between concept erasure and counterfactual generation. We empirically demonstrate its efficacy in improving downstream algorithmic fairness and generating counterfactual texts.

</details>


### [145] [A Dynamic Fusion Large Language Model for Traffic Flow Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11314)
*Xue Qiu,Jianli Xiao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出动态融合大语言模型DF-LLM用于交通流预测，通过时空嵌入、时空融合与LLM主干三大模块实现多尺度时空特征协同表示与全局依赖建模，在四个数据集上取得更优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交通流预测对智能交通系统至关重要。传统神经网络因依赖单一特征建模而难以突破精度瓶颈；大语言模型（LLM）则存在空间拓扑信息捕获不足、时空关联挖掘不充分的问题。因此需要一种能兼顾时空特征建模与LLM能力的融合方案。

Method: 模型包含三个核心组件：①时空嵌入模块，实现多尺度时空特征的协同表示；②时空融合模块，通过图卷积整合空间拓扑与动态依赖关系；③LLM主干，采用差异化参数适配策略以平衡训练效率与交通数据适应性，并引入上下文聚合注意力模块增强全局依赖，同时使用残差连接缓解深层网络中的梯度消失问题。

Result: 在四个数据集的全部评价指标上，DF-LLM均取得了优于对比方法的性能。

Conclusion: DF-LLM通过时空嵌入、图卷积时空融合与LLM差异化适配的结合，有效弥补了传统神经网络与现有LLM方法在时空建模上的不足，验证了其在交通流预测任务中的有效性。

Abstract: Traffic flow prediction is a core supporting technology for intelligent transportation systems. It uses historical data to infer future traffic dynamics in specific areas, thereby helping to alleviate congestion and improve resource allocation efficiency. Traditional neural networks struggle to break through accuracy limits due to their reliance on singular feature modeling, while large language models (LLMs) suffer from insufficient capture of spatial topological information and mining spatiotemporal correlation. This study proposes a Dynamic Fusion Large Language Model (DF-LLM) for traffic flow prediction. The model incorporates three core components: spatiotemporal embedding module, spatiotemporal fusion module, and LLM backbone. The spatiotemporal embedding module enables synergistic representation of multi-scale spatiotemporal features. The spatiotemporal fusion module integrates spatial topology and dynamic dependencies via graph convolution. The LLM backbone adopts a differentiated parameter adaptation strategy to balance training efficiency and traffic data adaptability. Additionally, it introduces a context aggregation attention module to strengthens global dependencies. More importantly, the LLM backbone takes the residual connections to mitigate the gradient vanishing in deep networks. Experiments show that DF-LLM has achieved better performance by comparing the metrics on all the four datasets.

</details>


### [146] [Estimating Inconsistency Response Surfaces under Uncertainty in Cyber-Physical System Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11331)
*Johannes Mäkelburg,Tim Schwabe,Maribel Acosta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文将CPS多模型一致性中的不一致性问题重构为干预-响应建模问题，利用Saltelli采样与多保真蒙特卡洛生成数据集并训练代理模型，实现不一致性的快速预测、敏感性分析与一致性修复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CPS通常由多个相互关联的模型表示，开发过程中需保证共享模型元素在各模型间保持兼容。不确定性（如传感器噪声、模型抽象）会改变模型元素的可行取值，从而引入不一致性。现有方法虽能针对给定不确定性配置判断一致性，但难以在庞大不确定性空间中系统探索、分析与解释不一致性。

Method: 作者将不一致性重构为干预-响应建模问题。采用Saltelli采样与多保真蒙特卡洛估计生成干预-响应数据集，并训练代理模型，直接从传播后的不确定性几何特征预测不一致性。基于学习到的响应曲面，开展敏感性分析以识别主导不确定性驱动因素，并提出基于梯度的一致性补救方法，求解可恢复一致性的最小不确定性干预。

Result: 在48个场景、10个CPS领域的实验中，代理模型与蒙特卡洛估计结果相匹配，同时将评估时间从毫秒级降至微秒级，在固定计算预算下可实现数量级更多的响应曲面评估。

Conclusion: 结果表明，不确定性下的不一致性可被有效学习、分析与修复，响应曲面建模为CPS开发中不确定性感知的一致性管理提供了可扩展的基础。

Abstract: Cyber-Physical Systems (CPS) are commonly represented through multiple interconnected models. During development, CPS consistency requires that shared model elements remain compatible across these models. Uncertainty, for example, due to sensor noise or model abstraction, changes the admissible values of model elements and can introduce inconsistencies, i.e., situations in which models can no longer be jointly satisfied. While existing approaches can determine consistency for a given uncertainty configuration, they provide limited support for systematically exploring, analyzing, and explaining inconsistency across large uncertainty spaces. We address this challenge by reformulating inconsistency as an intervention response modeling problem. Using Saltelli sampling and multi-fidelity Monte Carlo estimation, we generate intervention-response datasets and train a surrogate model that directly predicts inconsistency from the propagated uncertainty geometry. Experiments on 48 scenarios and 10 CPS domains show that the surrogate matches Monte Carlo estimates while reducing evaluation time from milliseconds to microseconds, enabling orders-of-magnitude more response-surface evaluations within fixed computational budgets. Building on the learned response surfaces, we perform sensitivity analysis to identify dominant uncertainty drivers and introduce a gradient-based consistency recourse method to determine minimal uncertainty interventions that restore consistency. The results show that inconsistency under uncertainty can be effectively learned, analyzed, and repaired through response-surface modeling, providing a scalable foundation for uncertainty-aware consistency management in CPS development.

</details>


### [147] [Reification as a Transferable Vocabulary: Zero-Shot Link Prediction with Vanilla GNNs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11347)
*Camille Pradel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 将迁移机制从架构移入表示：通过"物化"将事实变为节点，用固定六种元关系连接主语、宾语与关系类型，五种教科书级GNN在单图30分钟训练后零样本迁移至40个归纳链接预测基准，最佳GAT可媲美专用基础模型ULTRA；该词表还可扩展至关系数据库。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有知识图谱基础模型（如ULTRA）依赖专门架构硬编码迁移机制来实现对未见图的零样本链接预测，这种设计与具体架构强耦合，限制了通用性与可扩展性。作者希望把迁移能力从架构中解耦出来，转由数据表示本身承担，从而让标准GNN也能具备基础模型式的零样本迁移能力。

Method: 对输入图进行"物化/具体化"（reifying）：每条事实变为一个节点，通过固定词表中的六种元关系连接到其主语、宾语和关系类型；关系类型作为匿名共享节点而非模型参数。在此表示上直接使用五种现成GNN（GAT、GINE-sum、GINE-mean+max、GraphSAGE、R-GCN），每个模型仅在单个含4,245条三元组的知识图谱上训练，单张NVIDIA A100上训练30分钟。该方法进一步推广到关系数据库：一行作为实体、外键列作为关系类型。

Result: 五种GNN均能零样本迁移到40个归纳链接预测基准；其中表现最好的现成GAT在ULTRA自身的评测套件上可匹配ULTRA（一个在三张图上预训练的专用基础模型）。在数据库探测中，使用两个未见数据库、不提供单元格值、模式文本或上下文标签，该家族模型在三张知识图谱上预训练后，将外键目标排序远高于随机初始化与度数基线。

Conclusion: 迁移机制可被移出专用架构并编码进统一的图表示，使现成GNN以极小训练成本获得与专用基础模型相当的零样本迁移能力，且该表示可自然扩展到关系数据库等结构化数据。作者发布了全部40个基准的代码、检查点与评测流程。

Abstract: Knowledge graph foundation models such as ULTRA achieve zero-shot link prediction on unseen graphs through dedicated architectures that hard-code a transfer mechanism. In this work we move that mechanism out of the architecture and into the representation, by \emph{reifying} the input graph: every fact becomes a node, connected to its subject, object, and relation type through a fixed vocabulary of six meta-relations, with relation types as anonymous shared nodes rather than model parameters. On this representation, five textbook GNNs (GAT, GINE with sum and with mean+max aggregation, GraphSAGE, R-GCN), each trained on a single knowledge graph of 4,245 triples for 30 minutes on one NVIDIA A100, transfer zero-shot to 40 inductive link-prediction benchmarks. The best of them, an off-the-shelf GAT, matches ULTRA, a dedicated foundation model pretrained on three graphs, across ULTRA's own evaluation suite. The same fixed vocabulary extends to relational databases, a row becoming an entity and a foreign-key column a relation type; a preliminary probe on two unseen databases, with no cell values, schema text or in-context labels, shows a model of this family pretrained on three knowledge graphs ranking foreign-key targets far above random-initialization and degree controls. We release the code, the checkpoints, and the evaluation pipeline for all 40 benchmarks.

</details>


### [148] [Local Robustness Quantification for Naive Bayes Classifiers and Generative Forests: a General Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11366)
*Adrián Detavernier,Jasper De Bock*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出针对朴素贝叶斯与生成式森林（PGM 型生成分类器）的预测鲁棒性计算方法，通过扰动局部模型并按 ε-污染、全变差距离与卡方散度球刻画鲁棒性，并在基准数据集上验证其可作为可信度指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成分类器（如基于 PGM 的朴素贝叶斯与生成式森林）的预测可信度评估缺乏系统的鲁棒性量化手段，需要一个能刻画“分布可被扰动而不改变预测”程度的指标，以判断预测的可信性。

Method: 按鲁棒性量化范式，将预测鲁棒性定义为分类器分布在不改变该预测前提下可被扰动的最大程度；通过在图模型局部模型上施加扰动实现，扰动集合采用一般邻域，重点考虑 ε-污染、全变差距离球和卡方散度球；针对朴素贝叶斯分类器与生成式森林（随机森林的概率扩展）两类生成分类器给出计算鲁棒性的方法。

Result: 在基准数据集上验证了所提方法，表明预测的鲁棒性值可作为其可信度的指示器，并与其他可信度指标进行了对比。

Conclusion: 所提方法可为 PGM 型生成分类器提供有效的鲁棒性量化，且鲁棒性值可充当预测可信度指标，与现有可信度指标具有可比性。

Abstract: We provide methods for calculating the robustness of the predictions of two types of generative classifiers whose underlying distribution is a Probabilistic Graphical Model (PGM): naive Bayes classifiers and generative forests (a probabilistic extension of random forests). Following the paradigm of robustness quantification, we define the robustness of a prediction as the extent to which the distribution of the classifier can be perturbed without changing this prediction. We consider perturbations obtained by varying the local models of the PGMs within general neighborhoods and focus in particular on epsilon-contamination, total variation distance and chi-squared divergence balls. We test our methods on benchmark datasets, demonstrate that the robustness value of a prediction serves as an indicator for its trustworthiness and compare our approach with other such indicators.

</details>


### [149] [Prevalence Determines Precision:Silent Contamination in Detector-Defined Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11449)
*Jia Huang,Yankai Wan,Yangjun Ou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 数据集精度由候选池中真阳性流行率经贝叶斯决定，而非仅由检测器质量决定；忽略流行率迁移会导致精度估计严重失真，且污染效应方向依赖于估计量构造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多ML数据集通过检测器、启发式规则或模型在候选池上运行来构建，被接受的条目成为标签，因此数据集精度受各池真阳性流行率支配。作者希望纠正"仅凭检测器质量即可判定数据集精度"的常见误区，并刻画污染对统计量的具体影响。

Method: 在单一检测器与固定时间区间内，构建一个检测器定义的事件数据集，并配以独立官方索引，将每个被检测条目标注为真实或幻影。比较三个候选池的幻影率，并将高幻影率池的精度迁移到低幻影率池进行预测；同时用贝叶斯表达式校验。进一步将检测响应曲线分解为真实事件与幻影两个分量，并考察不同估计量在相同窗口下的行为差异。

Result: 三个池的幻影率分别为81.7%、9.0%和0.0%。将两个高幻影率池的精度迁移到低幻影率池，预测值为0.955，而实测为0.183，误差达+422%；贝叶斯表达式对三个池的预测误差均在3.3%以内。检测响应曲线是真实事件分量与幻影分量的精确凸组合（残差1.1e-16），幻影数量473超过真实事件308，表明污染是携带检测器形状的第二信号而非加性噪声。在相同窗口下，一个统计量被稀释而另一个被放大，因为其分母同样被污染。某种常用归一化使估计量变为比值均值，其期望可能不存在：在同一335个事件上返回0.40，而良定义估计量返回0.10。

Conclusion: 数据集精度不能被检测器质量单独界定，必须显式考虑候选池的流行率并通过贝叶斯折算。污染具有检测器继承的结构性形状，对估计量的影响方向取决于其构造（含分母）；某些常规归一化会引入期望不存在的病态估计量，应在实践者构建与评估数据集时予以避免。

Abstract: Many ML datasets are constructed by running a detector, heuristic, or model over candidate pools; accepted items become labels. Dataset precision is then governed by true-positive prevalence in each pool via Bayes, not solely by detector quality. Using one instrument and period, we hold a detector-defined event dataset plus an independent official index labeling every detected item as real or phantom. One detector, three pools yield phantom rates 81.7%, 9.0%, and 0.0%. Transferring precision from the two high-rate pools to the low-rate pool predicts 0.955 versus measured 0.183, a +422% error; the Bayes expression predicts all three within 3.3%. The detected response curve is an exact convex combination of a true-event and a phantom component (residual 1.1e-16), with phantoms outnumbering true events 473 to 308, so contamination is a second signal with detector-inherited shape, not additive noise. Contamination direction depends on the estimator: on identical windows one statistic is diluted and another inflated because its denominator is also contaminated. A common normalization turns the estimator into a mean of ratios whose expectation need not exist; on the same 335 events it returns 0.40 where the well-defined estimator returns 0.10.

</details>


### [150] [Combining Synthetic and Real Data for Low-Resource Historical OCR: A Manchu Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11495)
*Yan Hon Michael Chung,Hanlin Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究探讨低资源满文OCR中合成数据与真实历史数据的组合策略，发现引入真实训练图像可将词准确率提升至95%以上，且集成投票可达98.27%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 满文作为濒危语言，其清代档案虽大量数字化，但仍难以大规模检索与分析。此前工作表明仅用合成满文词图像训练的视觉语言模型（VLM）在真实清代手稿与刻本上仅达87.4%词准确率，存在显著的合成到真实（synthetic-to-real）差距，因此需要研究如何合理组合合成与真实历史训练数据。

Method: 使用60,000张合成与20,306张真实历史词图像，在统一的检查点选择与档案评估协议下，评估三种预训练VLM和一种紧凑的卷积循环神经网络（CRNN），比较四种训练范式：仅合成、仅真实、合成-真实联合、以及先合成后真实的顺序训练。

Result: 引入真实训练图像后，最优配置的词准确率提升至95.09%–96.28%，而所有仅合成配置均不超过87.92%。合成补充对三种VLM均有显著提升，但对CRNN的边际效果对训练目标敏感。联合训练与顺序训练在实用流程下档案准确率大致相当。紧凑CRNN在具备真实图像时也能达到领先水平，说明模型规模本身不决定识别精度。此外，强识别器间的互补错误可通过投票在无需额外训练的情况下将准确率提升至98.27%，而一部18世纪满文词典为分歧裁决提供了有原则的规则。

Conclusion: 真实历史训练数据是突破合成到真实性能瓶颈的关键；合成数据对VLM有稳定增益，但效果因模型与目标而异；模型规模并非准确率决定因素；结合互补错误投票与词典规则可在不额外训练的情况下进一步提升准确率。

Abstract: Manchu, now critically endangered, was one of the principal languages of the Qing empire (1636-1912), and its extensive archival record is increasingly digitized but remains difficult to search and analyze at scale. Previous work showed that vision-language models (VLMs) trained only on synthetic Manchu word images can reach 87.4% word accuracy on real Qing manuscripts and prints, leaving a substantial synthetic-to-real gap. This study examines how synthetic and real historical training data should be combined for low-resource OCR. Using 60,000 synthetic and 20,306 real historical word images, we evaluate three pretrained VLMs and a compact convolutional recurrent neural network (CRNN) under four regimes: synthetic-only, real-only, joint synthetic-real, and sequential synthetic-to-real training, following a common checkpoint-selection and archival evaluation protocol. Introducing real training images raises the leading configurations to between 95.09% and 96.28% word accuracy, while no synthetic-only configuration exceeds 87.92%. Synthetic supplementation substantially improves all three VLMs, whereas its marginal effect for the CRNN is sensitive to the training objective. Joint and sequential training yield broadly similar archival accuracy under the tested practical pipelines. A compact CRNN also reaches the leading performance range once real images are available, showing that model scale alone does not determine recognition accuracy. Finally, complementary errors among strong recognizers allow voting to raise accuracy to 98.27% without additional training, while an eighteenth-century Manchu dictionary provides a principled rule for adjudicating disagreements.

</details>


### [151] [DeFiFlowBench: Benchmarking and Improving Safe Executability in Natural-Language DeFi Workflow Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11504)
*Abhinav Rajeev Kumar,Harshit Arora,Varun Singh,Manikandan Nanjappan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 DeFiFlowBench 基准与 Koan-Safe 系统，用执行级测试评估自然语言 DeFi 工作流的安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结构上合法的 DeFi 工作流仍可能授权代价高昂的交易，说明静态结构校验无法保障安全，需要面向真实执行的评估与防护机制。

Method: 构建 DeFiFlowBench：207 条团队撰写的自然语言 DeFi 工作流合成提示，评测图覆盖度、配置完整性与声明的安全谓词，并在本地 EVM 上测试受支持的交易配置。提出 Koan-Safe，组合仅提示的意图解析器、可替换生成器，以及带默认安全参数的结构化修复；含匹配候选消融。

Result: 在固定 5% 价格影响上限下，直接、约束与少样本提示每种配置产生 14–19 次不安全的留出执行；来自报价的滑点约束无法阻止订单自身价格影响。75 条留出工作流提示上，混合变体静态安全代理得分 0.67，最佳基线仅 0.33，且未记录到不安全执行；禁用强制机制时消融出现 14–17 次不安全执行。默认注入有局限：宽松的既有阈值仍可授权不安全交易，另评估的策略上限在 36 例诊断网格上解决了该失效。

Conclusion: 结果支持显式交易保护与基于执行的评测，并强调应区分"声明的安全性"与一般性安全保证。

Abstract: A structurally valid DeFi workflow can still authorize a costly trade. We introduce DeFiFlowBench, a benchmark of 207 team-authored prompts for natural-language DeFi workflow synthesis. It measures graph coverage, configuration completeness, and declared safety predicates, then tests supported trade configurations on a local EVM. Direct, constrained, and few-shot prompting produce 14-19 unsafe held-out executions per configuration under a fixed 5% price-impact cap. A slippage bound derived from a quote does not prevent the price impact of the order itself. We propose Koan-Safe, which combines a prompt-only intent parser, a replaceable generator, and structural repair with default safety parameters. On 75 held-out workflow prompts, its hybrid variant scores 0.67 on the static safety proxy, compared with 0.33 for the best baseline. Koan-Safe records no unsafe executions on the saved benchmark outputs. A matched-candidate ablation produces 14-17 unsafe executions when enforcement is disabled. Additional tests expose the limits of default injection: permissive existing thresholds can still authorize unsafe trades. A separately evaluated policy cap addresses this failure on a 36-case diagnostic grid. These results support explicit trade protections and execution-based evaluation, while distinguishing declared safety from a general guarantee.

</details>


### [152] [Particle GFlowNets: Rethinking Generative Marginalization Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11538)
*Tiago da Silva,Diego Mesquita,Salem Lahlou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明生成边际模型（MaMs）与生成流网络（GFlowNets）等价，并将MaMs的采样策略扩展到非自回归生成过程，提出基于Gelman-Rubin统计量的全状态再生准则，形成Particle GFlowNets方法，在大型组合空间中显著加速训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前研究将MaMs视为与GFlowNets不同的方法，但二者在离散随机模型推断中的关系尚不明确；同时MaMs的采样策略局限于自回归过程，需要扩展到更一般的非自回归生成过程以提升学习效率。

Method: 首先从理论上证明MaMs与GFlowNets的等价性；进而将MaMs的采样策略推广到非自回归生成过程，并借助Gelman-Rubin统计量推导出Gibbs采样器全状态再生的自动判定准则，用于加速学习收敛。

Result: 实验表明，所提出的Particle GFlowNets方法在大型组合空间中显著加快了训练速度。

Conclusion: MaMs可统一到GFlowNets框架下，且基于Gelman-Rubin统计量的全状态再生准则能有效提升采样与学习的收敛效率，为非自回归生成建模提供了高效途径。

Abstract: Generative Marginalization Models (MaMs) have been recently introduced as efficient neural sampling models for any-order autoregressive modelling of discrete distributions. By learning both the marginal and conditional probabilities of a persistent-block Gibbs sampler, MaMs enable fast posterior evaluation with a single neural network forward pass. While prior work has considered MaMs to be distinct from Generative Flow Networks (GFlowNets), a well-established paradigm for inference in discrete stochastic models, we show that they are equivalent. Then, we also extend MaMs' sampling strategy to non-autoregressive generative processes. In particular, we describe an automatic criterion for full-state rejuvenation of the Gibbs sampler, derived from the Gelman-Rubin statistic, which plays a key role in speeding up learning convergence. Our experiments show that our method, called Particle GFlowNets, markedly accelerates training in large combinatorial spaces.

</details>


### [153] [A Dataset and Model for Imputing Water Surface Elevation on a Large and Extremely Sparse Spatiotemporal Graph](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11580)
*Ruben Cartuyvels,Karim Douch,Gabriele Bertoli,Mounia El Baz,Artemis Vrettou,Sébastien Lefèvre,Diego Fernandez Prieto*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出AmazonSWE数据集与一种双向选择性状态空间模型，用于亚马逊流域河网水位的大规模时空图插补。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球河流水位监测对洪水预报与水资源管理至关重要，但实地水文站稀缺，卫星测高又受时间覆盖稀疏所限，亟需可靠的插补方法。

Method: 构建AmazonSWE数据集，融合多源卫星测高（含SWOT宽刈幅）数据，覆盖亚马逊流域19K+河段、10年（2016-2026），并以实测站点做留出评估；采样连通子图，将时空展平为单一代币序列，结合拓扑感知位置编码，设计双向选择性状态空间模型进行插补。

Result: 现有时空图插补方法难以适配该拓扑、规模与稀疏度；所提模型相比已发表的SWOT水位加密SOTA方法，对实测站点的RMSE降低18–39%，且能对全部河段给出预测。

Conclusion: AmazonSWE提供了更稀疏、更大规模且拓扑有向无环的真实插补基准，验证了面向河网拓扑的双向选择性状态空间建模的有效性，兼具技术挑战与社会效益。

Abstract: Continuous monitoring of water surface elevation across river networks is critical for flood forecasting, water resource management, and understanding the global water cycle. Yet, the scarcity of in situ gauges across much of the globe constrains the development of reliable modeling frameworks. Satellite altimetry has the potential to alleviate this problem but its use is currently hindered by sparse temporal coverage. To this end, we introduce AmazonSWE, a dataset for training and evaluating large-scale spatiotemporal graph imputation methods that integrates processed satellite altimetry measurements from a range of sources, including the recent wide-swath SWOT sensor. The dataset covers over 19K river sections and 10 years (2016-2026) in the Amazon river basin, with in situ gauges held out for evaluation. Besides contributing a novel real-world use case with the potential for societal impact, AmazonSWE introduces significant technical challenges: with fewer than 1% of sections observed per day, the dataset is far sparser than existing imputation benchmarks, and its directed acyclic river topology is both structurally different from and larger than graphs in existing datasets. We show that prior spatiotemporal graph imputation methods are not adapted to this topology, scale and sparsity, and propose a simple bidirectional selective state space model that outperforms them by sampling connected subgraphs and flattening space and time into a single token sequence with topology-aware positional encodings. Compared to the state-of-the-art published method for SWOT-based WSE densification, which integrates statistics with physical modeling, our model reduces RMSE against in situ gauges by 18-39%, while producing predictions for every river section rather than only those with sufficient nearby satellite coverage.

</details>


### [154] [LoaDiff: Conditional Generation of Electricity Consumption Time Series for Energy Analytics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11639)
*Mariia Baranova,Adrien Petralia,Etienne Le Naour,Nathan Etourneau,Guillaume Hofmann,Themis Palpanas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 LoaDiff——一种基于扩散模型的生成方法，可合成全年、亚小时级智能电表负荷曲线，并支持对静态家庭属性与动态上下文变量（日历、气温）的条件控制，在三套住宅用电数据集上验证了其在保真度、多样性、记忆风险、下游任务效用与条件可控性方面的综合表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 能源转型推动了分布式发电、电气化家电与需求响应项目的普及，住宅用电行为日益复杂。负荷预测、家电识别、需求侧灵活性分析等应用需要细粒度智能电表数据，但此类数据受严格访问限制与数据保护法规约束，因此需要真实感强的合成数据替代方案。

Method: 提出 LoaDiff，一种扩散式生成模型，面向全年、亚小时级负荷曲线生成；支持对静态家庭属性（如家电持有情况）与动态上下文变量（日历信息、室外温度）的灵活条件控制。在三套住宅用电数据集上与多种生成基线进行对比。

Result: 实验从四个互补维度评估：保真度与多样性、训练记录记忆风险、负荷预测与家电识别的下游效用、替代温度条件下的条件可控性。结果显示 LoaDiff 能生成真实且多样的负荷曲线，在生成质量与有限的记忆证据之间取得良好折中，保留了对下游能源应用有用的信息，并对条件变量变化作出连贯响应。

Conclusion: LoaDiff 可作为受访问限制的智能电表数据的现实合成替代，兼顾生成质量、隐私风险与下游实用性，并具备良好的条件可控性。

Abstract: The energy transition is reshaping residential electricity consumption through the increasing adoption of distributed generation, electrified appliances, and demand-response programs. Understanding these evolving behaviors requires access to granular smart-meter data for applications such as load forecasting, appliance detection, and demand-side flexibility analysis. However, such data are subject to strict access restrictions and data-protection regulations. Thus, realistic synthetic alternatives are necessary. In this paper, we introduce LoaDiff, a diffusion-based generative model for year-long, sub-hourly smart-meter load curves. LoaDiff supports flexible conditioning on static household attributes, such as appliance ownership, and dynamic contextual variables, including calendar information and outdoor temperature. We evaluate the model against multiple generative baselines on three residential electricity-consumption datasets. Our experiments assess four complementary dimensions: fidelity and diversity, training-record memorization risk, downstream utility for load forecasting and appliance detection, and conditional controllability under alternative temperature conditions. The results show that LoaDiff generates realistic and diverse load profiles, achieves a favorable trade-off between generation quality and limited evidence of memorization, preserves information useful for downstream energy applications, and responds coherently to changes in conditioning variables.

</details>


### [155] [Musec: MomentUm SpEctral Clipping for Stable Muon-type Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11655)
*Zhuanghua Liu,Menglian Wang,Luo Luo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Musec与Soft Musec，用谱裁剪替代Muon的谱展平，从优化器层面解决训练不稳定问题，并首次给出Muon类方法在非凸非光滑随机优化下的收敛保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Muon在大型语言模型训练中相比Adam/AdamW具有更好的收敛与性能，但其谱展平（将所有奇异值置为约1）会导致损失尖峰和权重无界增长等训练不稳定现象。现有方法依赖权重或注意力logit裁剪，需针对特定架构修改，且无法直接解决所有模型组件的不稳定性。

Method: 提出MomentUm SpEctral Clipping（Musec）：不再将动量矩阵的所有奇异值设为约1，而是仅裁剪超过阈值的奇异值，保留动量的底层谱结构，从而提供一种优化器层面的、架构无关的稳定机制。进一步提出Soft Musec，利用由耦合Newton-Schulz迭代逼近的光滑谱饱和函数实现高效计算。

Result: 理论上，建立了Musec在非凸非光滑随机优化中的收敛保证，这是Muon类方法在该设定下的首个收敛性结果。实验上，Soft Musec在广泛学习率和模型规模下持续优于现有Muon变体的训练稳定性；在现有变体发散的配置中仍保持稳定，同时在良好调参配置下性能相当。

Conclusion: Musec/Soft Musec以优化器层面的谱裁剪实现架构无关的Muon稳定化，兼具理论收敛保证与实证稳定性优势，可作为Muon的稳健替代方案。

Abstract: Muon has emerged as a highly effective optimizer for large language model training, often achieving superior convergence and performance compared with the widely adopted Adam and AdamW optimizers. Nevertheless, Muon is prone to training instability due to its spectral flattening, manifested by loss spikes and unbounded growth of model weights. Existing approaches primarily rely on weight or attention-logit clipping, which require architecture-specific modifications and do not directly address instability across all model components. We propose MomentUm SpEctral Clipping (Musec), which replaces Muon's spectral flattening with spectral clipping: rather than setting all singular values of the momentum matrix to approximately one, Musec clips singular values that exceed a threshold while preserving the underlying spectral structure of the momentum. Our strategy provides an optimizer-level, architecture-agnostic mechanism for stabilizing Muon training. We further develop Soft Musec, an efficient implementation that uses a smooth spectral saturation function approximated by coupled Newton-Schulz iterations. Theoretically, we establish convergence guarantees for Musec in nonconvex nonsmooth stochastic optimization. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first convergence guarantee for Muon-type methods in the nonconvex nonsmooth setting. We provide empirical studies to show that Soft Musec consistently improves training stability over existing Muon variants across a wide range of learning rates and model sizes. Notably, Soft Musec remains stable in settings where existing Muon variants diverge, while matching their performance under well-tuned configurations.

</details>


### [156] [Learnware and AI Model Management System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11656)
*Zhi-Hua Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出从"AI模型存储"升级到"AI模型管理系统"的范式转变，通过将管理单元由模型升级为"学件（Learnware = 模型 + 规约）"，在无需访问开发者训练数据和用户原始数据的前提下实现模型的识别、复用与组装，并给出学件港系统（LDS）这一实现路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 类比文件存储到数据库管理系统的演进，当前模型池仅相当于"AI模型存储系统"，缺乏管理能力。现实需求是让不同开发者、不同任务、不同数据、不同目标下训练的模型可被识别、复用甚至组装以应对未来任务。但开发者不愿共享训练数据，且系统最好也不访问用户原始数据，这带来根本挑战：连模型的训练者都未必完全理解模型功能，系统如何判断哪些模型对给定任务有用并组装它们。

Method: 将管理的基本单元从机器学习模型升级为"学件"：学件 = 模型 + 规约。规约由机器学习过程生成，生成时不泄露开发者训练数据，并具有理论上有保证的数据保持（data-preservation）性质；该赋值过程把训练好的模型转变为学件。由于规约依据公开的参考标准生成、在模型间具有可比性，因此还可充当AI模型的"协作协议"。在此基础上构建学件港系统（LDS）作为通向AI模型管理系统的实现路径。

Result: 提出的学件与LDS框架使原本"看似不可实现"的目标变得可行：在保护开发者训练数据和用户原始数据的同时，支持模型的识别、复用与组装；规约的可比性使独立开发的模型乃至智能体能够协作。

Conclusion: AI领域正经历类似从文件存储到数据库管理的转变，学件（模型+规约）作为新的管理单元与协作协议，配合学件港系统，为实现强大的AI模型管理系统提供了可行路径。

Abstract: The transition from file storage to database management systems transformed stored data into managed resources. AI now faces an analogous transition from AI model storage to AI model management. Existing model pools essentially serve as \textit{AI model storage systems}. What is needed instead are \textit{AI model management systems} that enable models trained by different developers, for different tasks, with different data, and under different objectives to be identified, reused, and even assembled to address future user tasks. Because AI model developers are generally unwilling to share their training data, such systems should operate without accessing the training data of model developers and, ideally, without accessing raw data of future users. This requirement poses a fundamental challenge: the functionality of a modern AI model may not be fully understood even by the developer who trained it. How, then, can a system identify which models are useful for a given user task, let alone assemble models developed independently for different purposes? At first glance, this objective may appear unattainable. It becomes possible, however, by upgrading the basic unit of management from a machine learning model to a \textit{learnware}. \textit{Learnware = Model + Specification}. The specification, whose assignment transforms a trained model into a learnware, is generated with the help of a machine learning process without disclosing the training data of the developer and has a theoretically established data-preservation property. The \textit{Learnware Dock System (LDS)} provides a path toward powerful AI model management systems. Because specifications are generated according to a published reference and are comparable across models, they can also serve as an AI model \textit{collaboration protocol} through which independently developed models, including intelligent agents, can collaborate.

</details>


### [157] [Why Does Post-Training Quantization Work?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11716)
*Yuxiang Chen,Michael Beyer,Jun Zhu,Jianfei Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究训练后量化（PTQ）为何有效：通过对比全精度与前向量化，发现预训练模型具备两种鲁棒机制——层间误差的"反相抵消"与LM-head几何对高排名token的优先保持，二者共同抑制隐藏状态误差的累积。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量化权重会给隐藏状态引入误差，理论上误差应随深度累积并破坏下一token预测（随机初始化模型确实迅速累积误差）。但量化后的预训练模型隐藏状态误差累积小、下游任务性能基本保持，尽管它从未在量化噪声下训练过。这一反差引出核心问题：训练后量化为何有效？

Method: 对比全精度与量化前向传播，逐层追踪误差，识别出预训练量化鲁棒性的两种机制：（1）层新引入的误差与来自输入的继承误差方向相反，二者部分抵消，使全精度与量化传播间的偏差增长缓慢——该"抵消式残差交互"在预训练中形成，并通过定量分析确认其为减缓隐藏误差增长的主要因素；（2）LM-head几何结构优先保留高排名token的分数与概率，而这些通常是模型最有信心的预测。研究在多个模型与量化设置上验证。

Result: 定量分析表明，"抵消式残差交互"是延缓隐藏误差增长的主要因素；LM-head几何结构偏好保持高排名token的分数与概率。两种机制共同作用，解释了经过多层传播的量化误差为何只产生很小的输出变化。结论在多个模型与量化设置下均得到验证。

Conclusion: 训练后量化鲁棒性并非偶然，而是源于预训练过程中形成的两个机制：层间误差部分抵消，以及LM-head对高置信度token的几何保护。二者使深层累积的量化误差仍只带来微小的输出变化，为理解PTQ有效性提供了机理解释。

Abstract: Post-training quantization compresses large language models (LLMs) by storing their weights at reduced precision, and each quantized weight introduces an error into the hidden states. Naively, these errors should accumulate with depth and corrupt next-token prediction; randomly initialized models accumulate these discrepancies rapidly, whereas quantized pretrained models accumulate much less hidden-state error and largely maintain downstream task performance, even though they were never trained with quantization noise. This raises the question we address: why does post-training quantization work? Comparing full-precision and quantized forward passes, we identify two mechanisms that characterize pretrained quantization robustness. First, the error a layer newly introduces tends to oppose the error it inherits from the layer's input. The two cancel partially such that the discrepancy between full-precision and quantized passes grows slowly. This counteracting residual interaction develops during pretraining. Our quantitative analysis identifies it as a major factor slowing hidden-error growth. Second, LM-head geometry preferentially preserves the scores and probabilities of high-ranked tokens, which typically represent the model's most confident predictions. Together, these mechanisms explain why quantization error that passes through numerous layers can still produce only small output changes, and we verify the findings across models and quantization settings.

</details>


### [158] [Predicting Privacy Leakage from Weight Spectral Density](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11780)
*Richard J. Preen,Jim Smith*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文探索是否可用WeightWatcher谱度量作为成员推理攻击(MIA)风险的廉价代理指标，发现stable rank与MIA成功率强正相关，Log alpha-Norm与低假阳性区MIA脆弱性稳定负相关，且相关性优于泛化间隙。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前最先进的成员推理攻击需要训练计算昂贵的影子模型，使大规模隐私评估不切实际，因此需要廉价且可扩展的MIA脆弱性代理指标。

Method: 基于重尾自正则化框架，在图像与表格分类任务上评估多个WeightWatcher谱度量，并将其与MIA隐私泄露的关系同传统泛化度量（泛化间隙）进行对比。

Result: 跨数据集上，stable rank与整体MIA成功率呈强正相关；Log alpha-Norm在低假阳性区间与MIA脆弱性呈一致负相关；这些关联强于使用泛化间隙所得结果。

Conclusion: 神经网络谱可能包含传统过拟合度量未能完全刻画的隐私泄露信息，谱分析有望成为可扩展隐私审计的有前景方向。

Abstract: Membership inference attacks (MIAs) are widely used to audit the privacy disclosure risk of machine learning models, however current state-of-the-art attacks require training computationally expensive shadow models, making large-scale privacy evaluation impractical. In this work, we investigate whether inexpensive spectral metrics derived from the heavy-tailed self-regularisation framework can serve as proxies for MIA vulnerability. We evaluate several WeightWatcher spectral metrics on image and tabular classification tasks and compare their relationship with MIA privacy leakage against conventional measures of generalisation. Across datasets, stable rank exhibits a strong positive correlation with overall MIA success, while Log alpha-Norm shows a consistent negative correlation with MIA vulnerability at the low false-positive regime. These associations are observed to be stronger than those obtained using the generalisation gap. The results indicate that neural network spectra may contain information about privacy leakage that is not fully captured by conventional measures of overfitting, motivating spectral analysis as a promising direction for scalable privacy auditing.

</details>


### [159] [Dynamic language model representations for multi-objective reaction optimisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11790)
*Joshua W. Sin,David Ming Segura,Bojana Ranković,Siu Lun Chau,Marius D. R. Lutz,Andrea Anelli,Ryan P. Burwood,Kurt Püntener,Maximilian J. Notheis,Raphael Bigler,Philippe Schwaller*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该工作提出用文本描述反应条件，通过微调语言模型与高斯过程代理联合训练，动态学习任务自适应的反应表征，用于多目标贝叶斯优化，在钯/镍催化交叉偶联与不对称氢化中显著减少实验次数并实现克级放大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多目标（收率、选择性、安全性）化学反应优化依赖反应组分的表征方式。现有featurisation存在缺陷：one-hot编码缺乏化学信息，分子描述符难以跨化学上不同组分迁移。对于结构与功能多样的组分，共享表征应包含什么并不清楚，且为每个新反应体系重新构建表征本身是繁重的科研任务。

Method: 绕过人工构建表征，直接从文本动态学习反应表征：将反应条件的文本描述用微调语言模型编码，该模型与高斯过程代理模型联合训练，在多目标贝叶斯优化循环中产生任务自适应表征。在镍和钯催化的交叉偶联、顺序与并行实验条件下与描述符库和one-hot编码对比。

Result: 相比描述符库或one-hot编码，该方法以更少实验达到优化收敛。前瞻性应用于涉及混合配体齿合度与多相添加剂的钯催化氰化，以及跨手性铱/钌催化剂家族的三目标不对称氢化：两轮高通量实验（192个反应，不足各设计空间的3%）得到的条件可直接放大至克级，分离收率分别为94%和84%，后者对映体过量达99.6%。

Conclusion: 从文本动态学习任务自适应反应表征可有效支持多目标贝叶斯优化，无需为每个新反应体系重新设计共享表征，并在多样催化体系与多目标场景中实现高效实验收敛与直接克级放大。

Abstract: Optimising chemical reactions across multiple objectives, such as yield, selectivity, and safety, is central to chemical synthesis, and model-driven approaches depend critically on how reaction components are represented. Established featurisations are either chemically uninformative, as with one-hot encodings, or, as with molecular descriptors, do not readily extend across chemically distinct components. For structurally and functionally diverse components, it is therefore unclear what a shared representation should contain. Constructing such a representation is itself a challenging research undertaking that must be revisited for each new reaction system.
