Here we bypass this step by learning the reaction representation dynamically from text. Textual descriptions of reaction conditions are encoded by a fine-tuned language model trained jointly with Gaussian process surrogates, yielding task-adaptive representations within a multi-objective Bayesian optimisation loop. Across nickel- and palladium-catalysed cross-couplings in both sequential and parallel experimentation regimes, this approach reaches optimisation convergence in fewer experiments than descriptor libraries or one-hot encoding. Applied prospectively to a palladium-catalysed cyanation spanning mixed ligand denticity and heterogeneous additives, and to a three-objective asymmetric hydrogenation across chiral iridium and ruthenium catalyst families, two rounds of high-throughput experimentation (192 reactions, under 3% of each design space) delivered conditions translating directly to gram scale in 94% and 84% isolated yield, the latter at 99.6% enantiomeric excess.

</details>


### [160] [Thinking with Looped Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11801)
*Ayhan Suleymanzade,Chanhyuk Lee,Floor Eijkelboom,Nicholas M. Boffi,İsmail İlkan Ceylan,Jinwoo Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出"looped flows"，用局部去噪目标训练循环模型的递归更新，使梯度仅覆盖少数步时仍能学到可跨时间传递计算的循环状态，并将推理形式化为对概率流速度的积分，通过更细时间网格增加计算量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类和机器常通过投入更多计算时间来解决更难的问题。深度学习中，looped模型在推理时通过循环更新隐状态来实现这一点，但实际训练时反向传播只经过一次或少数几次更新，导致早期的更新难以被训练以支持后续更新，这是限制循环模型性能的核心训练难题。

Method: 提出looped flows：用局部去噪目标训练递归过程，规避长程反向传播问题。通过逐步降低的噪声水平和共享噪声，在去噪目标之间建立时间关联，激励模型学到能在时间上传递有用计算的循环状态，即便梯度只覆盖少数几次更新。推理阶段将推断形式化为对由所学去噪器参数化的概率流速度的积分，并与循环状态耦合，从而可通过更细的时间网格投入更多计算，也可从不同初始噪声样本得到多种有效预测。

Result: 在六个推理基准（含两个多解基准）上，looped flows整体优于此前最先进的looped模型，在ARC-AGI-1上达到58.8%测试准确率，在ARC-AGI-2上达到12.2%。

Conclusion: 以局部去噪目标替代长程反向传播来训练循环递归，并配合概率流积分式推理，是一条有效提升循环模型在困难推理任务上表现、并支持多解生成的可行路径。

Abstract: Humans and machines often solve harder problems by spending more time on computation. In deep learning, looped models implement this idea during inference by recurrently updating a hidden state. In practice, however, their training backpropagates through only one or a few updates, making it hard to train early updates to support future ones. We propose looped flows, an approach that sidesteps this issue by training the recurrence with local denoising objectives. By imposing temporal association across denoising objectives through progressively decreasing noise levels and shared noise, the model is incentivized to learn recurrent states that transfer useful computation over time, even when gradients cover only a few updates. We then formulate inference as integrating the velocity of a probability flow parameterized by the learned denoiser, coupled with recurrent states. This allows solving harder problems by spending more computation through a finer temporal grid and enables multiple valid predictions from different initial noise samples. Across six reasoning benchmarks including two multi-solution benchmarks, looped flows outperform prior state-of-the-art looped models overall, achieving 58.8% test accuracy on ARC-AGI-1 and 12.2% on ARC-AGI-2.

</details>


### [161] [Model-Aware Schedules Improve Generation via Fiberwise Optimal Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11842)
*Luyi Jia,Boyan Zhang,Yilun Liu,Steffen Rulands*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于fiberwise optimal transport的模型感知调度构建方法，在系数曲线上结合fiberwise预测风险与系数路径动能作用，得到闭式最优时间分配，显著超越模型无关基线，并揭示归一化风险剖面的经验普适性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散与flow matching调度通过最小化系数路径上的动能作用（受最优传输启发）来解释强基线，但该方法与模型无关，忽略了预测误差，因此无法反映具体模型的预测特性。

Method: 在概率路径上固定时间与状态时，兼容的信号/噪声分解构成仿射fiber；定义fiberwise预测风险为fiber内真实分解与预测器诱导分解之间最优传输代价的平均。将该风险与系数路径动能作用结合，在固定系数曲线上得到闭式最优时间分配。方法可推广到一般线性预测目标，风险剖面可由早期基线checkpoint估计。

Result: 在DDPM与flow matching上跨预测目标、训练配置、风险估计checkpoint、数据集与架构评估，模型感知调度持续优于强基线，CIFAR-10上flow matching在16次函数评估下FID相对降低38.6%。各模型无关动能基线确定自身动能参考坐标，归一化到单位面积后，不同设置下独立训练模型的fiberwise风险剖面高度一致，训练所用调度形变也一致。预训练checkpoint诊断将归一化风险一致性扩展到更大的条件潜扩散与2-RF模型。

Conclusion: fiberwise风险在动能参考坐标下呈现跨模型与设置的经验普适性；冻结的解析分配模板无需进一步风险估计或模型特定拟合即可保留大部分模型感知改进。

Abstract: Diffusion and flow-matching schedules control the signal and noise coefficients that mix data and noise along affine probability paths. Minimizing a kinetic action defined on coefficient paths, motivated by optimal transport, helps explain strong baselines but remains model-agnostic and ignores prediction error. Here we introduce a model-aware schedule construction based on fiberwise optimal transport. At a fixed time and state on the probability path, compatible signal/noise decompositions form an affine fiber. We define a fiberwise prediction risk by averaging optimal-transport costs between the true and predictor-induced decompositions within these fibers. On a fixed coefficient curve, combining this risk with coefficient-path kinetic action yields a closed-form optimal time allocation. This construction extends to general linear prediction targets, and the risk profile can be estimated from an early baseline checkpoint. We evaluate DDPMs and flow matching across prediction targets, training configurations, risk-estimation checkpoints, datasets, and architectures. Our model-aware schedules consistently outperform strong baselines, including a 38.6% relative FID reduction for flow matching on CIFAR-10 at 16 function evaluations. Each model-agnostic kinetic baseline determines its own kinetic reference coordinate. In these coordinates, fiberwise-risk profiles from independently trained models in different settings align closely after normalization to unit area. The resulting schedule deformations used in training also align, suggesting empirical universality across the evaluated models and settings. Pretrained-checkpoint diagnostics extend this normalized-risk agreement to larger conditional latent diffusion and 2-RF models. A frozen analytic allocation template retains most of the model-aware improvement without further risk estimation or model-specific fitting.

</details>


### [162] [The Last AI Built by Humans: Toward Genuine Recursive Self-Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11873)
*Yi Duan,Ying Liu,Zirui Tang,Haodong Chen,Jun Zhou,Yumou Liu,Bangrui Xu,Yukai Wu,Sidi Chen,Yuhan Zhou,Haoyu Wang,Xiaoyou Yu,Shaokun Han,Xuzhou Zhu,Le Zhou,Bolin Lu,Wei Zhou,Jiachen Liu,Nuozhou Fang,Jiaxin Tian,Ruoyu Chen,Yuxuan Li,Kai Zuo,Kaiyan Zhang,Jiantao Qiu,Conghui He,Guoliang Li,Bowen Zhou,Zhiyuan Liu,Zhoufutu Wen,Jihua Kang,Xuanhe Zhou,Fan Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出递归自我改进（RSI）概念及发展路线图，揭示现有LLM的局限，并探讨其多场景应用与关键挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM存在能力提升受限的问题，需要让AI系统将经验与反馈转化为持久性改进，从而实现能力与改进过程本身的双重提升。

Method: 首先使用Headroom-Closed Index（HCI）揭示现有LLM的问题，随后引入RSI概念并构建发展路线图：从改进-执行自主、改进-策略自主、经验获取自主、环境适应自主，到递归元改进；并结合科学发现、具身智能、软件工程等场景进行分析。

Result: 识别出不同应用场景对RSI的差异化需求与发展速度，并基于多样化的产业实践与初步实证证据，将RSI研究与实际系统相连接。

Conclusion: 指出实现真正RSI所面临的关键挑战，为后续研究与系统落地提供方向。

Abstract: Recursive self-improvement (RSI) enables AI systems to turn experience and feedback into persistent changes that improve both their capabilities and the process of future improvement. We first use the Headroom-Closed Index (HCI) to reveal the problems of existing LLMs, then introduce the RSI concept and its development roadmap: from improvement-execution autonomy, improvement-strategy autonomy, experience-acquisition autonomy, and environment-adaptation autonomy, to recursive meta-improvement. Next we examine RSI across scenarios (e.g., scientific discovery, embodied intelligence, software engineering), highlighting their distinct requirements and development speeds. Drawing on diverse industry practices and preliminary empirical evidence, we connect RSI research with practical systems and identify key challenges to achieving genuine RSI.

</details>


### [163] [CoRA-NAS: Coarse Ranking and Anchor-Residual Refinement for Neural Architecture Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11884)
*Yifan Yang,Zhaoyan Wang,Zheng Gao,Xiaoyu Li,Jiaojiao Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CoRA-NAS两阶段框架，将零成本静态代理排序与低成本学习曲线精炼结合，实现跨搜索空间的鲁棒架构排序与选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零成本代理（zero-cost proxies）虽能廉价地对架构排序，但其可靠性在不同搜索空间之间差异较大，缺乏跨空间稳定性，难以直接用于架构选择。

Method: CoRA-NAS分两阶段：CoRA-Rank聚合容量类与初始化结构类代理，通过等权对数秩共识（equal-weight log-rank consensus）与无目标共识门控（target-free consensus gate）形成静态排序先验；CoRA-Refine在该先验上采样锚点，外推其早期验证曲线，并用ExtraTrees模型传播学习到的残差修正。精炼成本约为完整训练候选集的1%，且不使用完整训练得到的架构-精度标签拟合排序器。全程采用统一配置，仅使用空间特定的架构编码。

Result: 在NAS-Bench-201、NAS-Bench-101、TransNAS-Bench-101与NATS-SSS上，CoRA-Refine的平均Spearman相关系数分别为0.946、0.715、0.786、0.894；最差空间相关系数0.715为对比方法中最高。在NAS-Bench-201/CIFAR-100上，所选架构精度达73.32%，接近已报告的真值最优73.37%。在纯尺寸空间上，精炼弥补了静态先验相对参数量的差距，同时与最强容量代理在噪声范围内持平。

Conclusion: CoRA-NAS将跨空间排序鲁棒性与低成本架构选择相结合，验证了静态先验加锚点残差精炼的有效性。

Abstract: Zero-cost proxies rank architectures cheaply, but their reliability varies across search spaces. We introduce CoRA-NAS (COarse Ranking + Anchor-residual), a two-stage framework combining a static ranking prior with low-cost learning-curve refinement. CoRA-Rank aggregates capacity and structure-at-initialization proxies through an equal-weight log-rank consensus and a target-free consensus gate. CoRA-Refine samples anchors across this prior, extrapolates their early validation curves, and propagates a learned residual correction with an ExtraTrees model. The refinement uses approximately 1% of the cost of fully training the candidate set. Fully trained architecture-accuracy labels are not used to fit the ranker. One configuration is used across spaces, with space-specific architecture encodings. Across NAS-Bench-201, NAS-Bench-101, TransNAS-Bench-101, and NATS-SSS, CoRA-Refine achieves mean Spearman correlations of 0.946, 0.715, 0.786, and 0.894, respectively. Its worst-space correlation of 0.715 is the highest among the compared methods. On NAS-Bench-201/CIFAR-100, its selected architecture reaches 73.32% accuracy, near the reported ground-truth best of 73.37%. On the pure size space, refinement recovers the static prior's shortfall relative to parameter count, while remaining tied with the strongest capacity proxies within noise. The resulting framework combines cross-space ranking robustness with low-cost architecture selection.

</details>


### [164] [CausalArena: Benchmarking Causal Discovery in the Foundation Model Era](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11897)
*Zi-Rong Li,Si-Yang Liu,Tian-Zuo Wang,Han-Jia Ye*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 CausalArena——一个统一且可演化的因果发现基准，通过整合合成SCM、语义操作SCM、公式化SCM及真实数据，揭示现有评测在结构家族、机制与评估协议上的不一致，并强调预训练-评测重叠问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 因果发现的评测高度依赖结构因果模型（SCM），但既有研究在图家族、生成机制和评测协议上差异较大，结果难以横向比较；此外，因果发现基础模型（CDFM）的出现使评测更复杂——其性能可能源自预训练环境与测试SCM的重叠，而非真正的因果发现能力，使得固定合成基准上的结果难以解释。

Method: 提出 CausalArena 统一基准，采用统一协议，包含四类SCM：合成SCM（在结构与机制上提供可控广度）、语义操作SCM（提供可人工审查、语义接地的环境）、公式化SCM（在显式科学机制下测试发现能力），并以公开真实数据集作为外部效度检验。在经典方法、神经网络方法和预训练方法上进行实验。

Result: 实验显示各类方法在不同SCM家族与协议下出现显著的排名变化，说明在某一基准情形下的优异表现并不能可靠迁移到其他情形。

Conclusion: 基准多样性与预训练-评测重叠是基础模型时代评估因果发现的核心挑战，CausalArena 为统一、可演化的评测提供了框架。

Abstract: Causal discovery aims to uncover causal structures from data and is fundamental to scientific reasoning and intervention-based decision making. Its evaluation relies heavily on structural causal models (SCMs), which specify a causal graph together with the mechanisms that generate data, yet existing studies differ substantially in graph families, mechanisms, and evaluation protocols. The emergence of causal discovery foundation models (CDFMs) further complicates evaluation: performance may reflect not only causal discovery ability, but also overlap between pretraining environments and test SCMs, making results on fixed synthetic benchmarks difficult to interpret. We introduce CausalArena, a unified and evolvable benchmark for causal discovery under a common protocol. Synthetic SCMs supply controlled breadth over structures and mechanisms; semantic operational SCMs provide human-auditable, semantically grounded environments beyond standard synthetic generators; and formula-grounded SCMs test discovery under explicit scientific mechanisms. Public real-world datasets provide an additional external-validity check. Experiments across classical, neural, and pretrained methods reveal substantial ranking shifts across SCM families and protocols, showing that strong performance in one benchmark regime does not reliably transfer to others. These results highlight benchmark diversity and pretraining--evaluation overlap as central challenges for evaluating causal discovery in the foundation model era.

</details>


### [165] [TART: A Modular Tool for Technique-Aware Audio-to-Tablature Guitar Transcription](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11904)
*Akshaj Gupta,Hwi Joo Park,Andrea Guzman,Shamak Gowda,Samhita Konduri,Jiachen Lian,Robin Netzorg,Gopala Anumanchipalli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TART：模块化四阶段音频到吉他谱流水线，零样本下在四项基准上提升音频转MIDI及弦品分配性能，并首次实现带指法与演奏技法标注的吉他谱生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有吉他自动音乐转录（AMT）存在三方面不足：(1) 难以捕捉滑音、推弦、打板等表现性技法；(2) 常将音符错误地分配到弦-品组合；(3) 多基于干净录音训练，泛化到噪声真实音频的能力受限。

Method: 提出TART，模块化四阶段音频到六线谱（tablature）流水线：(1) 音频到MIDI转录模型；(2) 表现性技法分类器；(3) 音频条件化的T5编码器-解码器用于弦-品分配；(4) 自动六线谱生成器。在GuitarSet、EGDB及增强基准Noisy GuitarSet、Noisy EGDB上以零样本方式评估。

Result: 四项基准平均：音频到MIDI F50为81.35%（较最佳基线+6.67）；弦-品Tab F1为71.8%（较最佳基线+8.5）；端到端Tab F1为54.08%。

Conclusion: TART是首个直接从吉他音频生成同时包含指法与表现性技法标注的吉他谱框架。

Abstract: Automatic Music Transcription (AMT) for guitar remains limited by three challenges: existing systems often fail to capture expressive techniques such as slides, bends, and percussive hits; they often assign notes to incorrect string-fret combinations; and they are typically trained on clean recordings, limiting their generalization to noisy real-world audio. To address these challenges, we propose TART, a modular four-stage audio-to-tablature pipeline consisting of (1) an audio-to-MIDI transcription model, (2) an expressive technique classifier, (3) an audio-conditioned T5 encoder-decoder for string-fret assignment, and (4) an automated tablature generator. We evaluate TART in a zero-shot setting on GuitarSet, EGDB, and two augmented benchmarks, Noisy GuitarSet and Noisy EGDB. Averaged across these four benchmarks, TART achieves 81.35% audio-to-MIDI F50 (+6.67 points over the best prior baseline), 71.8% string-fret Tab F1 (+8.5 points over the best prior baseline), and 54.08% end-to-end Tab F1. To our knowledge, TART is the first framework to generate guitar tablature with both fingering and expressive technique annotations directly from guitar audio.

</details>


### [166] [From Protocols to Evidence: Bounded Claims for AI in Service of the Common Good](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11910)
*Nitesh V. Chawla,Paulo Benanti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"断裂测试"(rupture test)，将制度基线纳入AI系统评估，区分"证据受限部署"与"测量受限治理"，并以RISE AI架构在责任、包容、安全、赋能四方面提出有界、循证的论断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI不仅是治理问题，更会暴露制度在响应性、归属感、关怀与问责方面的既有失败；部署后的AI会修复、加剧、替代或掩盖这些失败。因此负责任AI必须同时评估系统与其所嵌入的制度断裂，并厘清现有协议（EU AI Act、NIST AI RMF、ISO/IEC 42001及各类保障实践）究竟确立了何种规范、未触动何种权力、测量应在何处止步。

Method: 以教宗良十四世《Magnifica Humanitas》所强调的尊严、技术权力与共同善为道德框架，构建"断裂测试"，将制度基线与系统评估相连接；提出"证据受限部署"（将论断限于实际被评估的范围）与"测量受限治理"（记录有利证据亦无法推翻的约束）两个概念区分；并在此界限内设计RISE AI架构，用于对责任(Responsibility)、包容(Inclusivity)、安全(Safety)、赋能(Empowerment)作出有界且循证的论断。

Result: 形成了一套可操作的分析工具：断裂测试用于定位制度断裂并建立基线；两类"受限"概念为部署论断与治理边界提供判定标准；RISE AI提供了在明确边界内作出证据化论断的架构。

Conclusion: 负责任AI不能仅靠原则或工程优化，而需三重努力并举：更好的工程、制度修复，以及持续的道德与政治判断。协议化进程虽已推进，但关键在于认清其实际确立之物、未被触及的权力结构，以及测量的限度。

Abstract: Artificial Intelligence does more than create a governance problem. It can also reveal where institutions have already failed to provide responsiveness, belonging, care, and accountability. Once deployed, AI becomes an intervention in those conditions. It can repair, compound, substitute for, or conceal the failures it encounters. Responsible AI must therefore evaluate both the system and the institutional rupture into which it is introduced. The move from principles to protocols is already underway. The EU AI Act, NIST AI RMF, ISO/IEC 42001, and assurance practices translate commitments into roles, requirements, records, oversight, and assessment. The harder questions are what these protocols actually establish, whose power they leave untouched, and where measurement must stop. Pope Leo XIV's Magnifica Humanitas provides a broader moral frame centered on dignity, technological power, and the common good. Drawing on that frame, we develop a rupture test that links institutional baselines to system evaluation. We distinguish evidence-bounded deployment, which limits claims to what has actually been evaluated, from measurement-bounded governance, which records constraints that favorable evidence cannot override. Within those limits, RISE AI provides an architecture for making bounded, evidence-based claims about Responsibility, Inclusivity, Safety, and Empowerment. Responsible AI requires better engineering, institutional repair, and continued moral and political judgment.

</details>


### [167] [Data Scarcity and Model Sparsity: Mixtures-of-Experts Overfit More to Repeated Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11917)
*Atindra Jha,Margaret Li,Jure Leskovec,Percy Liang,Luke Zettlemoyer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究系统考察了MoE稀疏架构在数据重复训练下的表现，发现MoE比稠密模型更易过拟合重复数据，且该效应随稀疏度增强，并探讨了缓解方法及内在机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类书写文本数据正被耗尽，重复训练数据已成常态。已有工作研究了稠密Transformer的数据重复效应，但对当前主流的MoE等稀疏架构在数据重复下的影响尚不清楚，尤其考虑到其计算效率优势。

Method: 在单域与多域数据混合、不同重复率下，跨MoE设置（专家数量、粒度）进行实验；模型规模从80M到1B激活参数（8.5B总参数）；测试dropout等现有正则化方法的缓解效果；并分析MoE过拟合的内在机制。

Result: MoE在数据重复下退化更快，效应随稀疏度增强，由总参数而非激活参数决定。80M稠密模型可重复8x以上而退化极小，MoE在4x即开始受损，32x后性能反低于稠密模型。强掩码正则化下MoE在64x以上重复仍可超越稠密模型，但无一方法能匹敌全唯一数据训练。MoE路由在训练早期即稳定，专家特化与重复数据过拟合相关。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了稀疏性与数据重复的负面交互，阐明了过拟合的核心机制及潜在补救措施，并为通过打破记忆模式来减少参数过度特化指明了未来方法方向。

Abstract: As the supply of human-written text is exhausted, it has become standard practice to repeat language model training data. Prior work has studied data repetition for densely activated Transformers, but the effects of data repetition remains largely unexplored for recently dominant sparse architectures such as Mixture-of-Experts (MoE), despite their increased compute efficiency. We vary data repetition rates across single- and multi-domain data mixes, and across MoE settings, including expert count and granularity. We consistently find, for models ranging from 80M to 1B active (8.5B total) parameters, that MoEs degrade more rapidly under data repetition. This effect increases with sparsity, dictated by total rather than active parameters. While 80M dense models can repeat data over 8x with minimal degradation, MoEs instead begin to suffer at 4x, and deteriorate rapidly, ceding their performance benefits in all-unique data settings to underperform dense models after 32x. We experiment with existing regularization methods as a potential remedy. We find that some methods, such as dropout, can mitigate overfitting. In particular, with strong masking-based regularization, MoEs are able to outperform dense models even when data is repeated more than 64 times. However, no method fully matches the performance of all-unique training data. Finally, we analyze internal mechanisms correlated with MoE overfitting in high repetition regimes, and find that MoE routing universally stabilizes early in training, and that expert specialization correlates with overfitting to repeated data. In sum, our work addresses the adverse interactions between sparsity and data repetition: we present evidence for the core mechanisms of overfitting and its potential remediation, and suggest promising avenues for future methods to reduce over-specialization in model parameters by disrupting memorization patterns.

</details>


<div id='nlin.AO'></div>

# nlin.AO [[Back]](#toc)

### [168] [Reconstructing the information processing capacity of physical systems from noisy observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11268)
*Shun Kotoku,Rodrigo Martínez-Peña,Takatomo Mihana,Felix Köster,Johannes Nokkala,Lina Jaurigue,Ryoichi Horisaki,André Röhm*

Main category: nlin.AO

TL;DR: 本文澄清了噪声对信息处理容量（IPC）的影响，并提出协方差正交投影重构（CROP）方法，可在不建立系统与噪声模型的情况下，从含噪观测中直接重构无噪声分量的协方差与IPC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IPC框架虽能量化驱动动力系统的计算属性，但其在含噪系统中的解释尚不完整；特别是对噪声实现做系综平均所得的IPC，通常并不等于无扰动系统的IPC，因此需要厘清噪声作用并恢复真实的无噪声计算结构。

Method: 首先区分无扰动系统动力学与随机动力学的无噪声分量，指出系综平均响应的IPC与无扰动系统IPC一般不同；随后提出协方差正交投影重构（CROP），直接从含噪观测中重构无噪声分量的协方差与IPC，无需系统或噪声的详细模型；并在固定总测量预算下，与重复试验系综平均的常规做法进行数值对比。

Result: 噪声不仅会降低性能，还可重新分配计算容量，甚至在某些任务上提升表现。数值实验表明，在经典非线性储备池中，CROP比系综平均更准确地估计无噪声IPC；在受不可避免测量噪声影响的量子储备池中亦发现同样的优势。

Conclusion: 该工作揭示了噪声对IPC的非平凡影响，并提供了一个从有限含噪观测中恢复物理系统计算结构的通用途径。

Abstract: Driven dynamical systems can compute when their transient states encode complex transformations of past inputs. The information processing capacity (IPC) framework allows for a detailed accounting of these computational properties, however its interpretation in noisy systems has remained incomplete. In this work, we clarify how noise affects the IPC and how one can reconstruct the noiseless IPC. First, we show how to distinguish the dynamics of an unperturbed system from the noise-free component of the stochastic dynamics: The IPC measured for responses averaged over noise realizations is in general not the same as the IPC of the unperturbed system. We explicitly demonstrate that noise can redistribute computational capacity and sometimes even enhance performance on particular tasks, rather than merely degrading a fixed computation. We then introduce covariance reconstruction by orthogonal projection (CROP), which reconstructs the covariance and IPC of the noise-free component directly from noisy observations, without requiring a detailed model of either the system or the noise. At fixed total measurement budget, numerical tests on a classical nonlinear reservoir show that CROP estimates the noise-free IPC more accurately than the standard practice of ensemble averaging over repeated trials. We find the same advantage in a quantum reservoir subject to unavoidable measurement noise. Our results provide a general route to recovering the computational structure of noisy physical systems from finite observations.

</details>


<div id='physics.comp-ph'></div>

# physics.comp-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [169] [A new generation of effective core potentials: Selected heavy 5d and 6p elements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10853)
*Omar Madany,Lubos Mitas*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 该工作扩展了ccECP库，针对5d和6p区重元素开发半局域赝势与配套基组，采用三级芯-价划分策略以兼顾精度与效率，并提升量子蒙特卡洛数值稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重元素体系因强标量相对论效应、自旋-轨道耦合及电子关联而难以精确建模，现有ccECP库缺乏对重元素的高精度赝势与基组，且随机量子蒙特卡洛方法对势函数在原点的奇异行为敏感，构成预测性多体模拟的瓶颈。

Method: 针对5d区（Hf、Os、Hg）和6p区（Tl、Po、At、Rn）元素，构建半局域赝势及匹配基组；采用三级芯-价划分策略：60芯（Hf、Os、Hg，显式关联亚价壳层）、68芯中间划分（Hg、Tl，兼顾精度与效率）、78芯大芯划分（主族元素，处理芯极化与弛豫）；以相对论全电子CCSD(T)为参考进行参数优化，并对原点势进行正则化处理以保证数值稳定。

Result: 原子低激发态能量全局平均偏差仅0.045 eV；分子转移性良好，解离能偏差小于0.03 eV，平衡键长偏差在0.005 Å以内；原点正则化有限势提升了随机量子蒙特卡洛方法的数值稳定性。

Conclusion: 该工作扩展了ccECP库对重元素的覆盖，通过独特的三级芯-价划分在精度与计算效率间取得平衡，并移除随机量子蒙特卡洛模拟重元素体系的关键方法瓶颈，为预测性多体模拟提供可靠工具。

Abstract: We expand the correlation-consistent effective core potentials (ccECPs) library by developing semi-local pseudopotentials and matching basis sets by heavy-elements from $5d$ (Hf, Os, Hg) and $6p$ (Tl, Po, At, Rn) blocks. In order to accurately capture scalar relativistic effects, spin-orbit coupling, and electron-electron correlation, we implement a tiered core-valence partitioning strategy across three distinct resolutions. This includes a small 60-core (Hf, Os, Hg) that explicitly correlates subvalence shells, a large 78-core definition for the main-group elements that rigorously accounts for core polarization and relaxation effects in sparse valence environments, and an intermediate 68-core partition for Hg and Tl. This 68-core architecture represents a unique development in the ccECP library, optimizing the balance between accuracy and computational efficiency in a manner unexplored for ligther elements. Optimized against relativistic all-electron CCSD(T) references, the ccECPs deliver outstanding atomic precision, achieving a global average atomic low-lying states deviation of just 0.045 eV. This accuracy translates directly to robust molecular transferability, systematically restricting dissociation energy discrepancies to under 0.03 eV, equilibrium bond lengths to within 0.005 Å. By enforcing a regularized, finite potential at the origin for enhanced numerical stability in stochastic quantum Monte Carlo methods, this library removes a critical methodological bottleneck for predictive many-body simulations of heavy-element systems and materials.

</details>


### [170] [A Geometrically Parameterized Quasi-Stationary 3D Model for High-Frequency Induction Tube Welding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11250)
*Dmitry Ivanov,John Inge Asperheim,Bjørnar Grande,Prerana Das*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 提出三维多物理场有限元框架，模拟管材高频感应焊接，耦合时谐磁场与对流扩散传热，经AISI 304不锈钢管工业线验证后，比较两种导磁体材料的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业感应焊接过程中电磁与热场耦合复杂，需准确预测工业工况下的场分布，并评估导磁体材料对焊接效果的影响。

Method: 基于开源工具GetDP与Gmsh，构建参数化几何生成与物理定制半自动网格；将时谐磁标量势与稳定化准静态对流扩散传热方程耦合求解。

Result: 与AISI 304不锈钢管商用感应焊接线实测数据对比，模型与运行数据吻合良好；并据此比较了传统FeNiZnV铁氧体与软磁复合材料Ferrotron 559H两种导磁体。

Conclusion: 该三维多物理场框架可有效预测感应焊接的电磁-热行为，并支持导磁体材料选型与工艺优化。

Abstract: A three-dimensional multiphysics finite element framework for the simulation of high-frequency induction welding of tubes is presented. The model couples a time-harmonic magnetic scalar potential formulation with a stabilized quasi-stationary advection-diffusion heat transport equation, enabling accurate prediction of electromagnetic and thermal fields under industrial operating conditions. The framework incorporates parameterized geometry generation and semi-automated, physics-tailored mesh construction and is implemented using the open-source tools GetDP and Gmsh. Validation against measurements from a commercial induction welding line for AISI 304 stainless steel tubes demonstrates good agreement with operating data. The validated model is subsequently applied to investigate the influence of impeder material by comparing a conventional FeNiZnV ferrite with the soft magnetic composite Ferrotron 559H for the induction welding of AISI 304 stainless steel tubes.

</details>


### [171] [Stress-Testing Dynamical and Generative Downscaling Using Subseasonal Extreme Precipitation Forecasts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11696)
*Mauricio Lima,Marika Koukoula,Romain Pilon,Monika Feldmann,Erwan Koch,Daniela I. V. Domeisen,Tom Beucler*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 该研究在长达3周的子季节尺度上，将WRF动力降尺度与基于扩散的生成式降尺度进行对比，针对两类物理机制不同的极端降水事件，评估发现两者均优于原始ECMWF预报，但各有适用情境：WRF在多单体非平稳事件上概率技巧最高，扩散模型在更平稳的超级单体事件上表现更优且跨指标更稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 粗空间分辨率限制子季节预报模式刻画极端降水的能力；动力降尺度与深度生成降尺度均可缓解该问题，但二者在不同大气环流型（regime）下针对极端事件的相对表现尚不清楚，缺乏系统比较。

Method: 以瑞士CombiPrecip雨量计-雷达融合观测为参考，将WRF模式与基于扩散的生成式模型进行对比；对两类物理机制不同的极端降水事件进行降尺度，预报时效延伸至3周。为与无需针对特定驱动模式训练即可降尺度不同边界条件的WRF公平比较，扩散模型采用非配对（unpaired）方式训练。评价指标包括概率技巧等多种性能指标。

Result: 两种方法相较原始ECMWF预报均有改进，但优势依赖天气型：WRF对多单体、非平稳事件取得最高概率技巧；扩散模型在两类事件的多种指标间表现更一致，并在更平稳的超级单体事件中优于WRF。

Conclusion: 显式动力建模可为特定降水事件的子季节时效提供附加价值，而生成式降尺度在更多样化情境下具有更广泛的适用价值和更稳健的表现。

Abstract: Coarse spatial resolution limits the ability of subseasonal prediction models to resolve extreme precipitation. Downscaling with either dynamical or deep generative models can overcome this issue, but the comparative performance of these models for extremes across different atmospheric regimes remains poorly understood. In this work, we evaluate the Weather Research and Forecasting (WRF) model against a diffusion-based generative model by downscaling two physically distinct, extreme precipitation events up to lead times of 3 weeks. For a fair comparison with WRF, which can downscale boundary conditions from different driving models without model-specific training, the diffusion model is trained in an unpaired fashion. Both approaches improve upon the raw European Centre for Medium-Range Weather Forecasts forecasts, in comparison to fused rain gauge-radar observations in Switzerland (CombiPrecip), but exhibit regime-dependent strengths. WRF achieves the highest probabilistic skill for a multicell, non-stationary event. Conversely, the diffusion model is more consistent across different performance metrics for the two events, outperforming WRF in a more stationary supercell event. These results demonstrate that explicit dynamical modeling can add value for specific precipitation events for subseasonal lead times, and that generative downscaling adds value more broadly in different situations.

</details>


<div id='physics.flu-dyn'></div>

# physics.flu-dyn [[Back]](#toc)

### [172] [A Robust All-Mach Six-Equation Diffuse-Interface Method for Multiphase Flows with Surface Tension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10727)
*Ghanshyam Bharate,J. C. Mandal*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种适用于宽马赫数范围、带表面张力的可压缩多相流有限体积框架，基于双压力六方程扩散界面模型，通过广义黎曼不变量构造HLLC黎曼求解器并改进压力松弛以保持拉普拉斯压力跳跃，同时提出低马赫数修正以抑制数值耗散。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统近似黎曼求解器在低马赫数区域数值耗散过大，而带表面张力的可压缩多相流在跨越近不可压到可压缩工况时难以兼顾精度与鲁棒性，因此需要同时处理毛细压力跳跃与低马赫数耗散的统一框架。

Method: 采用双压力、六方程扩散界面模型，包含粘性、重力及通过连续表面力公式表达的毛细效应；基于广义黎曼不变量分析构造可一致处理毛细诱导压力跳跃的HLLC黎曼求解器；修正瞬时压力松弛过程以在相平衡中保持拉普拉斯压力跳跃；通过扩展已有公式并引入在强压力变化区域仍稳定的改进缩放策略，实现鲁棒的低马赫数修正。

Result: 在一系列涉及表面张力、粘性、重力和可压缩性的基准问题上验证了框架，结果表明界面动力学、毛细压力和低马赫数流动特征预测准确，在显著降低数值耗散的同时保持了稳定性。

Conclusion: 该方法在从近不可压到可压缩的整个范围内保持精度，同时保留六方程表述的鲁棒性，为跨越广泛流态区间的复杂多相流模拟提供了一种高效可靠的途径。

Abstract: A robust finite-volume framework is presented for the simulation of compressible multiphase flows with surface tension across a wide range of Mach numbers. The method is based on a two-pressure, six-equation diffuse interface model incorporating viscous, gravitational, and capillary effects through the continuum surface force formulation. To consistently account for capillary-induced pressure jumps, an HLLC Riemann solver is developed using generalized Riemann invariant analysis, and the instantaneous pressure relaxation procedure is modified to preserve the Laplace pressure jump during phase equilibration. To overcome the excessive numerical diffusion of conventional approximate Riemann solvers in the low-Mach regime, a robust low-Mach correction is proposed by extending our previous formulation with a modified scaling strategy that remains stable in regions of strong pressure variation. The resulting method retains accuracy from nearly incompressible flows to compressible regimes while preserving the robustness of the six-equation formulation. The numerical framework is validated using a series of benchmark problems involving surface tension, viscosity, gravity, and compressibility. The results demonstrate accurate prediction of interface dynamics, capillary pressure, and low-Mach flow features, while significantly reducing numerical dissipation without compromising stability. The proposed methodology provides an efficient and reliable approach for the simulation of complex multiphase flows spanning a broad range of flow regimes.

</details>


### [173] [An exposition on some viscosity dependent issues in refined potential flow theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10649)
*Taofiq Omoniyi Amoloye,Leke Thaddeus Oladimeji,Mahmoud A. Hayajnh,Olalekan Adebayo Olayemi*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文评估了改进势流理论（RPT）在圆柱绕流问题中由黏性导致的局限，通过在准无旋黏性流函数框架下求解连续性方程与N-S方程，兼顾自由来流与无滑移边界条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典势流理论的改进版本（RPT）声称可纳入黏性效应、流动脉动与三维变量，但其黏性相关适用边界尚不明确，需针对黏性依赖的限制进行系统评估。

Method: 以不可压缩流体绕脉冲启动圆柱流动为对象，引入准无旋黏性流函数，在同时满足自由来流条件与无滑移边界条件的前提下求解连续性方程和Navier-Stokes方程。

Result: 摘要未给出具体定量结果。

Conclusion: 摘要未给出明确结论。

Abstract: This study evaluates viscosity-dependent limitations in refined potential flow theory (RPT) applied to incompressible flow over an impulsively started circular cylinder. RPT adapts classical potential flow theory to account for viscous effects, flow fluctuations, and three-dimensional variables. By utilizing a quasi-irrotational viscous stream function, the framework solves the continuity and Navier-Stokes equations while satisfying both free-stream and no-slip boundary conditions.

</details>


### [174] [Giesekus Stick-Slip Singularity: Asymptotic Theory in the Log-Conformation Formulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10836)
*Florian Becker,Philipp Knechtges*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文首次将对数构象重构方法用于非数值目的，解析研究了Giesekus流体在平面黏滑奇异性附近的渐近行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对数构象重构此前主要作为数值手段用于稳定高Weissenberg数下的计算，作者希望探索其在解析渐近分析中的潜力，并扩展已有的Giesekus流体黏滑奇异性渐近理论。

Method: 在给定牛顿流体速度场的假设下，对Giesekus流体在平面黏滑奇异性附近进行对数构象形式的解析渐近分析，分别处理黏附区、滑移区和核心区，并与Evans (JNNFM 222 (2015) 24-33)的渐近应力分析结果进行对照。

Result: 结果与Evans已有的渐近应力分析一致；此外证明构象张量的行列式在渐近意义下为常数，并完整刻画了边界层与核心区之间的过渡行为；由此构造出在整个奇异性邻域内一致有效的对数构象方程复合渐近解。

Conclusion: 对数构象重构不仅是数值工具，也可作为强有力的解析手段，能够显著扩展现有渐近理论并给出奇异点附近的统一复合解。

Abstract: In this paper, the log-conformation reformulation of a Giesekus fluid near a planar stick-slip singularity is investigated analytically. In what is, to our knowledge, the first use of the log-conformation formulation beyond its numerical purpose, we present results that match the asymptotic stress analysis of Evans [JNNFM 222 (2015) 24-33] for the stick, slip, and core regions under the assumption of a given Newtonian velocity field. Furthermore, the logarithmic reformulation allows us to extend the existing asymptotic theory substantially, showing, e.g., that the determinant of the conformation tensor is asymptotically constant, as well as thoroughly characterizing the transition behavior between the boundary layers and the core region. Combining these results, we obtain a composite asymptotic solution of the log-conformation equation that is valid uniformly throughout a close neighborhood of the singularity.

</details>


### [175] [Mixing dynamics and transport mechanisms during laminar stirring flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10773)
*Mohammad Reza Daneshvar Garmroodi,Ida Karimfazli*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 该论文通过数值模拟圆轨道旋转搅拌器在二维牛顿流体中的层流混合，揭示流场拓扑转变控制三种混合模态，并建立流动特征与混合效率的机制联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解层流条件下流场拓扑结构如何决定被动标量的混合过程，弥合混合运动学与流体动力学之间的鸿沟。

Method: 在无限二维域中对初静止牛顿流体进行数值模拟，令圆柱搅拌器沿圆形路径匀速旋转；下半域用被动染料标记以追踪染料界面演化，并在层流范围内改变搅拌雷诺数。

Result: 识别出三种混合模态：扩散主导、过渡及对流混合。扩散主导下形成中心充分混合区与近似自相似的螺旋染料图案；对流混合表现为染料界面大幅变形。混合事件源于搅拌器与界面的直接作用及远离中心区的涡脱落；涡结构逃逸中心区并非局域输运标量梯度时混合增强，涡活动受限时每周期混合仅弱依赖搅拌速度。

Conclusion: 通过识别流动特征输运标量的机制，建立了流场拓扑与混合之间的机制性联系，为混合运动学与底层流体动力学搭建了桥梁。

Abstract: We investigate laminar mixing of a passive dye in an infinite, two-dimensional domain filled with a Newtonian fluid by simulating a cylindrical stirrer that rotates at constant speed along a circular path, stirring an initially quiescent fluid. The fluid is marked by a passive dye in the lower half of the domain, enabling a systematic analysis of dye-interface evolution and mixing dynamics. By varying the stirring Reynolds number within the laminar regime, we identify how transitions in flow topology govern mixing and distinguish three mixing regimes spanning diffusion-dominated and advective mixing. In the diffusion-dominated regime, mixing is characterized by the formation of a well-mixed central region and the development of a spiral dye pattern that evolves in an approximately self-similar manner. Advective mixing is marked by substantial deformation of the dye interface beyond the central region.
  We provide a mechanistic interpretation of advective mixing by relating mixing events to specific flow features: (i) direct interaction between the stirrer and the dye interface, and (ii) vortex shedding near the stirrer and away from the central region. Enhanced mixing occurs when vortical structures are able to escape the central region and transport scalar gradients nonlocally. When vortical activity remains confined near the stirrer's path, mixing per stirrer period exhibits only weak dependence on stirring speed despite increasing stirring intensity. Significant enhancement of mixing per period occurs only when escaping vortices introduce a new transport mechanism that carries scalar gradients far beyond the stirrer's path.
  Overall, we present a mechanistic link between flow topology and mixing by identifying the transport mechanisms through which flow features govern scalar evolution, providing a bridge between the kinematics of mixing and the underlying fluid dynamics.

</details>


### [176] [Long interfacial internal waves with currents: current profiles with quadratic depth-dependence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10820)
*Conor T. Curtin,Rossen I. Ivanov*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 研究赤道内波在赤道潜流（EUC）背景流中的传播，采用深度的二次函数刻画EUC流速剖面，推导出描述温跃层波动的可积非线性Gardner方程，并进一步给出变底地形下的广义Gardner方程；结果表明方程系数对EUC参数高度敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 内波与海流在海洋中普遍共存，赤道内波与赤道潜流（EUC）是二者相互作用的典型代表。既有研究多关注无流或简化流场情形，对真实EUC剖面（与已知观测结果一致的二次型深度分布）下温跃层内波的非线性演化建模仍不充分，因此需要建立能反映该背景流的可积模型并考察地形变化的影响。

Method: 将海水分层为密度略有差异的两层，以温跃层为界面；假设背景流流速剖面为深度的二次函数，与EUC的已知结果相符。在此假设下，通过约化推导得到描述温跃层波动的可积非线性Gardner方程；并针对变底地形情形，进一步推导广义Gardner方程；随后分析方程各系数对EUC参数的依赖关系。

Result: 在二次流速剖面假设下成功得到温跃层波动的可积非线性Gardner方程；在变底条件下得到广义Gardner方程；发现方程系数对EUC的参数（如流速剖面的相关参数）非常敏感，即背景流的细微变化会显著改变非线性与色散等系数。

Conclusion: 赤道潜流的二次流速剖面可自然地纳入温跃层内波的非线性演化模型，所得Gardner方程具有可积性并可推广至变底情形；由于方程系数对EUC参数极为敏感，赤道内波的传播特性会强烈依赖于背景流的垂向结构，这为后续基于EUC观测参数的理论与数值研究提供了建模框架。

Abstract: Internal waves often propagate in the presence of currents. Internal waves and currents are widespread in the world's oceans. The equatorial internal waves and the equatorial undercurrent (EUC) are perhaps the most representative illustration of this phenomenon. The current profile in this study is a quadratic function of depth, which is in agreement with the known results of the EUC. With this assumption the integrable nonlinear Gardner's equation is derived as a model for the wave motion of the thermocline, separating the two layers of slightly different densities. In addition, we derive the generalised Gardner's equation in the case of variable bottom. We demonstrate that the coefficients of the equation are very sensitive with respect to the parameters of the EUC.

</details>


### [177] [Correlative effects of induced magnetic field-buoyancy on reactive solute dispersion dynamics in couple-stress fluids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11099)
*Aritra Roy,Debabrata Das,Pranab Kumar Mondal*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 研究反应性溶质在压力驱动、浮力与诱导磁场共同作用下、于两平行板间流动的 couple-stress 流体中的弥散，建立三阶多尺度渐近模型，结合数值与随机模拟，给出有效纵向弥散系数及速度解奇异特征，并揭示磁与力偶应力主导区间的四分之一次幂标度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: couple-stress 流体在磁场、浮力及壁面/体相反应耦合下的溶质弥散机理尚不清楚，缺乏高阶渐近理论刻画有效弥散系数、瞬态矩及浓度结构，也缺少解析与数值、随机模拟的相互验证，因此需要建立统一框架并揭示各物理机制的竞争与标度规律。

Method: 采用 Mei 多重尺度均匀化技术展开至三阶，推导高阶渐近解以确定有效纵向弥散系数与浓度场；用 Brownian 动力学模拟与有限差分求解进行验证；以 Aris 矩方法量化瞬态平均位移、空间方差和有效弥散度；模型含压力驱动流、浮力、诱导磁场、壁面一阶非均相反应与体相反应。

Result: 水动力分析发现当 Hartmann 数等于 couple-stress 参数一半时速度解存在奇异分支，并识别出 Hartmann 数与 couple-stress 参数间的四分之一次幂特征标度，用于区分力偶应力主导区与磁主导区；在非反应 Newtonian 极限下退化为经典 Taylor 弥散并与实验吻合；couple-stress 流变与磁阻尼抑制剪切诱导弥散，浮力通过附加横向速度梯度增强弥散；随 couple-stress 增大弥散系数出现饱和区；壁面吸收不等导致持续横向不对称，强吸收增强源附近溶质移除；数值与随机结果验证解析框架并解析高阶浓度结构与颗粒尺度壁面吸附。

Conclusion: 所建三阶多尺度渐近框架能一致刻画 couple-stress 流体中反应性溶质在磁场、浮力与壁面/体相反应耦合下的弥散行为，统一揭示抑制与增强机制、饱和及不对称效应，并由数值和随机模拟验证，兼具理论一般性与对经典极限的兼容性。

Abstract: We investigate the dispersion of a reactive solute in a couple-stress fluid flowing between two parallel plates under the combined effects of pressure-driven flow, buoyancy, and an induced magnetic field. The model incorporates first-order heterogeneous reactions at both channel walls alongside a bulk reaction. Using Mei's multiscale homogenization technique accurate to third order, we develop a higher-order asymptotic formulation to determine the effective longitudinal dispersion coefficient and concentration field. Analytical predictions are complemented by Brownian dynamics simulations and finite-difference solutions, while the Aris method of moments quantifies transient mean displacement, spatial variance, and effective dispersivity. The hydrodynamic analysis reveals a singular branch in the velocity solution when the Hartmann number equals half the couple-stress parameter and identifies a characteristic quarter-power scaling between the Hartmann number and couple-stress parameter, separating couple-stress- and magnetically dominated regimes. The model recovers classical Taylor-dispersion behavior in the non-reactive Newtonian limit and agrees well with experimental measurements. Couple-stress rheology and magnetic damping suppress shear-induced dispersion, whereas buoyancy enhances dispersion through additional transverse velocity gradients. A distinct saturation regime of the dispersion coefficient emerges with an increasing couple-stress parameter, while unequal wall absorption induces persistent transverse asymmetry, and stronger absorption enhances solute removal near the source. Numerical and stochastic results validate the analytical framework while resolving higher-order concentration structures and particle-scale wall adsorption.

</details>


### [178] [A Novel Multi-fidelity Surrogate for Turbomachinery Design Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11111)
*Qineng Wang,Liming Song,Zhendong Guo,Jun Li,Zhenping Feng*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 针对多保真优化(MFO)在序贯采样下高保真样本分布不均导致最终解劣于单保真优化(SFO)的问题，提出多单保真优化(MSFO)算法，融合DBSCAN检测与全局多保真/局部单保真代理集成，在数值基准与涡轮气动、端壁气膜冷却布局优化中取得更优且更鲁棒的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 涡轮机械设计优化属于昂贵的黑箱问题，多保真优化(MFO)借助低保真样本减少高保真样本需求，理论上可提升样本效率。然而在序贯采样优化中，MFO虽在早期阶段表现更好，但最终最优解常劣于单保真优化(SFO)。其根源在于MFO依赖样本"均匀且嵌套分布"的假设，而序贯加点策略会破坏该假设，使低保真样本在局部区域失去指导作用甚至产生误导。

Method: 提出多单保真优化(MSFO)算法。在代理模型构建中，采用基于密度的噪声应用空间聚类(DBSCAN)识别低保真样本不再有效的局部区域；进而将全局多保真代理(MFS)与仅用高保真样本构建的局部单保真代理组合为集成模型，以增强算法对误导性低保真数据的抗干扰能力。高保真样本来自细网格CFD仿真，低保真样本来自同一仿真的粗网格。

Result: 首先在数值基准函数上验证MSFO的有效性；随后应用于涡轮气动型面优化与涡轮端壁气膜冷却布局设计。结果表明，MSFO算法显著优于传统SFO与MFO流程，并具有更高的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: MSFO通过DBSCAN局部检测与全局多保真/局部单保真集成建模，克服了传统MFO因序贯采样破坏样本均匀嵌套假设而导致的性能退化问题，在涡轮机械昂贵黑箱优化中兼顾了样本效率、优化质量与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Turbomachinery design optimization involves expensive black-box problems. Sample-efficient multi-fidelity optimization (MFO) offers an efficient solution. By utilizing multi-fidelity surrogates (MFS), the MFO algorithm can use fewer high-fidelity samples aided by low-fidelity samples to establish an accurate surrogate model. However, when MFS is used in sequential sampling optimization, it has been observed that the final optimal solution obtained by single-fidelity optimization (SFO) is better than that of MFO, even though MFO performs better at the early stages. This can be attributed to the assumption of an even and nested distribution of samples, which is incorrect when using a sequential adding strategy. To address these issues, we propose a novel algorithm called multi-single-fidelity optimization (MSFO) to overcome the limitations of the conventional MFO procedures. In the surrogate establishment of MSFO, we use the density-based spatial clustering of applications with noise (DBSCAN) method to detect local areas where low-fidelity samples are no longer effective. A combination of both global MFS and local single-fidelity surrogate model, built using high-fidelity samples alone, is used to establish an ensemble, which improves the anti-interference ability of the algorithm against misleading low-fidelity data. The effectiveness of the MSFO algorithm is verified first on numerical benchmark functions. Then, the algorithm is used to optimize the aerodynamic profile of a turbine and the film cooling layout design of a turbine endwall. Here, high-fidelity sample sources are obtained from fine-mesh CFD simulations, whereas low-fidelity sample sources are obtained from the same simulations run on a coarser mesh. The results demonstrate that our MSFO algorithm performs significantly better than the conventional SFO and MFO processes, with a higher level of robustness.

</details>


### [179] [A Novel Multi-fidelity Surrogate for Efficient Turbine Design Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11112)
*Qineng Wang,Liming Song,Zhendong Guo,Jun Li,Zhenping Feng*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 针对多保真代理优化在高保真样本增多时低保真样本产生负面影响、导致最终解变差的问题，提出融合多保真与单保真代理的优化算法MSFO，并在GE-E3叶片优化与涡轮端壁气膜冷却布局设计中验证有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 涡轮设计优化计算成本高，SBO与MFS虽被广泛用于降本，但作者观察到MFS优化往往仅在早期收敛更快，最终解却劣于仅用高保真样本的SFS优化。其原因是优化过程中随高保真样本增多，低保真样本会产生负面效应，误导搜索方向。

Method: 提出集成加权多保真代理（EMFS）。采用DBSCAN检测MFS无法构建更精确代理的区域，在这些区域构建局部单保真代理（SFS）；再以自适应权重将MFS与SFS组合成EMFS以指导优化，对应算法称为多保真与单保真代理融合优化（MSFO）。

Result: 在GE-E3叶片优化和涡轮端壁气膜冷却布局设计两项测试中，MSFO方法的有效性得到充分验证。

Conclusion: 通过DBSCAN区域检测与自适应加权融合多保真与单保真代理，MSFO有效抑制了低保真样本的负面影响，改善最终优化解质量，为涡轮设计优化提供了实用方法。

Abstract: To solve the turbine design optimization problems efficiently, surrogate-based optimization (SBO) algorithms are frequently used. To further reduce the cost of turbine design, the multi-fidelity surrogate (MFS) based optimization is proposed by the researchers, who resort to augmenting the small number of expensive high-fidelity (HF) samples by a large portion of low-fidelity (LF) but cheap samples in surrogate modeling and optimization process. Nonetheless, according to our observations, the MFS based optimization sometimes can only have better convergence rate at the early stage of optimization process, but yielding worse final solution than the single-fidelity surrogate (SFS) based optimization that uses high-fidelity samples alone. The reason behind can be explained as follows. With the increase of HF samples in the optimization process, the LF samples can cause negative effect and therefore misleading the optimization search. To address the above issue, an ensemble weighted multi-fidelity surrogate (EMFS) is proposed. Specifically, the density-based spatial clustering of applications with noise (DBSCAN) is used to detect the region where the MFS cannot build a more accurate surrogate, and a local SFS is built there. Then, an EMFS is built by combining the MFS and SFS with adaptive weights, which is used to guide the optimization process. The related algorithm is named as multi- and single-fidelity surrogate fused optimization, i.e., MSFO. Through tests on GE-E3 blade optimization and the film cooling layout design of a turbine endwall, the effectiveness of proposed MSFO is well demonstrated.

</details>


### [180] [DiffSWE2d: a differentiable Shallow Water Equations solver for end-to-end flood and tsunami modelling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11426)
*Zhonghou Xu*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文提出DiffSWE2d，一个基于PyTorch的开源可微分二维浅水方程求解器，用于端到端洪水与海啸建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统浅水方程求解器在求解反问题和优化问题时计算代价高昂，尤其是需要通过反复前向模拟来估计关于模型输入或参数的梯度。

Method: 利用自动微分技术，将时间推进的物理过程表示为可微分计算图，使梯度能够直接通过数值求解器反向传播；采用PyTorch实现，并通过两个基准算例进行验证，应用于海啸波形反演。

Result: 在两个基准算例上验证了求解器；在海啸波形反演中展示了通过基于梯度的优化推断模型输入的能力。

Conclusion: DiffSWE2d为将基于物理的流体动力学建模与现代优化及机器学习方法相结合提供了灵活框架，源代码与可复现示例已公开。

Abstract: Solving inverse and optimisation problems with traditional shallow water equations (SWE) solvers can be computationally expensive, particularly when gradients with respect to model inputs or parameters must be estimated through repeated forward simulations. In this paper, we introduce DiffSWE2d, an open-source differentiable shallow water equations solver for end-to-end flood and tsunami modelling implemented in PyTorch. By leveraging automatic differentiation, DiffSWE2d represents the time-marching physics as a differentiable computational graph, enabling gradients to be propagated directly through the numerical solver. We validate the solver against two established benchmark cases and demonstrate its application to tsunami waveform inversion, showing its ability to infer model inputs through gradient-based optimisation. DiffSWE2d provides a flexible framework for integrating physics-based hydrodynamic modelling with modern optimisation and machine learning methods. The source code and reproducible examples are publicly available at: https://github.com/ZhonghouXu/DiffSWE2d

</details>


### [181] [Low inertia limit of elasto-inertial turbulence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11581)
*Shoaib Kamil,Sarath Sankar Suresh,Jose M. Lopez,Björn Hof*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 该文通过管道实验确定了弹性湍流（EIT）的转捩阈值，覆盖三个数量级的弹性数，发现转捩始终呈中心模态特征，并在高弹性极限下失稳，首次实验观测到粘弹性管道流中的行波，与模型预言的中心模态"箭头"解高度吻合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 粘弹性流体的管流与槽道流在异常低流速下即出现混沌动力学（弹性湍流EIT），但百年来其起源机制缺乏实验证实。理论计算与模型模拟提出了多种竞争性情景——环向应力模态、中心模态、Tollmien-Schlichting波等，实际聚合物溶液流动究竟对应哪一种，仍未知。

Method: 在管道流动实验平台上测量EIT的转捩阈值，参数范围覆盖三个数量级的弹性数（elasticity number），并结合对转捩起始处结构形态（中心模态结构）的识别与失稳极限的判定，进而观测局域化结构与行波行为。

Result: 在整个参数范围内，EIT转捩均以中心模态结构出现；当惯性进一步降低（弹性升高）时，该不稳定在某一极限处失稳，成为中心模态机制的有力标志。同时首次在粘弹性管道流实验中观测到行波，其局域化结构序列与模型模拟中发现的中心模态行波"箭头"（arrowhead）解高度一致。

Conclusion: 实验证据支持EIT起源的中心模态（traveling wave/arrowhead）情景，而非环向应力模态或Tollmien-Schlichting波机制；该结论为长期争议的弹性湍流转捩机制提供了直接实验判据。

Abstract: Pipe and channel flows of viscoelastic fluids display chaotic dynamics at unusually low speeds, a phenomenon referred to as elasto-inertial turbulence, EIT. First reported in experiments a century ago, recent theoretical studies and model computations predict a variety of scenarios for the phenomenon's origin, ranging from hoop stress modes to center modes and to Tollmien-Schlichting waves. Lacking experimental confirmation, the relevant scenario in actual flows of polymer solutions remains unknown. We here determine the transition threshold of EIT in pipe experiments, covering three decades in elasticity number. Across this entire parameter range, the transition features center mode structures at onset. Eventually the instability diverges at a lower inertia (upper elasticity) limit, which is a robust signature of this center mode scenario. Finally, we report the first experimental observation of a traveling wave in viscoelastic pipe flow, and the sequences of localized structures found, are in excellent agreement with a center mode traveling wave, the "arrowhead" solution, discovered in model simulations.

</details>


### [182] [Surface gravity wave-mean flow interaction with comparable spatial scales. Part I: reduced wave equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11587)
*Basile Gallet,Alexandre Tlili*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 提出"等效可解性条件"方法，将深水表面重力波在背景流（空间尺度与波长相当、流速远小于群速度）中的三维问题降维为二维约化方程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深水表面重力波在背景流中传播时，背景流空间尺度与波长相当且流速远慢于波群速度。完整三维描述计算代价高，尤其需要解析快波周期，因此需要构建能够保持主导阶解和首个可解性条件的约化模型。

Method: 引入"等效可解性条件"方法：要求约化方程在多重时间尺度展开下与原系统共享相同的零阶解和首个可解性条件。分别推导了深度不变背景流上的宽带波约化方程，以及完全三维背景流上的窄带波约化方程，后者呈薛定谔方程形式，仅涉及背景流的近表层涡度。

Result: 对于窄带波在三维背景流中的情形，约化方程仅依赖背景流近表层涡度，近表层水平流散度的影响被证明为次主导。约化方程不仅降低空间维度，还使波场在背景流的慢平流时间尺度上演化，从而省去解析快速波周期的计算负担。通过弱散射解析解与强散射数值解验证了约化方程的能力。

Conclusion: 该约化框架为背景流中的深水表面重力波提供了一种高效且准确的二维描述，兼顾降维与消除快时间尺度计算负担两方面优势，适用于宽带与窄带波、深度不变与完全三维背景流等多种情形。

Abstract: We consider deep-water surface gravity waves propagating above a background flow whose spatial scale is comparable to the wavelength, focusing on the regime where the flow is slow compared to the group velocity of the waves. We introduce an "equivalent solvability condition" method to construct reduced equations, demanding that, upon multiple-timescale expansion, the reduced equations share the same leading-order solution and first solvability condition as the original system. This approach turns the full 3D problem into a 2D reduced equation for the wave field. We derive such reduced equations for broad-band waves above a depth-invariant background flow, and for narrow-band waves above a fully 3D background flow. In the latter case the reduced equation takes the form of a Schrodinger equation involving the near-surface vorticity of the background flow only, with the impact of the near-surface horizontal flow divergence shown to be subdominant. Beyond the reduction in spatial dimensionality, the latter reduced equation describes the evolution of the wave field over the slow advective timescale of the background flow, thereby eliminating the computational burden of time-resolving the fast wave period. We illustrate the capabilities of the reduced equations through an analytical solution for the weak scattering of a wave packet by a patch of organized flow, followed by numerical solutions for stronger scattering of a wave packet by a patch of disorganized flow.

</details>


### [183] [Surface gravity wave-mean flow interaction with comparable spatial scales. Part II: two-way coupling and wave-wave interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11631)
*Basile Gallet*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 研究深水窄带表面重力波在空间尺度与波长相当的背景流上传播，考虑背景流速度与波浪Stokes漂移同量级的情形，推导出快波与慢背景流的双向耦合模型。该模型包含非线性波相互作用，由非线性约化波方程与Craik-Leibovich方程耦合构成，可在背景流慢翻转频率尺度上描述波场与背景流演化，避免时间上分辨快波频率的计算负担。无粘模型精确守恒波作用量、机械能和水平动量，粘性模型精确守恒水平动量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 背景流空间尺度与波长相当且流速与Stokes漂移可比时，快波与慢背景流之间存在双向耦合；非线性波相互作用与双向耦合处于同一渐近阶，必须纳入推导。目标是在慢时间尺度上高效描述波场与背景流演化，降低计算成本。

Method: 针对深水窄带表面重力波，在背景流速度与波浪Stokes漂移同量级的渐近区域，推导快波与慢背景流双向耦合模型；将非线性约化波方程与Craik-Leibovich方程耦合，并把耦合项整理成紧凑形式以便物理解释和数值实现。

Result: 得到非线性约化波方程与Craik-Leibovich方程耦合的双向模型，可在背景流慢翻转频率尺度上描述波场与背景流演化，无需时间上分辨快波频率；无粘模型精确守恒波作用量、机械能和水平动量，粘性模型精确守恒水平动量。

Conclusion: 该模型为背景流与表面重力波相互作用提供了高效且守恒的双向耦合框架，耦合项紧凑，便于物理解释与数值实现。

Abstract: We consider narrow-band deep-water surface gravity waves propagating above a background flow whose spatial scale is comparable to the wavelength. We focus on the regime where the background-flow speed is comparable to the Stokes drift of the waves to derive a two-way coupled model between the fast waves and the slow background flow. Nonlinear wave interactions arise at the same asymptotic order as the two-way coupling and must therefore be included in the derivation. The resulting model consists of a nonlinear version of the reduced wave equation obtained in part I, coupled to a Craik-Leibovich equation governing the evolution of the background flow. The model describes the evolution of the wave field and background flow over the slow turnover frequency of the background flow, thus saving the computational burden of temporally resolving the fast wave frequency. The inviscid model exactly conserves wave action, mechanical energy and horizontal momentum, while the viscous model exactly conserves horizontal momentum. The coupling terms are cast in compact form for ease of physical interpretation and numerical implementation.

</details>


### [184] [A Conservative Hybrid Eulerian-Lagrangian Method with Persistent Structure Tracking for Multiscale Cavitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11776)
*Mahdi Lavari,Aswin Gnanaskandan*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 提出一种守恒型混合欧拉-拉格朗日框架，内置在线持续结构追踪，用于多尺度空化模拟


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有混合方法将欧拉-拉格朗日表示转换视为局部几何操作，且解析结构逐时刻独立识别，无法利用时间历史，限制了表示切换的物理合理性

Method: 用连通域标记检测解析蒸汽结构，并通过确定性并行流程跨处理器边界协调；基于物理预测关联连续观测，维持结构持久身份与历史；引入历史感知的欧拉-拉格朗日转换准则；采用守恒双向传递算子

Result: 典型测试验证了碎片化、合并与近距离穿越下的持续追踪，重复表示切换，质量与动量守恒，以及确定性并行行为

Conclusion: 该框架通过持久结构追踪与守恒传递，实现了历史感知的表示转换，提升了多尺度空化混合模拟的一致性与守恒性

Abstract: Hybrid Eulerian Lagrangian methods for multiscale cavitation represent grid resolved vapor structures in an Eulerian formulation and unresolved bubbles in a Lagrangian description, requiring transitions between the two representations as structures evolve. Existing approaches typically treat these transitions as local geometric operations, while resolved structures are identified independently at each time step, limiting the use of temporal history in representation changes. This work presents a conservative hybrid Eulerian Lagrangian framework with embedded, on the fly persistent structure tracking. Resolved vapor structures are detected using connected component labeling and reconciled across processor boundaries through a deterministic parallel procedure. Successive observations are associated using physics informed predictions of structure position and size, maintaining persistent identities and histories through birth, death, breakup, coalescence, and representation transitions. The retained history enables history aware Eulerian to Lagrangian transition criteria and initialization of Lagrangian bubbles with the radius and radius rate information required by the bubble dynamics model. Conservative bidirectional transfer operators preserve vapor mass and linear momentum while preventing simultaneous representation of the same vapor volume. Canonical tests verify persistent tracking through fragmentation, coalescence, and close crossings, repeated representation switching, conservation of mass and momentum, and deterministic parallel behavior.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [185] [EVTradeMatch: A Mobility-Aware Multi-Objective Matching Framework for EV--EV Energy Trading](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10551)
*Md. Mahfujur Rahman,Alistair Barros,Raja Jurdak,Darshika Koggalahewa*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出 EVTradeMatch，一种预测引导的多目标优化框架，用于移动感知的电动汽车（EV）之间点对点能量交易，通过 NSGA-II 求解匹配问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在充电基础设施有限的情况下，EV 间点对点能量交易可提升充电灵活性，但有效交易需在行程特定条件下协调供能方—用能方匹配。现有方法（如基于邻近度或拍卖的方法）难以兼顾移动性、能量与上下文特征。

Method: 基于 EVNextTrade 的学习排序模型，定义充电节点适宜度为预测分数；将 EV–EV 匹配建模为多目标混合整数线性规划，最大化匹配覆盖率、转移能量与节点适宜度，同时最小化移动成本，并满足时空、一对一匹配与节点容量约束；设计定制 NSGA-II 近似帕累托有效解。

Result: 相比邻近度与拍卖类 SOTA 方法，转移能量提升 74.2–82.8%，节点适宜度提升 8.3–84.4%，匹配覆盖率提升 3.74–25.07 个百分点；均衡 NSGA-II 解达 53.07±0.76% 覆盖率、转移 1701.94±17.03 kWh，移动成本较高作为权衡；帕累托前沿支持按运营优先级灵活选解。

Conclusion: EVTradeMatch 可作为预测引导的决策工具，在广域动态场景中实现多目标权衡的 EV–EV 能量交易匹配。

Abstract: Peer-to-peer energy trading among electric vehicles (EVs) can improve charging flexibility under limited charging infrastructure, but effective EV--EV trading requires coordinated provider--consumer matching under journey-specific conditions. This paper proposes EVTradeMatch, a prediction-guided multi-objective optimization framework for mobility-aware EV--EV energy trading. Building on the EVNextTrade study, a prior learning-to-rank model for charging-node recommendation, we define charging-node suitability as a prediction-derived score reflecting the appropriateness of assigning a provider--consumer pair to a candidate charging node based on mobility, energy, and contextual trading features. This score is used as a guidance signal and as an explicit optimization objective rather than as a hard selection rule. The EV--EV matching problem is formulated as a multi-objective mixed-integer linear program that maximizes matching coverage, transferred energy, and charging-node suitability while minimizing mobility cost under spatial, temporal, one-to-one matching, and charging-node capacity constraints. To approximate Pareto-efficient solutions in wide-area dynamic settings, we develop a tailored non-dominated sorting genetic algorithm II (NSGA-II). Experimental results show that EVTradeMatch improves transferred energy by 74.2--82.8% and charging-node suitability by 8.3--84.4% compared with proximity- and auction-based state-of-the-art methods, while improving matching coverage by 3.74--25.07 percentage points. Balanced NSGA-II solutions achieve 53.07$\pm$0.76% matching coverage and transfer 1701.94$\pm$17.03 kWh, with higher mobility cost as an explicit trade-off against travel-minimizing methods. Pareto-front analysis shows that the framework supports flexible selection among high-coverage, high-energy, low-mobility-cost, and high-suitability solutions according to operational priorities.

</details>


### [186] [Disturbance rejection for classes of nonlinear systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10553)
*Saverio Messineo*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究两类非线性系统的非自适应全局鲁棒抗扰问题：C1为带线性Hurwitz零动态（状态不可测）且含强迫、非匹配加性扰动的严格反馈系统；C2为最小相位、不确定、相对阶大于1、输出相关非线性可能无界且导数无界、含匹配加性扰动的非线性系统。基于高增益范式实现C1的输入到状态稳定性与全局渐近收敛，并叠加滑模单元降低高增益开销；对C2采用开环观测器替代动态扩张，得到输出反馈控制器实现全局一致有界与渐近调节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决非线性系统在非自适应条件下对强迫加性扰动的全局鲁棒抑制问题，克服既有方法（如经典动态扩张）在存在未知强迫扰动时不可实现的局限。

Method: 对C1采用基于高增益范式的控制架构，利用非线性工具证明闭环输入到状态稳定性，并嵌入基于滑模范式的均匀有界控制单元；对C2以开环观测器替代经典动态扩张，并将C1结果适配至输出反馈情形。

Result: C1实现闭环输入到状态稳定性及全局渐近收敛至可任意缩小的吸引子，并获得均匀有界控制作用、有望降低所需高增益控制开销；C2实现闭环全局一致有界及渐近调节至可任意缩小的吸引子。

Conclusion: 所提非自适应控制架构可对两类非线性系统实现全局鲁棒抗扰，吸引子可任意缩小，且输出反馈方案在未知强迫扰动下仍然可行。

Abstract: This paper addresses the problem of non-adaptive global robust disturbance rejection for two distinct classes of nonlinear systems. The first class, denoted by C1, consists of nonlinear systems in strict-feedback form, with linear and Hurwitz zero-dynamics (whose states are unavailable for feedback), and enhanced - within this work - by forcing, unmatched, additive disturbances. Nonlinear tools are herein employed to demonstrate that the proposed control architecture - based on the high-gain paradigm - achieves closed-loop input-to-state stability with respect to the forcing disturbances, along with global asymptotic convergence towards an attractor which can be rendered as small as desired. Then, owing to the established input-to-state stability property, a uniformly bounded control action is additionally embedded within the control architecture. The additional unit, designed following the sliding-mode paradigm, is aimed at improving the disturbance rejection task, by potentially lowering the required high-gain control expenditure. The second class of systems, denoted by C2, is constituted by minimum-phase, uncertain, nonlinear systems with relative degree greater than one, featuring possibly unbounded, with possibly unbounded derivatives, output-dependent nonlinearities, with matched additive forcing disturbances. To solve the problem of output-feedback, non-adaptive, global robust disturbance rejection for systems within C2, first, an open-loop observer is employed in lieu of a classic dynamic extension adopted in earlier works, as the latter is no longer implementable due to the presence of unknown forcing disturbances. Subsequently, the results derived for C1 are then adapted to C2, to yield an output-feedback dynamic controller providing closed-loop global uniform boundedness, along with asymptotic regulation towards an attractor which can be rendered as small as desired.

</details>


### [187] [Constructing Pareto Sets Using Noether's Second Theorem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10555)
*Evgeny Nikulchev*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于诺特定理第二定理的多目标变分控制问题Pareto集解析构造方法，揭示动力学系统规范对称性与Pareto前沿结构的联系，并提出规范正则化以降低解空间维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多目标变分控制问题的Pareto集通常只能通过数值优化近似求解，缺乏解析刻画，且解空间维度高、计算代价大。作者希望借助对称性理论，从系统的不变性出发，为Pareto前沿提供解析结构，并减少无关变量。

Method: 以诺特定理第二定理为理论基础，建立动力学系统规范对称性与Pareto集结构之间的基本联系；将Pareto前沿解释为由系统及质量准则关于某对称群的不变性所产生的守恒律；提出"规范正则化"概念，在不改变Pareto集的前提下消去不影响准则的变量，从而降低解空间维度。

Result: 给出了Pareto前沿的守恒律解释与规范正则化方法，并通过两个数值算例验证：具有两个冲突准则的线性二次调节器问题，以及标准ZDT1测试问题。结果表明该方法能够解析构造Pareto集并有效降低问题维度。

Conclusion: 对称性分析为多目标变分控制问题的Pareto集提供了新的解析构造途径，规范对称性与守恒律视角可简化问题结构；规范正则化为高维多目标优化中的变量约简提供了理论工具。

Abstract: This paper presents an analytical approach to constructing the Pareto set in multiobjective variational control problems, based on Noether's second theorem. A fundamental connection is established between the gauge symmetries of the dynamical system describing the plant and the structure of the Pareto set. It is shown that the Pareto front can be interpreted as a conservation law arising from the invariance of the system and the quality criteria with respect to a symmetry group. A gauge regularization concept is proposed, which allows eliminating variables that do not affect the criteria without changing the Pareto set, thereby reducing the dimension of the solution space. Numerical examples are provided for the linear-quadratic regulator with two conflicting criteria and for the standard ZDT1 test problem.

</details>


### [188] [PEJWAK: A Profile-Echoing Self-Anchored Aggregation Architecture for Multi-Criteria Decision Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10562)
*Seyyed Ahmad Edalatpanah*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: PEJWAK是一种"轮廓回声"式自锚定聚合架构，用于多准则决策分析（MCDA）。每个准则在最终聚合前与源自同一备选方案的内生参考几何耦合，从而在保留准则身份的同时，使其贡献受备选方案轮廓的条件化调节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统直接加权聚合无法刻画准则贡献对备选方案轮廓的依赖，而非可加交互模型则参数密集、可解释性与可审计性不足。PEJWAK旨在两者之间提供一种可审计的、轮廓条件化的聚合机制。

Method: 提出通用公式，将锚定函数、锚定核、保留指数、贡献系数与外层聚合规则分离；典型成员采用加权算术自锚、令保留指数等于对应准则重要性值、并通过归一化平方根映射导出贡献系数。同时构建确定性MCDA审计流程，提供准则级分解、精确权重稳定区间与秩转变、贡献规则秩相、集合依赖性诊断及基于置换的精确秩亲和性分析。

Result: 解析结果证明该架构满足连续性、单调性、对角校准、内部性、全域联合连续性、精确边界行为与非可分离性；在等权二元限制下双对称性失效；并给出精确的对数保留律。典型PEJWAK无需epsilon正则化即可处理精确零值：当其余活跃得分可支撑正锚时，活跃零仅移除其自身准则贡献。

Conclusion: PEJWAK提供了介于直接加权聚合与参数密集型非可加交互模型之间的可审计、轮廓条件化聚合机制。

Abstract: PEJWAK is introduced as a profile-echoing self-anchored aggregation architecture for multi-criteria decision analysis. Each criterion is coupled geometrically with an endogenous reference derived from the same alternative before final aggregation, so criterion identity is preserved while its expressed contribution is conditioned by the alternative's profile. A general formulation separates the anchor function, anchoring kernel, retention exponents, contribution coefficients, and outer aggregation rule. The canonical member uses a weighted-arithmetic self-anchor, sets each retention exponent equal to the corresponding criterion-importance value, and derives contribution coefficients through a normalized square-root map. Analytical results establish continuity, monotonicity, diagonal calibration, internality, full-domain joint continuity, exact boundary behavior, nonseparability, failure of bisymmetry in the equal-weight binary restriction, and an exact logarithmic retention law. A deterministic MCDA audit provides complete criterion-level decomposition, exact weight-stability intervals and rank transitions, contribution-rule rank phases, set-dependence diagnostics, and exact permutation-based rank-affinity analysis. Canonical PEJWAK handles exact zeros without epsilon regularization: an active zero removes only its own criterion contribution whenever the remaining active scores sustain a positive anchor. The architecture therefore supplies an auditable profile-conditioned aggregation mechanism between direct weighted aggregation and parameter-intensive nonadditive interaction models.

</details>


### [189] [Supply Chain Analytics: A Data-Driven Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10563)
*Elioth Sanabria*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该文综述性著作将统计学习与鲁棒决策统一于供应链分析框架，覆盖需求预测、库存与网络控制、分布鲁棒优化及车辆路径等主题，为数据驱动自动化系统提供理论蓝图与算法指南。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代供应链面临结构不确定性、市场波动与运营中断，实时数据驱动的学习与鲁棒决策框架之间存在脱节，需要系统性的理论整合。

Method: 采用数学严谨的处理方式，从经验需求预测出发，运用样本最小化、时变库存补货的动态规划递推、网络履约框架、基于传输理论的分布鲁棒优化，并结合列生成求解车辆路径与非齐次排队机制等预测性与规定性算法。

Result: 构建了一个从预测统计模型到规定性控制算法的完整方法论体系，涵盖应对稀有事件的DRO方法及多种优化与排队工具。

Conclusion: 该著作既是理论蓝图又是算法指南，为处于机器学习、数学优化与应用概率交叉领域的研究者与从业者设计有韧性的数据驱动自动化系统提供基础工具。

Abstract: Modern supply chain networks increasingly rely on real-time data to navigate structural uncertainties, market volatility, and operational disruptions. This manuscript bridges the gap between statistical data-driven learning and robust decision-making frameworks in logistics and operations management. We present a comprehensive, mathematically rigorous treatment of supply chain analytics, moving from empirical demand forecasting to optimal inventory and network control under uncertainty. Key topics explored include sample minimization, dynamic programming recursions for time-varying inventory replenishment, network fulfillment frameworks, and advanced distributionally robust optimization (DRO) via transport theory to hedge against rare events. By integrating predictive statistical models with prescriptive control algorithms, such as column generation for vehicle routing and non-homogeneous queueing regimes, this text provides the foundational tools necessary for designing resilient, data-driven automated systems. It serves as both a theoretical blueprint and an algorithmic guide for researchers and practitioners operating at the intersection of machine learning, mathematical optimization, and applied probability.

</details>


### [190] [Operator Splitting Methods with Online Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10732)
*Wanyu Zhang,Wenzhi Gao,Madeleine Udell*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一个自动调优算子分裂方法预条件子的理论框架，将前向-后向分裂、Douglas-Rachford分裂和ADMM解释为对应包络函数上的预条件梯度下降，并用在线学习自适应学习预条件子，实现渐近更快的收敛速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 算子分裂方法（如FBS、DR、ADMM）的收敛性能高度依赖预条件子的选择，但预条件子通常需要人工调参，缺乏自动、有理论保障的调优机制。

Method: 将三类算子分裂方法统一解释为其对应包络函数（envelope functions）上的预条件梯度下降；在此基础上引入在线学习（online learning）框架，在优化过程中自适应地学习预条件子。

Result: 理论上获得渐近更快的收敛速率；在Lasso、ℓ1正则化逻辑回归和二次规划等重要问题上加速求解；大量数值实验验证了理论结果并展示了实际有效性。

Conclusion: 该框架为算子分裂方法的预条件子自动调优提供了有原则的统一理论视角，兼具理论收敛保证与实际加速效果。

Abstract: This paper develops a principled framework for automatically tuning preconditioners in operator splitting methods, including forward-backward splitting, Douglas-Rachford splitting, and the alternating direction method of multipliers. We interpret these splitting methods as preconditioned gradient descent applied to their corresponding envelope functions and use online learning to adaptively learn the preconditioner during optimization. Our framework achieves asymptotically faster convergence rates and accelerates the solution of important optimization problems, including Lasso, $\ell_1$-regularized logistic regression, and quadratic programming. Extensive numerical experiments validate our theoretical results and demonstrate the practical effectiveness of the proposed approach.

</details>


### [191] [Method for Constrained Computational Guidance and Control with Application to Hypersonic Entry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10813)
*Emily M. Palmer,Anil V. Rao*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种约束计算制导与控制方法，通过周期性重解带约束最优控制问题并引入平滑最大值惩罚项提高可行性，在RLV最大横程再入问题上验证有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扰动系统可能导致约束越界，使原有最优控制问题可行性下降，需要一种能防止约束违反的制导与控制方法。

Method: 周期性重解约束最优控制问题实现制导更新；在问题中增广惩罚项，当扰动系统接近约束极限时增大约束与极限间的裕度；惩罚项采用平滑最大值近似。

Result: 在最大横程可重复使用运载器（RLV）再入问题上验证，参考解包含长时间约束弧，结果表明该方法对路径约束最优制导与控制可行。

Conclusion: 所提约束计算制导与控制方法能够有效防止约束违反、提升最优控制问题可行性，适用于路径约束下的最优制导与控制。

Abstract: A method for constrained computation guidance and control is described. The method employs guidance updates by periodically re-solving a constrained optimal control problem. Feasibility of the optimal control problem is improved by preventing constraint violations in the perturbed system. Constraint violation is prevented by augmenting the optimal control problem with a penalty term intended to increase the margin between a constraint and its limit when the perturbed system is within certain proximity of the constraint limit. The additional penalty term employs a smooth maximum approximation to increase the margin between the constraint and its limit. The method developed in this paper is demonstrated on the maximum cross-range reusable launch vehicle (RLV) entry problem, for which the reference solution contains a long-duration constrained arc. The results demonstrate that the method developed in this paper is viable for path-constrained optimal guidance and control.

</details>


### [192] [A Simple Complexity Lower Bound for Solving $Ax=b$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10874)
*Ohad Shamir*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文给出一个简短直接的证明：在无维度限制下，将 $Ax=b$ 近似求解至相对误差 $\varepsilon$（条件数为 $\kappa$）在最坏情况下需要 $Ω(κ\log(1/\varepsilon))$ 次矩阵-向量乘法，即使对随机算法也成立；并由此推出强凸二次函数优化的经典下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Dereziński、Epperly 和 Meyer [2026] 已给出更一般的下界结果，但其方法更复杂。作者受其优雅方法启发，希望为该特定设定寻找一个简短而直接的证明。

Method: 作者针对该特定问题提供了一个短小直接的证明，用来展示求解 $Ax=b$ 到相对误差 $\varepsilon$ 需要 $Ω(κ\log(1/\varepsilon))$ 次矩阵-向量乘法；随后通过一个直接的归约，将结论推广到强凸二次函数优化的下界。

Result: 证明得到：在最坏情况下，近似求解 $Ax=b$ 至相对误差 $\varepsilon$ 需要 $Ω(κ\log(1/\varepsilon))$ 次矩阵-向量乘法，且该下界对随机算法同样成立，实质上重现了 Dereziński 等人的下界。此外，归约得到强凸二次函数优化问题的经典 $Ω(\sqrtκ\log(1/\varepsilon))$ 下界，并适用于随机算法。

Conclusion: 本文用一个简短直接的证明恢复了该设定下的矩阵-向量乘法下界，并通过直接归约得到强凸二次优化中经典的 $Ω(\sqrtκ\log(1/\varepsilon))$ 下界（对随机算法适用）。

Abstract: In this note, we provide a short and direct proof that approximately solving $Ax=b$ to relative error $\varepsilon$, where $A$ has condition number $κ$ and unrestricted dimension, requires $Ω(κ\log(1/\varepsilon))$ matrix-vector multiplications in the worst case, even for randomized algorithms. This essentially recovers the lower bound of Dereziński, Epperly and Meyer [2026] for this setting, whose elegant and more general approach inspired us to seek a short direct proof. A straightforward reduction implies the classical $Ω(\sqrtκ\log(1/\varepsilon))$ lower bound for optimizing strongly convex quadratic functions, applicable to randomized algorithms.

</details>


### [193] [The Polarity Process for the Maximum-Volume Inscribed Ellipsoid Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10888)
*Kaizhao Sun*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了多面体的最大体积内接椭球（MaxIE）问题，通过基于极性的几何迭代，利用极MinCE的对数体积作为势函数，证明了全局线性收缩并给出有限体积比迭代界，同时分析了非精确极性过程及多种MinCE求解器的条件算术估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Khachiyan和Todd在1993年提出的极性迭代法此前仅有渐近收敛论证，缺乏体积比速率；本文旨在补充这一理论空白。

Method: 给定内点形成平移极性多面体，计算其最小体积覆盖椭球（MinCE），再极化回得到新的内接椭球；以极MinCE的对数体积为势函数，证明其凸性并给出显式梯度公式，建立全局线性收缩；进一步分析非精确极性过程并推导充分预言机容差。

Result: 获得了独立的收敛性证明和有限体积比迭代界，收缩因子为存在性且依赖实例；结合路径跟踪牛顿法、重心坐标下降和远离步Frank-Wolfe方法给出条件算术估计；数值实验比较了这些预言机选择与两个MaxIE基线。

Conclusion: 性能依赖于MinCE求解器与实例几何的匹配；该方法为MaxIE问题提供了新的理论分析和实用算法框架。

Abstract: We study the maximum-volume inscribed ellipsoid (MaxIE) problem for a polytope through a geometric iteration based on polarity. Given an interior point, the method forms the shifted polar polytope, computes its minimum-volume covering ellipsoid (MinCE), and then polarizes this covering ellipsoid back to obtain a new inscribed ellipsoid. This procedure was suggested by Khachiyan and Todd in 1993. Prior work studied basic polarity identities and gave an asymptotic-convergence argument for the exact process, but did not furnish a volume-ratio rate. Independently of that asymptotic argument, we develop a new analysis based on the log-volume of the polar MinCE as a potential function. We prove its convexity with an explicit gradient formula and establish global linear contraction of the potential gap along each trajectory. The contraction factor is existential and instance-dependent. This gives both a separate convergence proof and a finite volume-ratio iteration bound. We further analyze an inexact polarity process in which each MinCE subproblem is solved only approximately, and derive sufficient oracle tolerances for producing a prescribed volume approximation. Combining this outer analysis with existing algorithms for MinCE gives conditional arithmetic estimates based on the path-following Newton method, the barycentric coordinate descent, and the away-step Frank-Wolfe method. Numerical experiments compare these oracle choices with two MaxIE baselines and illustrate that performance depends on matching the MinCE solver to the instance geometry.

</details>


### [194] [Deterministic and Random Bipartite Matching on General Networks: Convex Flow Reformulation, Asymptotic Properties, and Fast Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10938)
*Yuhui Zhai,Yanfeng Ouyang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究一般网络上的最小距离二部匹配问题，提出精确的边可分凸流重构、基于平滑单调重排近似的可解算法，以及闭式一步拉普拉斯估计器；并刻画随机供需分布下最优匹配距离的标度律（同分布时为点数的平方根，否则线性）及最优流性质，最后用快速算法与数值实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最小距离二部匹配在众多领域有广泛应用，但一般网络上的精确求解通常计算代价高。作者希望给出可高效求解的重构与近似方法，并揭示随机情形下匹配成本的普适标度规律，从而在保证精度的同时大幅降低计算量。

Method: 1）针对确定性问题，给出精确的边可分凸流重构；2）引入边级不平衡剖面的平滑单调重排近似，使凸流重构可高效求解；3）进一步对凸程序做一阶阻力近似，解析导出闭式的一步拉普拉斯估计器；4）研究供需点随机分布问题，分析期望最优匹配距离的标度及其极限阻力网络，并据此提出快速近似算法。

Result: 证明了期望最优匹配距离的标度：供需分布相同时随点数呈平方根增长，否则呈线性增长；同分布情形下最优流被证明为中心化、对称且次高斯；异分布情形下极限阻力网络刻画了供需不平衡的重分配方式。数值实验表明所提估计器能紧密逼近精确匹配成本，同时显著减少计算时间；随机匹配解的理论性质经大规模蒙特卡洛模拟得到数值验证。

Conclusion: 该工作为一般网络最小距离二部匹配提供了从精确凸重构到闭式近似估计的完整框架，并揭示了随机匹配的标度律与结构性质，兼顾理论刻画与计算效率，具有推广到大规模实际应用的潜力。

Abstract: Minimum-distance bipartite matching on general networks has numerous applications various fields. This paper first focuses on deterministic problems and presents an exact edgewise-separable convex-flow reformulation. By introducing a smooth monotone rearrangement approximation of the edge-wise imbalance profiles, the convex-flow reformulation's can be solved efficiently. If we further conduct a first-order resistance-based approximation of the convex program, a one-step Laplacian-based estimator can be analytically derived in closed forms. The paper also studies random problems where supply and demand points are randomly distributed. We show that the expected optimal matching distance scales with the square root of the number of points if the supply/demand point distributions are identical, or linearly otherwise. In the former case, the optimal flow is proven to be centered, symmetric, and sub-Gaussian. In the latter case, the limiting resistance network characterizes how supply-demand imbalance is redistributed and motivates a fast algorithm that approximate the optimal flow based on the limiting resistance. Numerical experiments show that the proposed estimators closely approximate the exact matching cost while substantially reducing computation time. The proven theoretical properties of the random matching solution are numerically verified by large-scale Monte Carlo simulations.

</details>


### [195] [Conformal-DRO: Distributionally Robust Optimization with Conformalized Ambiguity Set](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11073)
*Luhao Zhang,Shixiang Zhu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出Conformal-DRO，利用嵌套共形区域为未来的潜在分布构造模糊集，在有限样本下以至少1-α的概率覆盖潜在法则，无需估计潜在法则或混合机制，最坏情况问题化为有限线性规划并具稀疏对抗解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据驱动的分布鲁棒优化（DRO）通常将条件结果法则视为固定，仅用模糊集刻画估计误差，忽视了潜在的分布异质性：每个实例有未观测的法则但只贡献一个观测，即便混合法则已知，不确定性依然存在。因此需要一种能刻画这种潜在异质性的鲁棒方法。

Method: 提出Conformal-DRO：使用嵌套共形区域为未来潜在法则构造模糊集；在可交换性假设下，该集合以至少1-α的概率在有限样本下覆盖该法则，且无需估计底层潜在法则或混合机制。共形路径诱导出数据驱动的传输几何，α决定半径；最坏情况问题简化为共形壳上的有限线性规划。

Result: 所构造的模糊集在有限样本下以至少1-α的概率覆盖未来潜在分布法则；最坏情况问题化为有限线性规划，并具有稀疏的对抗解；得到的鲁棒值可为所选决策的期望成本提供有限样本证书。

Conclusion: Conformal-DRO为潜在分布异质性下的决策提供了有限样本保证，无需估计潜在法则或混合机制，兼具计算可行性与稀疏对抗解，可作为决策期望成本的稳健认证工具。

Abstract: Data-driven distributionally robust optimization (DRO) typically treats the conditional outcome law as fixed and uses ambiguity sets to capture estimation error. This paper studies latent distributional heterogeneity, where each instance has an unobserved law but contributes only one observation, so uncertainty persists even if the mixture law is known. We propose Conformal-DRO, which uses nested conformal regions to construct an ambiguity set for the future latent law. Under exchangeability, the set covers this law with probability at least $1-α$ in finite samples, without estimating underlying latent laws or their mixing mechanism. The conformal path induces a data-driven transport geometry, while $α$ determines the radius. The worst-case problem reduces to a finite linear program over conformal shells and admits sparse adversarial solutions. The resulting robust value provides a finite-sample certificate for the selected decision's expected cost.

</details>


### [196] [The best approximation tuple: an extension of the Cheney-Goldstein algorithm and results to the multiple sets case](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11105)
*Yair Censor,Daniel Reem*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文推广Cheney-Goldstein的最优逼近对（BAP）算法：处理多于两个集合，且每个集合可为有限族集合的交集，提出"最优逼近元组（BAT）"问题，以"枢纽集+卫星集"视角替代循环极小化，在欧氏空间、卫星集严格凸紧的条件下证明算法收敛到唯一解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 1959年Cheney与Goldstein关于两集合最优逼近对（BAP）的经典结果仅适用于两个集合，且难以推广到多集合或交集结构；已有以循环（cycle）作为目标泛函极小元的思路存在理论障碍，无法保证收敛到真正解。

Method: 将问题推广为BAT：集合数可多于两个，每个集合可表示为有限族集合的交集。核心思想是区分"枢纽集"与其余"卫星集"，而非寻找目标泛函的循环极小元，从而绕开循环与泛函极小元相关的理论障碍。收敛性分析不依赖Fejér单调性，转而利用正交投影在非扩张性定义中等式与不等式的性质。

Result: 在欧氏空间且卫星集为严格凸紧集的条件下，证明了所提算法收敛到BAT问题的唯一解。

Conclusion: 该工作从两个方向扩展了Cheney-Goldstein理论：多集合情形与集合交集结构，并给出非标准收敛性证明，为多集合逼近问题提供了新的算法框架与理论基础。
